Method: PixelART采用单流多模态扩散Transformer，在像素空间直接对区域RGBA像素块进行去噪，训练用rectified flow，从零训练。基于"高噪声时间步决定图层分配与粗组织、低噪声时间步主要精修颜色/alpha/纹理/边界"的观察，提出terminal-boosted时间步采样策略，增加高噪声图层分配区间的训练覆盖。在4M多层设计模板上训练。

Result: 在Design-Multi-Layer-Bench和LICA上取得SOTA的图层分解与合成重建效果，相比Qwen-Image-Layered参数量减少80%以上、延迟降低98%、内存降低85%。消融显示像素空间x-prediction、terminal-boosted时间步采样、数据/模型scaling是关键，而T2I预训练对I2L任务收益甚微。

Conclusion: 图层分解无需重型预训练组件，从零训练的像素空间rectified-flow Transformer即可高效实现SOTA，为I2L任务提供了更简洁高效的范式。

Abstract: Image-to-layer decomposition converts a flattened image into editable RGBA layers, enabling element-level editing in design workflows. Existing diffusion-based systems typically adapt large pretrained text-to-image (T2I) models and introduce RGBA autoencoders or variable-layer architectural modules. We revisit this design choice and ask whether layer decomposition truly requires these heavyweight components. We introduce PixelART, a pixel-space rectified-flow Transformer trained from scratch for image-to-layer (I2L) decomposition. PixelART directly denoises regional RGBA pixel patches using a single-stream multi-modal diffusion Transformer, avoiding RGBA-VAEs, pretrained T2I backbones, and layer-specific decoders. We identify a key property of the task: high-noise timesteps determine layer assignment and coarse layer organization, while low-noise timesteps mainly refine color, alpha, texture, and boundaries. Based on this observation, we propose a terminal-boosted timestep sampling strategy to increase training coverage in the high-noise layer assignment regime. Trained on 4M multi-layer design templates, PixelART achieves state-of-the-art layer decomposition and composite reconstruction on Design-Multi-Layer-Bench and LICA with over 80% fewer parameters, 98% lower latency, and 85% lower memory than the recent Qwen-Image-Layered model. Ablation experiments show that pixel-space $\mathbf{x}$-prediction, terminal-boosted timestep sampling, and data/model scaling are critical, while T2I pretraining brings marginal benefits to the I2L task.

</details>


### [36] [C$^{2}$-INR: Customized Convolutional Implicit Neural Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22807)
*Jinglei Shi,Xinran Chang,Jiaqi Cui,Yingjie Xia,Zhaolin Xiao,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 C²-INR，一种面向卷积式隐式神经表示（INR）的网络定制方法：用方向性不规则卷积核替代常规卷积核，依据图像频谱方向能量分配核数量，并借助正交基稀疏化表示、退火 Gumbel-Softmax 进行核级激活函数选择，在参数量相当条件下于表示、修复、超分任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流 INR 以 MLP 为骨干，逐像素独立渲染，无法利用相邻像素的空间相关性；卷积式 INR 可并行处理并建模像素间依赖，但研究不足且多采用固定架构，缺乏面向具体图像的自适应能力。

Method: 以卷积 INR 为对象进行网络定制：1）用不规则方向性核替换常规滤波器，依据图像频谱中的方向能量分配核数量，能量强的方向分配更多核，实现内容定制的卷积配置；2）将核用正交基重构以获得更优的稀疏表示；3）引入基于退火 Gumbel-Softmax 的核级激活函数选择机制，为每个卷积核匹配最合适的激活函数。

Result: 大量实验表明，在参数预算相当的条件下，C²-INR 在图像表示、图像修复（inpainting）和超分辨率等多种图像处理任务上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 面向卷积 INR 的网络定制（方向性核分配、正交基稀疏化、核级激活选择）是提升 INR 表示能力的有效途径，能在相同参数预算下取得更优性能。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representation (INR) leverages neural networks to represent discrete signals such as images as continuous ones, where the network weights serve as a compact form of the signal itself. Most existing INR methods adopt Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) as their backbone. Since these models render each pixel independently, they inherently fail to exploit the spatial correlations that exist between neighboring pixels. In contrast,convolutional INRs can process pixels in parallel while inherently accounting for inter-pixel dependencies, making them a more natural fit for representing images. Nevertheless, convolutional INRs remain relatively underexplored, and the majority of them rely on fixed architectural settings, leaving little room for image-specific adaptation. In this paper, we investigate network customization for convolutional INRs. We replace conventional filters with irregular directional kernels, whose allocation is guided by the directional energy in the image spectrum, i.e., directions exhibiting stronger energy are assigned a larger number of kernels, enabling content-tailored convolution settings. These kernels are further reformulated via an orthogonal basis to achieve a superior sparse representation. Moreover, we introduce an annealed Gumbel-Softmax-based mechanism for kernel-level activation function selection, which gives the most suitable activation function for each convolution kernel. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method, namely C$^{2}$-INR, achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art approaches under comparable parameter budgets across a wide range of image processing tasks, including representation, inpainting, and super-resolution.

</details>


### [37] [SatOV: Restoring Spatial Priors for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Segmentation in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22834)
*Changhao Zhao,Linglin Zeng,Hai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatOV：一种免训练的开放词汇遥感语义分割框架，通过恢复表示阶段与空间分辨率阶段的空间先验来提升分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型在通用领域表现良好，但其图像级分类设计削弱了高分辨率遥感分割所需的空间先验：深层特征变换导致结构空间关系退化，下采样导致细粒度空间细节丢失。

Method: 提出免训练框架SatOV，在表示流水线的两个阶段恢复空间先验。1）ResQQ（Residual QQ Attention）：从CLIP中间层提取Query-Key自注意力，与最终层的Query-Query注意力以残差组合方式融合，恢复被最终层表示抑制的结构空间先验；2）SatUp（Spatially Modulated Upsampling）：以原始高分辨率RGB图像作为空间引导，结合空间特征调制与引导式交叉注意力，重建像素级纹理与边界。

Result: 在DOTA、UDD、LoveDA和Vaihingen数据集上的大量实验表明，SatOV持续提升免训练OVS性能，并在定量与定性结果上达到与最先进方法相当的水平。

Conclusion: 在表示阶段与空间分辨率阶段同时恢复空间先验，对遥感开放词汇分割是有效的。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVS) of remote sensing imagery is a challenging pixel-level task requiring strong generalization and adaptation to the spatial characteristics of remote sensing data. Although existing vision-language foundation models perform well in general domains, their image-level classification design weakens the spatial priors needed for high-resolution remote sensing segmentation: structural spatial relations are degraded during deep feature transformation, and fine-grained spatial details are lost during downsampling. To address these complementary deficiencies, we propose SatOV, a training-free framework for open-vocabulary remote sensing segmentation that restores spatial priors at two stages of the representation pipeline. Specifically, Residual QQ Attention (ResQQ) extracts Query-Key self-attention from an intermediate CLIP layer and fuses it with final-layer Query-Query attention via a residual combination, restoring structural spatial priors suppressed by the final-layer representation. Spatially Modulated Upsampling (SatUp) uses the original high-resolution RGB image as spatial guidance, combining spatial feature modulation with guided cross-attention to reconstruct pixel-level textures and boundaries. Extensive experiments on DOTA, UDD, LoveDA, and Vaihingen show that SatOV consistently improves training-free OVS and achieves competitive quantitative and qualitative results against state-of-the-art methods. These results validate the effectiveness of restoring spatial priors at both the representation and spatial-resolution stages for remote sensing open-vocabulary segmentation.

</details>


### [38] [LINGO: Latent Initialization and Gradient Optimization for Sparse-view X-ray Novel View Synthesis and CT Reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22849)
*Lifeng Xing,Dequan Jin,Kunpeng Bu,Peigeng He,Shihui Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LINGO框架，通过潜空间掩码初始化和动态梯度优化，改善稀疏视角3DGS-X射线重建中的噪声初始化、位置不敏感和低密度区梯度弱问题，并引入IPSD指标评估初始化质量，在X3D数据集上兼顾训练加速与重建质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏视角X射线成像的新视角合成与CT重建中，角度覆盖不足会导致结构歧义与噪声累积。将3DGS与X射线吸收物理结合虽有前景，但存在初始化噪声大、位置不敏感、低密度区域梯度弱等问题，限制重建质量与训练效率。

Method: 提出LINGO统一框架，融合潜空间掩码初始化与动态梯度优化：从X射线掩码构建体素级3D滤波器以抑制背景噪声并提供几何先验；采用自适应体素缩放策略与动态缩放损失，调节空间分辨率并显式放大低密度结构的梯度；同时提出IPSD指标量化初始化点云的结构偏差。

Result: 在X3D数据集上，新视角合成任务中PSNR和SSIM平均提升0.72和0.0039，并在5k步内达到SOTA模型30k迭代相当的品质；CT重建任务中PSNR和SSIM平均提升0.36和0.0134，表现出稳定的改进。

Conclusion: LINGO有效缓解稀疏视角X射线重建中的初始化与优化难题，在加速训练的同时提升重建质量，验证了其在多种稀疏视角成像场景中的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: In novel view synthesis and Computed Tomography (CT) reconstruction with sparse-view X-ray imaging, insufficient angular coverage leads to structural ambiguity and accumulated noise. Integrating 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with X-ray absorption physics can achieve promising results, but it suffers from noisy initialization, positional insensitivity, and weak gradients in low-density regions. In this paper, we propose a unified Latent Initialization and Gradient Optimization (LINGO) framework to address these issues. LINGO combines latent mask-space initialization with dynamic gradient optimization to improve point cloud structural completeness while accelerating training. It constructs voxel-level 3D filters from X-ray masks to robustly suppress background noise and provide reliable geometric priors. By employing an adaptive voxel scaling strategy and dynamically scaling loss, LINGO can adjust spatial resolution and explicitly amplify gradients in low-density structures. To evaluate the quality of initialization, we introduce the Initialization Point Cloud Structural Deviation (IPSD) metric. Experiments on the X3D dataset indicate that for the novel view synthesis task, LINGO improves the Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) and Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) by an average of 0.72 and 0.0039, respectively, over baselines under identical sparse-view settings, achieving comparable reconstruction quality within 5k steps to state-of-the-art models typically trained with 30k iterations. For the CT reconstruction task, LINGO also demonstrates consistent improvements, with average PSNR and SSIM gains of 0.36 and 0.0134. These results highlight LINGO's effectiveness in both accelerating training and enhancing reconstruction quality across different sparse-view imaging scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [Image Frame Dynamic Object Segmentation and Ego Motion Estimation using Radar Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22857)
*Astik Srivastava,Suhani Grover,Avinash Sharma,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出雷达-视觉框架Radar-Dot，利用多普勒测量解耦自运动估计与动态物体分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体分割与自运动估计在自动驾驶中高度耦合：准确的自运动估计依赖静态场景观测，而识别静态观测又需要已知自运动。论文旨在利用雷达多普勒信息打破这一循环依赖。

Method: 先用雷达回波通过线性多普勒约束估计自速度，并以残差法进行静态/动态分割与鲁棒估计以抑制运动物体干扰；再将估计的运动与度量深度、稠密光流结合，识别图像中观测运动与刚性场景运动不一致的区域，从而实现动态物体分割。

Result: 在10个nuScenes场景（nuscenes-mini的一部分）、394对帧上，几何流水线达到20.24%动态IoU和33.67% F1分数；基于雷达的静态点滤波相比使用全部雷达回波提升了自速度估计精度。

Conclusion: 雷达可作为联合提升自运动估计与动态物体分割的有效模态，验证了多普勒约束解耦耦合问题的潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic object segmentation and ego-motion estimation are closely coupled problems in autonomous driving, as accurate ego-motion estimation typically requires static scene observations, while identifying static observations requires knowledge of the ego motion. We present Radar-Dot, a radar--RGB framework that exploits radar Doppler measurements to address this coupling. Radar returns are first used to estimate ego velocity through a linear Doppler constraint, with residual-based static/dynamic segmentation and robust estimation used to reduce the influence of moving objects. The estimated motion is then combined with metric depth and dense optical flow to identify image regions whose observed motion is inconsistent with the rigid scene motion. Experiments on 10 nuScenes scenes (part of nuscenes-mini) demonstrate that the resulting geometric pipeline achieves 20.24% dynamic IoU and 33.67% F1-score over 394 frame pairs, while radar-based static-point filtering improves ego-velocity estimation compared with using all radar returns. These results demonstrate the potential of radar as a modality for jointly improving ego-motion estimation and dynamic object segmentation.

</details>


### [40] [Planning-Aligned Pretraining of BEV Representations with Sparse Action-Conditioned Targets for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22868)
*Jaeha Song,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAVER 提出一种面向规划对齐的 BEV 编码器预训练方法，仅用单帧 LiDAR 构建稀疏风险与未知区域目标，通过动作状态条件化的预测头引导掩码 BEV 特征学习候选运动轨迹上的几何约束，无需驾驶任务标注或稠密重建，仅迁移 BEV 编码器；在 nuScenes 上显著降低碰撞率并提升多项指标，且在 Bench2Drive 闭环评测中提升驾驶得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶需要与规划相关的 BEV 表征，但现有预训练方法多依赖任务标注或稠密场景重建，成本高且未必与规划需求对齐；因此需要一种无需驾驶任务标注、无需稠密重建，并能将监督直接指向候选运动几何约束的 BEV 编码器预训练范式。

Method: PAVER 从单次 LiDAR 扫描出发，沿基于规则的自车运动构建稀疏的风险与未知目标，用于描述被占据与未被观测的证据；使用约 1 万参数的预测头，在动作状态条件下从掩码 BEV 特征预测这些目标，从而将监督直接施加于候选运动轨迹的几何约束。预训练不需要驾驶任务标注或稠密重建；仅迁移 BEV 编码器，保持下游架构与仅相机推理不变。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上，PAVER 将 VAD-Tiny 的平均碰撞率从 0.51% 降至 0.19%，同时改善规划 L2、运动预测、检测与建图指标；所选 VAD-Tiny 与 VAD-Base 训练方案相比从零训练（含预训练）估计总训练时间约减少 36%。在 Bench2Drive Town05 Long 上，PAVER 将 UniAD-Tiny 的闭环驾驶得分从 48.45 提升至 58.79。

Conclusion: PAVER 表明，基于稀疏风险/未知目标的规划对齐预训练可在无任务标注、无稠密重建条件下有效学习规划相关的 BEV 表征；仅迁移 BEV 编码器即可在多类下游任务与开/闭环评测中获得一致提升，并降低总体训练开销。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires planning-relevant bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations, but existing pretraining approaches often rely on task annotations or dense scene reconstruction. We introduce PAVER, Planning-Aligned BEV Encoder Pretraining. From a single LiDAR sweep, PAVER constructs sparse risk and unknown targets describing occupied and unobserved evidence along rule-based ego motions. A 10K-parameter head predicts these targets from masked BEV features conditioned on the action state, directing supervision toward geometric constraints on candidate motions. Pretraining requires no driving-task annotations or dense reconstruction. Only the BEV encoder is transferred, preserving the downstream architecture and camera-only inference. On nuScenes, PAVER reduces VAD-Tiny's average collision rate from 0.51% to 0.19%, while improving planning L2, motion prediction, detection, and mapping. The selected VAD-Tiny and VAD-Base schedules use about 36% less estimated total training time than scratch training, including pretraining. On Bench2Drive Town05 Long, PAVER improves UniAD-Tiny's closed-loop Driving Score from 48.45 to 58.79. The project page is available at https://archiiive99.github.io/PAVER.

</details>


### [41] [Combining Foundation Model Confidence and Monocular Depth for Training-Free Out-of-Distribution Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22896)
*Serin Varghese,Fabian Hüger,Kira Maag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的方法，直接从基础分割模型的置信度预测中推导密集OOD分数，并融合单目深度估计的几何信息，在SegmentMeIfYouCan基准及视频OOD跟踪任务上取得良好表现，尤其适合道路中心场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界中的自动驾驶会遭遇未知物体（如异域动物、散落货物），对这些分布外（OOD）物体进行可靠的检测与分割对安全的环境理解和决策至关重要。现有方法大多依赖OOD训练样本、需重训分割主干或专用辅助架构，限制了实际适用性。

Method: 一种免训练方法：(1) 无需任务特定微调，也无需接触异常数据，直接从基础分割模型的置信度预测中导出密集OOD分数；(2) 引入单目深度估计的几何信息，为基于不确定性的预测提供互补线索，以提升OOD分割的鲁棒性。

Result: 在SegmentMeIfYouCan基准上进行了评估，并在视频序列的OOD跟踪任务上进一步测试其性能，体现了真实感知系统的时间特性；该方法在道路中心基准上表现较强。

Conclusion: 该免训练、无需异常数据的OOD分割方案具有较强实用性与鲁棒性，在道路中心场景表现突出，并可扩展至视频OOD跟踪；但在其他场景下的泛化能力仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles operating in open-world scenarios are inevitably confronted with previously unknown objects, such as exotic animals or loose cargo. The reliable detection and segmentation of these out-of-distribution (OOD) objects is therefore crucial for a safe understanding of the environment and decision-making. Most existing approaches require access to OOD training samples, retraining of the segmentation backbone, or dedicated auxiliary architectures, limiting their practical applicability. We propose a training-free method that derives dense OOD scores directly from the confidence predictions of a foundation segmentation model, without any task-specific fine-tuning or access to anomalous data. To improve the robustness of our OOD segmentation, geometric information from monocular depth estimation is incorporated into the decision process, providing complementary cues to uncertainty-based predictions. We evaluate the proposed method on the SegmentMeIfYouCan benchmark and additionally assess its performance on OOD tracking in video sequences, reflecting the temporal nature of real-world perception systems. The method performs strongly on road-centered benchmarks.

</details>


### [42] [Scout: Open-World Species Recognition on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22897)
*Mohammad Mehdi Rastikerdar,Hui Guan,Deepak Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scout 是一个面向边缘设备的自主开放世界识别系统，通过间歇调用云端 VLM 将新物种知识蒸馏到紧凑的边缘模型，在仅有部署位置和空白场景帧的条件下自动构建持久化、场景条件化的识别能力；在 30 个相机陷阱部署上，其精度接近预定义物种列表的模型，并显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型（VLM）具备超出固定类别集的开放世界识别能力，但计算需求高，难以在众多边缘设备上运行。云端卸载虽可提供该能力，却会因上传每张图像而消耗稀缺带宽与通信能量。作者希望在不依赖预定义物种列表、人工标注和手动调参的前提下，将 VLM 的开放世界识别能力带到受计算、能量与带宽严格约束的边缘端。

Method: 以野生动物监测为场景，提出 Scout 系统：间歇性调用云端 VLM 来"教授"紧凑边缘模型新的类别；仅给定部署地点与空白场景帧，Scout 自动将 VLM 识别出的每个物种转化为边缘模型中持久化、场景条件化的识别能力。系统无需预定义物种列表、无需人工标注、无需手动调参，并通过减少图像上传实现资源高效。

Result: 在三个区域、基于 NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano 的 30 个相机陷阱部署中：Scout 的精度与给定预定义物种列表的模型相差仅 0.1–2.5%；对其初始类别集之外的物种，Scout 达到 53.7–59.1% 的准确率，而完全云端卸载为 56.5–65.1%，同时部署能耗降低 59–71%。

Conclusion: Scout 证明可以在严格的计算、能量与带宽预算内，将 VLM 的开放世界识别能力有效地迁移到边缘设备，在保持接近云端方案精度的同时大幅降低能耗，适用于相机陷阱等无预定义类别清单的部署场景。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) enable recognition beyond a fixed class set, but their computational demands prevent them from running on many edge devices. Cloud offload makes this capability accessible, but sending every image consumes scarce bandwidth and communication energy. We ask how to bring the open-world recognition capability of VLMs to the edge while operating within tight compute, energy, and bandwidth budgets. Wildlife monitoring provides a natural setting for exploring this question because camera traps encounter species not known at deployment. We present Scout, an autonomous open-world recognition system that invokes a cloud VLM intermittently to teach new classes to a compact edge model. Given only the deployment location and empty site frames, Scout autonomously turns each species identified by the VLM into persistent, site-conditioned recognition capability in a resource-efficient edge model, without a predefined species list, human labeling, or manual tuning. Across 30 camera-trap deployments in three regions on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, the accuracy of Scout remains within 0.1-2.5% of a model given a predefined species list. On species outside its initial class set, Scout achieves 53.7-59.1% accuracy, compared with 56.5-65.1% for full cloud offload, while using 59-71% less deployment energy.

</details>


### [43] [AVTR-1: Open Stack for Real-Time Interactive Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22913)
*Artem Kravtsov,Dmitrii Ziganshin,Vsevolod Poletaev,Gleb Balitskiy,Anastasia Tikhonova,Egor Burkov,Vadim Lebedev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVTR-1是一个面向实时交互式虚拟人对话的开放技术栈，核心为153M参数、以双方音频为条件的自回归流匹配动作生成器，通过自蒸馏实现流式音频编码；配套系统将分块生成转为由外部语音代理驱动的连续同步音视频流，并解析其延迟界；同时提出R-DGG指标以检验说话人语音对听者动作的预测贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话人头与双人模型虽已实现实时推理，但单纯的快速动作生成并不能构成交互式对话：实时系统需与外部语音代理的语音同步、调度视频帧播放并处理打断。此外，传统听者动作指标无法验证配对说话人的语音是否真正驱动了生成动作。

Method: 1）构建AVTR-1开放技术栈，核心是153M参数、以双方音频为条件的紧凑自回归流匹配动作生成器；2）通过自蒸馏改造音频编码器以支持流式处理；3）将模型的分块生成整合为受外部语音代理驱动的连续、同步音视频流，并解析推导其对用户可感知延迟的贡献；4）提出Reference-Based Directed Granger Gain（R-DGG），在扣除听者历史与说话人动作后衡量说话人语音带来的额外预测信息。

Result: 延迟界的理论推导经两个商业语音代理验证；AVTR-1在所有报告的视觉质量指标与多数常规听动指标上领先同类双人系统，唇同步保持竞争力，推理在数据中心与消费级GPU上均可实时运行。R-DGG对录音听者及所有评估的双人系统均检出统计显著的预测依赖，而对无配对音频的说话人头生成器及说话人-听者错配组合则未检出。模型权重、渲染器与服务后端按组件分别授权开源。

Conclusion: AVTR-1表明，实时交互式虚拟人对话需要模型、流式同步与调度、打断处理等系统级协同，而非仅靠快速生成；R-DGG为"生成动作是否真正依赖对方语音"提供了可检验的判据，并将开源发布。

Abstract: Talking-head and dyadic models now achieve real-time inference, yet fast motion generation alone does not produce an interactive conversation. A live system must synchronize the model's output with speech from an external voice agent, schedule video frames for playback, and handle interruptions. We introduce AVTR-1, an open stack for real-time interactive avatar conversations, built around a compact 153M-parameter autoregressive flow-matching motion generator conditioned on both participants' audio. We adapt its audio encoder for streaming through self-distillation. The stack turns the model's chunk-based generation into a continuous, synchronized audio-video stream driven by an external voice agent, and we analytically derive its contribution to the user-facing latencies and validate the resulting bounds with two commercial voice agents. Further experiments demonstrate that AVTR-1 leads the compared dyadic systems on all reported visual-quality metrics and most conventional listening-motion metrics while remaining competitive in lip synchronization. Its inference runtime operates in real time on data-center and consumer GPUs. However, conventional listening metrics do not establish whether the paired speaker's speech contributes to generated motion. We therefore introduce the Reference-Based Directed Granger Gain (R-DGG), which measures the additional predictive information carried by speaker speech after accounting for listener history and speaker motion. R-DGG finds statistically supported predictive dependence for recorded listeners and all evaluated dyadic systems, but not for talking-head generators without paired audio or mismatched speaker-listener pairs. We release the model weights, renderer, and serving backend under component-specific licenses.

</details>


### [44] [Planning and Rendering in Concert: DeepFusion of Autoregressive Layouts and Diffusion for Visual Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22916)
*Guanqiao Chen,Jingru Tan,Dongxing Mao,Catherine Chen,Zijian Du,Libo Qin,Hu Jian Guo,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DuetGen，一个基于 DeepFusion 的自主可视化文本生成器，将自回归规划与连续扩散渲染联合学习，实现高文本保真度的图像文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从提示生成富文本图像需要兼顾文本保真度与文本在图像中的连贯融合。显式布局虽能提供结构引导，但良好规划并不保证渲染器忠实实现。现有基于布局的自回归-扩散系统通常将规划与渲染分开优化，导致规划器表示无法与图像合成联合适配。

Method: 提出 DuetGen，构建于 DeepFusion 之上，联合学习自回归规划与连续扩散渲染。DeepFusion 以规划器的提示与 bbox-content 隐状态为条件驱动扩散 Transformer，使渲染监督反向塑造连接文本规划与视觉输出的表示。联合目标包含：自回归规划监督、文本区域加权扩散学习、辅助坐标监督。推理时引入 Phase-Aware Attention Modulation 强化图像区域与匹配坐标/内容状态的对应关系。采用 2B 规划器与 4B 单流 DiT。

Result: 在 CVTG-2K 上达到 0.8293 词准确率，在 LongText-Bench 上达到 0.938 准确率，与规模大得多的 Qwen-Image 在两个基准上表现接近。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合学习的规划表示与区域特定渲染对自主可视化文本生成具有重要价值。

Abstract: Generating text-rich images from prompts requires both textual fidelity and the coherent integration of text into the surrounding image. An explicit layout can provide structured guidance about what text should appear and where, but a well-formed plan alone does not guarantee that the renderer will realize it faithfully. Existing layout-based AR-diffusion systems typically optimize planning and rendering separately, preventing the planner's representations from being adapted jointly with image synthesis. We introduce DuetGen, an autonomous visual text generator built on DeepFusion, which jointly learns autoregressive planning and continuous diffusion rendering. DeepFusion conditions a diffusion transformer on the planner's prompt and bbox-content hidden states, allowing rendering supervision to shape the representations connecting textual plans with visual outputs. Its joint objective combines autoregressive plan supervision, text-region-weighted diffusion learning, and auxiliary coordinate supervision to maintain structured planning, emphasize text-bearing regions, and improve the spatial precision of planner representations. During inference, Phase-Aware Attention Modulation strengthens the correspondence between image regions and their matched coordinate and content states, facilitating region-specific execution of the generated plan. With a 2B planner and a 4B single-stream DiT, DuetGen achieves 0.8293 word accuracy on CVTG-2K and 0.938 accuracy on LongText-Bench, closely matching the substantially larger Qwen-Image on both benchmarks. These results demonstrate the value of jointly learned planning representations and region-specific rendering for autonomous visual text generation.

</details>


### [45] [D3GS: Depth, DINO, and RGB Diffusion Co-Guided 3D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22941)
*Yunqi Gao,Zhanfeng Liao,Hanzhang Tu,Zhaoqi Su,Guoqing Zheng,Songtao Wang,Hongwen Zhang,Zhou Xue,Leyuan Liu,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D³GS：融合深度扩散补全、DINO引导视角一致学习与扩散高斯细化，提升稀疏视图3DGS重建与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏输入下3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）存在几何歧义、跨视角不一致以及欠约束区域细节缺失问题，导致重建质量退化与渲染不稳定。

Method: 提出D³GS框架，联合增强几何与外观：1）基于扩散补全与DPT细化恢复高分辨率度量深度图，用于稳健的高斯初始化与几何约束；2）DINO引导的视角一致学习，将结构特征增强到高斯属性以提升多视角一致性；3）基于扩散的高斯细化模块，将生成先验注入迭代优化，增强高频几何与外观细节。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF 360数据集上相较强基线取得一致且显著的提升；消融实验验证了各组件的有效性及其互补作用。

Conclusion: D³GS通过深度、DINO语义与扩散先验的协同引导，有效缓解稀疏视图3DGS的几何歧义与细节缺失，实现更稳定、更高质量的新视角合成。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse inputs remains challenging for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) due to ambiguous geometry, cross-view inconsistency, and missing details in under-constrained regions, resulting in degraded reconstruction and unstable rendering. To tackle these issues, we propose D$^{3}$GS, a Depth-DINO-Diffusion guided sparse-view Gaussian reconstruction framework that jointly enhances geometry and appearance. D$^{3}$GS first recovers a high-resolution, metric depth map via diffusion-based completion and DPT (Dense Prediction Transformer) refinement, providing robust Gaussian initialization and geometric constraints. Then, a DINO-guided view-consistent learning is introduced to augment Gaussian attributes with structural features, improving multi-view consistency. Finally, a diffusion-based Gaussian refinement module injects generative priors into an iterative optimization strategy, enhancing high-frequency geometric and appearance details within the Gaussian representation. Experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF 360 show that D$^{3}$GS achieves consistent and substantial improvements over strong baselines, with ablation studies validating the effectiveness and complementary roles of each component.

</details>


### [46] [An Evolutionary Agentic Approach for Open-ended Image Quality Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22942)
*Zhenchen Tang,Bo Peng,Zichuan Wang,Songlin Yang,Leilei Cao,Fengjie Zhu,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练多智能体框架 PACE，将开放式图像质量评估（IQA）重构为可验证的 VQA 探针协议构建，仅需每个维度 4 张人工标注图像校准，显著缓解整体性偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型推动 IQA 从传统保真度扩展到物理合理性、文本渲染正确性等新兴感知维度，但现有 IQA 模型依赖固定定义与重监督，难以扩展到开放式感知维度。作者识别出"整体性偏置"（holistic bias）这一关键局限：模型在评估未见维度时复用通用质量先验，导致评分错误与排序反转。

Method: PACE（Perceptual Agentic Collaborative Evolution）是一个免训练的多智能体框架，将开放式 IQA 表述为显式的协议构建问题。给定目标维度，协作智能体构建由可验证的 VQA 探针组成的评估协议，将评估建立在具体视觉证据而非整体印象之上；所得协议仅用每个维度 4 张人工标注图像进行校准，并通过双轨评分机制使模型感知与人评分尺度对齐。

Result: 在传统 IQA、结构保真度、上下文感知美学以及新定义的开放式维度上，PACE 持续提升其 MLLM 骨干的性能，在多种 IQA 设置下达到有竞争力的表现，并将整体性覆盖率（HOR）从 44.4% 降至 8.6%。

Conclusion: PACE 通过协议化、证据接地与轻量校准，为开放式感知维度的 IQA 提供了免训练、可扩展的解决方案，有效抑制整体性偏置导致的评分与排序问题。

Abstract: Generative models are rapidly expanding image quality assessment (IQA) beyond traditional fidelity factors to emerging dimensions such as physical plausibility and text-rendering correctness. However, existing IQA models rely on fixed definitions and heavy supervision, making them difficult to extend to open-ended perceptual dimensions. We identify holistic bias as an important limitation: when scoring an unseen dimension, models reuse generic quality priors, leading to scoring errors and rank inversion. To address this, we propose PACE (Perceptual Agentic Collaborative Evolution), a training-free multi-agent framework that formulates open-ended IQA as explicit protocol construction. Given a target dimension, PACE uses collaborative agents to construct an evaluation protocol composed of verifiable Visual Question Answering (VQA) probes, grounding evaluation in concrete visual evidence rather than holistic impressions. The resulting protocol is calibrated using only four human-annotated images per dimension, while a dual-track scoring mechanism aligns model perception with human scoring scales. Across traditional IQA, structural fidelity, context-aware aesthetics, and newly defined open-ended dimensions, PACE consistently improves its MLLM backbone, achieving competitive performance across diverse IQA settings, and reduces the Holistic Override Rate (HOR) from 44.4\% to 8.6\%.

</details>


### [47] [RewardVerse: Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization for Video Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22947)
*Zhenchen Tang,Yang Li,Songlin Yang,Bo Peng,Xiaotong Zhao,Shuai Li,Haotian Fan,Alan Zhao,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RewardVerse：一种基于评分细则（rubric）的视频奖励框架，以动态 rubric 作为查询与打分器之间的中间表示，缓解标量漂移；并配套两阶段训练算法 RGPO 实现协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习对视频生成模型的优化至关重要，而稳健的奖励模型是其基石。然而现有视频奖励模型常输出不稳定的标量分数：它们把复杂、主观的视频质量直接映射为单一分数，缺乏显式评价标准，导致"标量漂移"——评分尺度在不同 prompt 间坍塌或偏移，使奖励信号对 RL 不可靠。

Method: 受专业人工标注工程启发，构建 RewardVerse：在评价查询与打分器之间引入动态 rubric 作为中间表示。不做无约束的直接打分，而是先生成显式评价准则，再进行 rubric 引导的打分，提供稳定语义锚点以缓解标量漂移。为高效优化该协同流水线，提出 Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization（RGPO），一个两阶段训练算法：先用自演化种子 rubric 对打分器进行预热，再联合优化 rubric 生成器以产出查询自适应的评价准则，同时持续将打分器与人类评分对齐。

Result: 在 16 维 EvalVerse 基准及外部数据集上的大量实验表明：RewardVerse 缓解了标量漂移，在 pointwise 与 pairwise 评估上均达到 SOTA，并为视频生成的 RL 提供了稳健且可解释的奖励信号。

Conclusion: 以 rubric 作为中间表示的奖励建模范式，能够为视频生成 RL 提供稳定、可解释的奖励信号；RGPO 的两阶段协同优化有效提升了评价性能与对齐效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is vital for optimizing video generation models, with a robust reward model (RM) serving as the cornerstone. However, existing video reward models often produce unstable scalar scores because they directly map complex, subjective video quality into a single score without explicit evaluation criteria. This leads to scalar drift, where the scoring scale collapses or shifts across different prompts, making the reward unreliable for RL. Drawing inspiration from professional human annotation engineering, we address this problem with RewardVerse, a rubric-based video reward framework that introduces a dynamic rubric as an intermediate representation between the evaluation query and the scorer. Instead of unconstrained direct scoring, RewardVerse first generates explicit evaluation criteria and then performs rubric-guided scoring, providing a stable semantic anchor that mitigates scalar drift. To efficiently optimize this collaborative pipeline, we propose Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), a two-stage training algorithm. RGPO first warms up the scorer using self-evolving seed rubrics and then jointly optimizes the rubric generator to produce query-adaptive evaluation criteria while continuously aligning the scorer with human ratings. Extensive experiments on the 16-dimensional EvalVerse benchmark and external datasets demonstrate that RewardVerse mitigates scalar drift, achieves state-of-the-art performance on both pointwise and pairwise evaluation, and provides a robust and interpretable reward signal for RL in video generation.

</details>


### [48] [CLEAR: Complex Learned Explicit Analytical Regularization for Ultra-Accelerated 4D Flow CMR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22950)
*German Shâma Wache,Sebastian Neumayer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR：首个面向4D重建任务的可学习正则化器，在保持压缩感知可解释性（透明变分目标、可解释正则结构）的同时引入学习模型灵活性，参数量<10k，在10×–50×超高加速4D Flow CMR重建中优于压缩感知LLR与变分网络FlowVN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩感知正则化器（如局部低秩LLR）可实现可解释的4D Flow CMR重建（依赖透明的变分目标），但其手工设计特性在高加速倍率下过于受限；现有学习类方法虽缓解此问题，却通过展开网络模块隐式编码正则，牺牲了可解释性。

Method: 提出CLEAR，将压缩感知的可解释性与学习模型的灵活性相结合，是首个用于4D重建任务的可学习正则化器：以可解释的正则化结构保留透明的变分形式，同时用学习方式获得正则器的灵活性。在CMRx4DFlow2026挑战赛10×–50×超高加速场景下进行验证。

Result: CLEAR在10×–50×超高加速条件下优于压缩感知局部低秩（LLR）以及流行的变分网络FlowVN，且参数量不足10k，同时保持可解释的正则化结构。

Conclusion: CLEAR表明可学习正则化器可在4D重建中同时实现高重建性能与可解释性，在超高加速4D Flow CMR中以极小参数代价超越压缩感知与隐式学习基线。

Abstract: While compressed-sensing regularizers enable interpretable reconstruction of 4D Flow CMR through transparent variational objectives, their hand-crafted nature is too restrictive under high acceleration. State-of-the-art learning-based approaches mitigate this, but typically encode regularization implicitly through unrolled network modules, which limits their interpretability. To address this limitation, we propose CLEAR, designed to combine the interpretability of compressed sensing with the flexibility of learned models. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first learned regularizer for a 4D reconstruction task. In the ultra-accelerated \(10\times\)--\(50\times\) regime of the CMRx4DFlow2026 challenge, CLEAR outperforms compressed sensing locally low-rank (LLR) and the popular variational network FlowVN, while using less than 10k parameters and preserving an interpretable regularization structure.

</details>


### [49] [General Collaborative Intelligence: Architecting Cognition for Resilient Multi-Agent Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22967)
*Lei Zhang,Chun Ye,Le Yang,Zhaozhong Wang,Deng-Ping Fan,Hang Dai,Binglu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述多智能体无人系统的协作智能研究，通过五维分类法与三项认知协同条件构建统一框架，梳理架构、通信、具身、韧性与信任等维度，覆盖V2X、无人机、工业物流与智慧城市四类应用，并提出GCI-Bench五支柱评测协议以应对基准饱和与评测碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体无人系统正从孤立的自中心感知转向协作智能，以克服遮挡、有限感知范围与环境退化造成的局部观测困境，但现有综述多将这些维度孤立考察，未能揭示其相互依赖关系，缺乏统一综合。

Method: 采用双重视角进行综述综合。其一为五维分类法，涵盖协作阶段、通信范式、融合架构、学习策略与应用领域；其二为三项认知协同条件——语义消歧、语用信息交换与主动信息觅食，将认知协同转化为可操作判据。基于此梳理协作架构与拓扑、将信道视为可微管线组件的神经通信协同设计、基于多智能体强化学习的具身行动—感知回路，以及面向同步、不确定性量化与标签高效学习的韧性机制。

Result: 将上述进展映射到V2X、无人机、工业物流与智慧城市四类运行领域，并对应安全—隐私—效用三元组；针对基准饱和与评测碎片化，提出GCI-Bench五支柱评分协议及成熟度模型，使协作方法的权衡在不同研究间可比。

Conclusion: 通过对可复现性、仿真到现实差距以及协作反而导致性能退化的条件的批判性反思，识别开放挑战，并指出面向真实世界不确定性下通用协作智能的发展方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent unmanned systems are moving from isolated, ego-centric sensing toward collaborative intelligence, in which distributed agents exchange compact features to overcome a local observation trap that no single agent can escape: occlusions, finite sensor range, and environmental degradation. The field has matured across architectural, communication, embodied, resilience, and trust dimensions, yet existing surveys examine these dimensions in isolation and rarely expose their dependencies. This review offers a unified synthesis through two complementary lenses. The first is a five-dimensional taxonomy spanning collaboration stage, communication paradigm, fusion architecture, learning strategy, and application domain. The second is three cognitive synergy conditions, Semantic Disambiguation, Pragmatic Information Exchange, and Proactive Informational Foraging, that turn cognitive synergy into operational criteria. Across these lenses we survey collaboration architectures and topologies, neural-communication co-design that treats the channel as a differentiable pipeline component, embodied action-perception loops via multi-agent reinforcement learning, and resilience mechanisms for synchronization, uncertainty quantification, and label-efficient learning. We then map these advances onto four operational domains, V2X, unmanned aerial, industrial logistics, and smart cities, and onto the safety-privacy-utility triad. To counter benchmark saturation and evaluation fragmentation, we propose GCI-Bench, a five-pillar scoring protocol with a maturity model that makes the trade-offs of collaborative methods comparable across studies. A critical reflection on reproducibility, the sim-to-real gulf, and conditions under which collaboration degrades performance identifies open challenges and charts directions toward general collaborative intelligence under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [50] [M3GA-Wild: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Multi-Modal Multi-session Ground-to-Aerial Place Recognition in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23003)
*Ethan Griffiths,Maryam Haghighat,Simon Denman,Clinton Fookes,Milad Ramezani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向森林环境的"多模态、多会话地对空"地点识别基准 M3GA-Wild，统一并扩展既有森林定位数据集，包含地面RGB+LiDAR、空中影像与多高度LiDAR及6-DoF位姿，基线实验揭示LiDAR优于纯视觉、多模态融合受跨模态对齐限制等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林等非结构化自然环境中的跨平台（地面—空中）长期自主定位缺乏统一、多模态、多会话的评测基准；现有森林定位数据集分散、模态单一，难以系统评估视觉、LiDAR、跨模态与多模态方法的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建统一基准：整合并扩展森林定位数据集，提供同步的RGB与LiDAR地面轨迹（36 km）、对齐的高分辨率航空影像与多高度LiDAR（370 公顷）、精确地理参考的6-DoF位姿；覆盖多视角、遮挡与环境变化场景，支持视觉、LiDAR、跨模态及多模态方法的系统评测；并利用航空RGB与地理参考航空LiDAR配对，评估单目深度估计基础模型作为低成本3D几何来源。

Result: 基线实验表明：在严重视角差异下，基于LiDAR的方法显著优于纯视觉方法；现有多模态融合策略因跨模态对齐不佳而增益有限；单目深度估计基础模型作为森林影像廉价3D几何来源时，初始实验暴露其现有方法的不足。

Conclusion: M3GA-Wild 揭示了跨平台定位中的关键挑战，包括模态错位与严重域间差异，为鲁棒多模态定位与非结构化自然环境中的长期自主性研究提供了新基准（数据集与代码将在录用后公开）。

Abstract: We present M3GA-Wild, the first benchmark for multi-modal, multi-session ground-to-aerial place recognition in forests. M3GA-Wild unifies and extends existing forest localisation datasets, providing a holistic benchmark with synchronised RGB imagery and LiDAR from ground traversals spanning 36 km, aligned high-resolution aerial imagery and multi-altitude LiDAR covering 370 hectares, and accurate geo-referenced 6-DoF poses for precise evaluation. M3GA-Wild captures diverse forest scenes with varying viewpoints, occlusion, and environmental conditions, enabling systematic evaluation of visual, LiDAR, cross-modal, and multi-modal methods. Baseline experiments show that LiDAR-based approaches significantly outperform vision-only methods under severe viewpoint differences, while current multi-modal fusion strategies yield limited gains due to poor cross-modal alignment. By pairing aerial RGB imagery with geo-referenced aerial LiDAR, M3GA-Wild also enables evaluation of foundation models for monocular depth estimation as a cheap source of 3D geometry from forest imagery, with initial experiments revealing shortfalls of current methods. These results highlight key challenges in cross-platform localisation, including modality misalignment and severe domain gaps. M3GA-Wild establishes a new benchmark to support research in robust multi-modal localisation and long-term autonomy in unstructured natural environments. The dataset and code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>

### [51] [Recognition, Simulation, and Refusal: A Contamination-Aware Study of Classic Psychological Effects in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22090)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PsyAgentBench 基准，通过"命名/盲测 × 经典/反事实"因子设计重跑经典心理学实验，证明 LLM 表面类人效应背后存在多种本质不同的机制，主张以"复制剖面"取代标量偏差分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 输出与人类心理效应相符的响应模式，并不等于其真正具备该偏差。二者被现有研究普遍混淆，且常用的标量"偏差易感性"评分掩盖了效应产生的真实机制差异，因此需要一种能分离"响应模式"与"内在偏差"、并刻画机制异质性的评测框架。

Method: 构建 PsyAgentBench，对 LLM 智能体重跑经典心理学范式，采用因子设计：①提示中显式标注范式（named）vs 包装为常规任务（blind）；②教科书原始任务（canonical）vs 结构匹配但降低与训练数据词汇/情境重叠的变体（counterfactual）；并与人格操纵交叉。完成 5 个范式，覆盖至多 3 个开源权重模型家族，释放 41,904 次试验。人格操纵为单句指令式（宜人性），非已验证特质操纵。另形式化并实证记录三种心理学范式向 LLM 智能体移植失败的路径。

Result: 类人效应经不同路径涌现而非单一易感性：①范式标签门控与显式覆盖（Asch 从众，gpt-oss-120B 盲测 0% 至命名 83.3%）；②知识依赖的信号依赖（锚定，有据事实上恰为零，虚构数量上近乎完全，亦与"理性使用唯一可用信号"一致）；③标注下对新颖内容的放大（框架效应）；④稳健缺失（沉没成本）；⑤安全中介的选择，拒绝本身即主要发现（最小群体分配）。单句人格改变可消除、削弱或逆转效应，且因效应而异，不支持单一响应偏差解释。三种移植失败：人格支配、群体坍缩、安全选择。

Conclusion: 标量偏差易感性分数会掩盖效应背后的机制结构，应改用复制剖面（replication profiles）进行报告。

Abstract: An LLM producing the response pattern associated with a human psychological effect is not the same claim as the LLM possessing that bias. We present PsyAgentBench, a benchmark that re-runs classic psychology experiments on LLM agents under a factorial design built to separate these: each paradigm is run with the paradigm explicitly labeled in the prompt (named) or framed as a routine task (blind), and on the literal textbook version of the task (canonical) or a structurally matched variant written to reduce lexical and scenario overlap with likely training data (counterfactual), crossed with a persona manipulation. Across five completed paradigms, evaluated on up to three open-weight model families with 41,904 trials released, apparently human-like effects arise through qualitatively different routes rather than one susceptibility: paradigm-label gating with explicit override (Asch conformity, 0 percent blind to 83.3 percent named on gpt-oss-120B), knowledge-dependent signal reliance (anchoring, exactly zero on grounded facts versus near total on invented quantities, a pattern equally consistent with rational use of the only available signal), amplification on novel content under labeling (framing), robust absence (sunk cost), and safety-mediated selection where refusal itself is the primary finding (minimal-group allocation). A one-sentence persona change (agreeableness, framed as an instruction rather than a verified trait manipulation) eliminates, dampens, or reverses these effects depending on which effect it is, arguing against any single response-bias account. We further formalize, and in two cases document empirically, three ways a psychology paradigm can fail to port to LLM agents: persona dominance, population collapse, and safety selection. We argue scalar bias-susceptibility scores obscure this structure and report replication profiles instead.

</details>


### [52] [Memory That Looks Forward: A Zero-Inference Prospective Term for Personal Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22091)
*Jonathan Groff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需查询时推理的"前瞻性"记忆检索机制：将用户承诺以显式账本（ledger）记录，与承诺条目关联的记忆项获得显著性加成，并以乘法方式融入嵌入检索，从而在不牺牲相关性的前提下提升召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人记忆存储的检索是回溯式的：只返回与查询相似的内容，而对用户已承诺要做的事是"盲"的。需要在检索阶段引入前瞻性（prospective）信号，且希望不增加查询时的推理开销。

Method: 将承诺保存在显式账本中，条目带日期或触发条件；当某条目触发时，与之关联的记忆项获得显著性加成（salience boost），该加成以乘法方式混入基于嵌入的检索得分（保证相关性仍占主导）。并在模拟 TriggerBench 结构的合成前瞻记忆任务集（48 段盲写对话、175 个任务）上评测，含默认混合权重与 floor 变体，并统计误加成率与自然语言承诺-触发对中嵌入相似度失效的比例。

Result: 困难子集 recall@5 从 0.000 提升至默认权重下 0.955、floor 变体下 1.000；在 53 个已解除承诺任务上零误加成。盲写发现：仅 17-29% 的自然表述承诺-触发对会击败嵌入相似度。作者-构造的评测集限制了结论强度；TriggerBench 正式评测为后续工作。

Conclusion: 前提是"预计算承诺关联"作为分层设计中的常开基础层，其扩展层为查询时前瞻（query-time prospection）。该机制仅在少数真实场景中必要，但在其余场景中不造成损害；结果为初步（preliminary），需在 TriggerBench 数据发布后验证。

Abstract: Retrieval over a personal memory store is retrospective: it surfaces what resembles the query, and it is blind to what the user has committed to do. We describe a prospective term for memory retrieval that costs no inference at query time. Commitments are held in an explicit ledger as dated or trigger-conditioned entries; memory items linked to a firing entry receive a salience boost, blended multiplicatively into embedding-based retrieval so that relevance remains sovereign. On a synthetic prospective-memory task set modeled on TriggerBench's published structure (48 blind-authored dialogues, 175 tasks), the term raised recall@5 on the hard stratum from 0.000 to 0.955 at the default blend weight and to 1.000 under a floor variant, with zero false boosts across 53 resolved-commitment tasks. Blind authorship also produced a scope finding: only 17-29% of naturally phrased commitment-trigger pairs defeat embedding similarity, so the term matters on a real minority of cases and must do no harm on the rest, which it does not. We position precomputed commitment linkage as the always-on floor of a layered design whose expansion layer is query-time prospection. Results are preliminary: the evaluation set is author-constructed, and evaluation on TriggerBench proper is committed follow-up work once its data is released.

</details>


### [53] [Summarize, Judge, Refine: Decoupled Content Understanding and Policy Learning for Multimodal Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22094)
*Zeeshan Ahmed,Yang Qin,Hanqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR) 双模型架构，通过自然语言接口将多模态内容理解与政策特定分类解耦，实现策略变更无需重训全流程、并借助文本空间增强解决多模态标注稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容审核系统将多模态理解与政策特定分类耦合，导致政策变更时需重训整个流水线，同时多媒体数据难以有效增强而面临标签稀缺问题。

Method: SJR 采用双模型架构：多模态 Content Model 生成结构化文本摘要，纯文本 Policy Model 依据政策定义对摘要分类。通过迭代协同训练循环用 GRPO 优化 Content Model 生成政策相关摘要；文本空间增强生成对抗性摘要变体（原始多模态上不可行的增强路径），支持少样本政策冷启动。所有决策基于人类可读摘要，可解释性成为结构性副产物。

Result: 在误导性广告检测任务上，SJR 相较零样本思维链基线在非误导类 F1 上取得 +23.6\% 的相对提升，优于端到端 SFT、STaR/RFT 和 RLFT。一个仅在零真实违规样本（正类数据全部合成生成）上训练的变体，在违规类 F1 上与全量数据模型相对差距在 0.2\% 以内。

Conclusion: SJR 通过解耦内容理解与政策分类，证明新政策无需任何真实违规数据即可上线部署，同时以摘要为证据提供内在可解释性。

Abstract: Content moderation systems traditionally entangle multimodal understanding with policy-specific classification, requiring full pipeline retraining for every policy change and suffering from label scarcity since multimedia cannot be meaningfully augmented. We propose Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR), a two-model architecture that decouples these concerns via a natural language interface: a multimodal Content Model produces structured text summaries, and a text-only Policy Model classifies them against policy definitions. An iterative co-training loop refines the Content Model via GRPO to produce policy-relevant summaries, while text-space augmentation generates adversarial summary variants---an augmentation pathway impossible on raw multimedia---enabling few-shot policy bootstrap. Every decision is grounded in a human-readable summary, providing interpretability as a structural byproduct. On misleading advertisement detection, SJR achieves +23.6\% relative non-misleading F1 over a zero-shot chain-of-thought baseline, outperforming end-to-end SFT, STaR/RFT, and RLFT. Notably, a variant trained on zero real violating examples---with all positive-class data synthetically generated---matches the full-data model within 0.2\% relative on violating F1, demonstrating that new policies can launch without any real violation data.

</details>


### [54] [AI-inferred expressed well-being and collective-action discourse in climate-change campaigns on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22096)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究基于19个出现年份中四个气候活动日（地球日、地球一小时、全球气候行动日、世界环境日）的364,118条Twitter/X帖子，使用加权词汇模型测量幸福感、未来导向希望、集体能力、痛苦与行动语言。结果显示活动期间幸福感比活动前基线高9.02个百分点，而行动语言配对对比下降10.75个百分点，呈现"幸福—行动背离"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候运动常以关注度和动员程度来评估，但伴随运动的"福祉语言"（well-being language）研究不足。活动期是否改变积极情感与希望、幸福感是否与行动语言一致，尚无定论。

Method: 分析19个出现年份中四个气候活动日的364,118条公开Twitter/X帖子，采用30天活动前、活动中、活动后时间窗；使用带版本的加权词汇模型（versioned weighted lexical model）估计幸福感、未来导向希望、集体能力、痛苦和行动语言；并进行文本去重与构成稳健性检验、19簇wild bootstrap及匹配转发级联分析。

Result: 活动期幸福感流行度比活动前基线高9.02个百分点；配对出现年份对比显示行动语言下降10.75个百分点，表明幸福—行动背离。幸福感估计在构成与文本去重检验中保持为正，但在19簇wild bootstrap下精度降低。幸福感更高的源帖子出现匹配转发级联的几率更低。

Conclusion: 气候活动日期间公共话语中的幸福感上升，但并未伴随行动语言的同步增强，二者出现背离；且更高的幸福感与更低的转发级联几率相关，提示积极情感表达未必转化为动员传播。

Abstract: Climate campaigns are often evaluated through attention and mobilization, but less is known about the well-being language that accompanies them. Whether campaign periods alter positive affect and hope, and whether happiness aligns with action language, remains unresolved. We analysed 364,118 public Twitter/X posts from Earth Day, Earth Hour, Global Climate Action Day and World Environment Day in 19 occurrence-years, using 30-day pre-event, event and post-event windows. A versioned weighted lexical model estimated happiness, future-oriented hope, collective capability, distress and action language. Event-period happiness prevalence was 9.02 percentage points higher than the pre-event baseline , whereas paired occurrence contrasts showed a 10.75-point decline in action language, indicating a happiness--action divergence. The happiness estimate remained positive across composition and text-deduplication checks, but was less precise under a 19-cluster wild bootstrap. Happier source posts had lower odds of an observed matched retweet cascade.

</details>


### [55] [Token Signatures of Code: Comparing Coding Behaviors Across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22097)
*Junpeng Wang,Yuzhong Chen,Menghai Pan,Uday Singh Saini,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLIC，一种基于 token 频率分析的视觉分析方法，用于刻画与比较 LLM 的编码行为。CLIC 将代码样本表示为 token 频率特征向量，训练可解释决策树区分两个 LLM 的代码集合，并定义鲁棒性与集中度两个新指标。配套的交互式可视分析系统支持多尺度、假设驱动的探索。在 22 个 Kaggle ML 任务上比较 10 个 LLM，得到对模型选择与提示工程有指导意义的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 编码能力评估主要依赖 pass@k 等性能指标。随着模型整体水平提升，多数模型已满足基线要求，性能指标本身区分度下降，难以刻画模型间差异。而"不同 LLM 的编码行为究竟如何不同"这一问题仍缺乏探索。作者因此转向行为层面的比较，希望超越单一性能分数，揭示模型编码风格的差异。

Method: 提出 CLIC 视觉分析方法：1）将每个代码样本表示为 token 频率特征向量；2）训练可解释决策树来区分两个 LLM 的代码集合，从而识别最具判别性的 token；3）在分类准确率之外定义两个新指标——鲁棒性（逐步移除最具判别性 token 后两个模型是否仍可区分）与集中度（差异由少数主导 token 驱动还是分散于大量 token）。由于 pairwise 比较涉及模型对、任务、tokenization 层级等多维度，且需追踪完整分析链路，作者进一步开发交互式可视分析系统，支持在比较全景中导航、定位感兴趣的模型对，并下钻到判别性 token 及其代码上下文。

Result: 在 22 个 Kaggle 机器学习任务上对 10 个 LLM 进行了案例研究，验证了方法有效性，并产出对 LLM 选择与提示工程具有可操作价值的洞察。

Conclusion: CLIC 将 LLM 编码评估从单一性能指标拓展到基于 token 频率的行为比较，通过可解释决策树、鲁棒性与集中度指标以及交互式可视分析系统，支持跨模型对、任务和 tokenization 层级的假设驱动探索。该方法能够揭示性能指标难以捕捉的模型差异，为模型选择与提示设计提供实证依据。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on coding tasks has primarily focused on performance metrics such as pass@k. As LLMs continue to advance, many models now meet baseline performance requirements, reducing the discriminative power of performance-based evaluation alone. Yet a key question remains largely unexplored: how do LLMs differ in their coding behavior? We propose CLIC (Code Learning for Identification and Comparison), a visual analytics approach that characterizes LLM coding behavior through token-frequency analysis. CLIC represents each code sample as a feature vector of token frequencies and trains an interpretable decision tree to separate two LLMs' code sets. Beyond classification accuracy, we define two new metrics: robustness, which measures whether the two LLMs remain distinguishable as their most-discriminative tokens are progressively removed, and concentration, which measures whether the difference is driven by a few dominant tokens or spread across many. Interpreting numerous pairwise comparisons (across LLM pairs, tasks, and tokenization levels) and tracing the full analytical chain form an inherently multi-scale, hypothesis-driven exploration task. We therefore develop an interactive visual analytics system to navigate the comparison landscape, identify pairs of interest, and drill down into discriminative tokens and their code contexts. Case studies comparing 10 LLMs across 22 Kaggle ML tasks reveal actionable insights for LLM selection and prompt engineering.

</details>


### [56] [TreeSpark: Calibrated, Load-Adaptive Draft Trees for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22098)
*Huapeng Zhou,Huayu Wang,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeSpark 提出一种基于路径存活率的自适应推测解码树构建与调度方法，用 drafter 现有 Markov 头读取父条件分布并校准为边接受率，实现最优优先扩展、每轮停止与负载自适应服务，在无损前提下比调优链式基线每轮多接受 15-25% 草稿 token，单请求墙钟加速 8-14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 block drafter 使草稿近乎零成本（单次 backbone 前向生成整块 token），但推测树构造存在两个缺陷：(1) 候选排序仅用逐位置 marginal，忽略了候选所继承的父节点，在半自回归 drafter 上扩大树宽只增加排序错误的节点；(2) 固定树大小无法适应每轮解码可支持的推测量以及不同服务负载。因此需要一种父条件化、自适应的树构造与调度策略。

Method: 从 drafter 已有的 Markov head 以极低开销读取父条件分布，校准为边接受率估计；以路径存活率驱动全部决策：最优优先（best-first）扩展、每轮停止准则、负载自适应服务策略。采样方面采用无放回兄弟采样，并在递归拒绝中匹配残差，保证任意温度下解码无损。

Result: 自适应树在各温度下均优于匹配的固定预算；在同一 drafter 上与调优链式基线相比，每轮多接受 15-25% 草稿 token；单请求墙钟解码快 8-14%；在负载升高时树可平滑退化为链式。

Conclusion: 通过父条件化分布、路径存活率驱动与负载自适应，TreeSpark 在保持无损解码的同时提升了推测解码的接受率与端到端速度，并具备良好的负载弹性。代码与工件已开源于 GitHub。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by letting a cheap drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressive drafters wider trees mostly add mis-ranked nodes; and a tree of fixed size ignores how much speculation each decoding round, and each serving load, can support. We introduce TreeSpark, which reads a parent-conditioned distribution from the drafter's existing Markov head at negligible cost, calibrates it into an edge-acceptance estimate, and lets path survival govern everything else: best-first expansion, per-round stopping, and a load-adaptive serving policy. Sampling siblings without replacement, with matching residuals in recursive rejection, keeps decoding lossless at any temperature. Adaptive trees improve on matched fixed budgets at every temperature; against a tuned chain on the same drafter, TreeSpark accepts 15-25% more draft tokens per round and decodes 8-14% faster in single-request wall-clock, and under rising load it gracefully shrinks the tree back to the chain. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/PopSoda2002/TreeSpark

</details>


### [57] [A framework for recipe data structure with applications for culinary and nutritional insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22099)
*Mansi Goel,Sumit Bhagat,Saloni Srivastava,Malav Patel,Shlok Vinodkumar Mehroliya,Ganesh Bagler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对食谱数据缺乏统一可计算表示的问题，提出食谱数据结构的形式化框架，并构建了RecipeDB2数据库，覆盖128,942份食谱、35,474种食材，整合营养、地理文化与饮食属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食谱虽承载原料转化为菜肴的复杂过程，但主要以自由文本形式存在，人类可读却难以直接计算。现有食谱集合只捕获零散信息，缺少能在单一可查询模式内连接结构化食材组成、地理文化来源与营养特征的共享表示，因此存在表示缺口。

Method: 形式化食谱数据结构框架：将每份食谱分解为带类型的食材实体，映射到参考营养数据库，并标注地理文化与饮食背景。构建RecipeDB2：用基于Transformer的命名实体模型将食材短语解析为7种烹饪属性；用BERT嵌入策略链接USDA参考表；用随机森林分类器向全词汇表传播34个食材类别；用确定性保守规则集为每份食谱分配饮食风格。

Result: RecipeDB2包含来自32个地区、99个国家的128,942份食谱和35,474种食材。食材与USDA参考表链接在200个最高频食材的人工裁决集上F1=87.90，每个映射食材获得148个营养参数；随机森林传播34个食材类别；规则集分配饮食风格。数据库在线地址为https://cosylab.iiitd.edu.in/recipedb2/。

Conclusion: RecipeDB2展示了使食谱可计算的规模化框架，将长期被视为艺术而非量化对象的烹饪遗产转化为数据驱动分析，为连接食材组成、地理文化来源与营养特征提供了统一可查询模式。

Abstract: Cooking is a complex process that transforms raw ingredients into delicious and nutritious dishes, yet the recipes that encode this process remain largely free text; readable by people but not directly computable. Existing recipe collections capture fragments of this information, but no shared representation links a recipe's structured ingredient composition, its geo-cultural provenance, and its nutritional profile within a single queryable schema. We address this representation gap by formalizing a framework for recipe data structure that decomposes each recipe into typed ingredient entities, grounds those entities in a reference nutritional database, and annotates them with geo-cultural and dietary context. We present RecipeDB2, a structured compilation of 128,942 recipes with 35,474 ingredients from 32 regions and 99 countries. Ingredient phrases are parsed into seven culinary attributes using a transformer-based named-entity model; ingredients are linked to the USDA reference tables through a BERT embedding strategy (F1 = 87.90 on a manually adjudicated set of the 200 most frequent ingredients), yielding 148 nutritional parameters per mapped ingredient; a Random Forest classifier propagates 34 ingredient categories across the full vocabulary; and a deterministic, conservative rule set assigns each recipe a dietary style. Through RecipeDB2 (https://cosylab.iiitd.edu.in/recipedb2/), we demonstrate a scalable framework for making recipes computable, turning culinary heritage (long treated as an artistic rather than a quantitative object) into a data-driven analysis.

</details>


### [58] [AdaMem: Adaptive Memory Token Allocation for Soft Compression in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22100)
*Artem Sakhno,Grigorii Davydenko,Omar Zoloev,Julia Belikova,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AdaMem，一种基于相关性的软压缩框架，将段落相关性估计映射为查询相关的记忆令牌分配，在固定预算下提升 RAG 效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 处理大量长段落成本高且引入干扰信息；软压缩将其编码为紧凑连续记忆嵌入，但现有方法对每个保留段落分配相同的记忆嵌入数量，忽略查询特定的相关性差异。

Method: AdaMem 使用共享的查询条件压缩器，单次前向同时生成连续段落记忆与相关性分数；再通过确定性分配规则在固定记忆令牌预算下，为高分段落分配更多记忆令牌，并可省略低分段落。

Result: 在六个开放域 QA 基准上，AdaMem 在相同记忆预算下持续优于均匀分配的软压缩基线 OSCAR 及其他软压缩方法。16× 压缩下子串匹配最高提升 3.2 点（5.5%），平均相对增益 3.4%；64× 压缩下平均相对增益达 14.6%，PopQA 上最高提升 9.8 点（19.7%）。可达到未压缩答案质量，推理延迟比全上下文基线低至多 4×。

Conclusion: 相关性引导的记忆分配在检索池大且记忆预算紧张时尤为有效，且效率与均匀压缩基线相当。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models with retrieved evidence, but processing many long passages is costly and can introduce distracting information. Soft compression addresses this challenge by encoding passages as compact sequences of continuous memory embeddings before generation. However, existing methods typically assign each retained passage an identical number of memory embeddings, irrespective of its query-specific relevance. To address this, we propose AdaMem, a relevance-guided soft-compression framework that maps learned passage-relevance estimates to a query-dependent allocation of a fixed memory-token budget. A shared query-conditioned compressor produces both continuous passage memories and relevance scores in a single pass; a deterministic allocation rule assigns more memory tokens to higher-scoring passages and can omit low-scoring ones. Across six open-domain QA benchmarks, AdaMem consistently outperforms OSCAR (the closely matched soft-compression baseline that uses uniform allocation) as well as other soft-compression methods at matched memory budgets. Under standard 16$\times$ compression, AdaMem improves sub-string match by up to 3.2 points (5.5%) over uniform allocation baseline, with an average relative gain of 3.4%; under aggressive 64$\times$ compression the average relative gain grows to 14.6%, with a maximum of 9.8 points (19.7%) on PopQA. AdaMem matches the answer quality of the uncompressed at up to 4$\times$ lower inference latency than full context baseline. AdaMem retains an efficiency profile comparable to the uniform-compression baseline, while achieving up to $4\times$ lower inference latency than full-context inference. Thus, relevance-guided memory allocation is particularly effective when retrieval pools are large and the available memory budget is tight.

</details>


### [59] [Context Poisoning as Extreme-Value Attention Interference in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22101)
*Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Bhaskar Sen,Nasim Sabetpour,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"上下文投毒"现象，即长上下文中关键证据的检索能力随干扰项增多而退化，并从注意力极值干扰角度给出理论刻画与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能处理越来越长的提示，但当无关或易混淆的上下文增加时，其定位并使用决定性证据的能力会下降。作者希望揭示这一"长上下文退化"背后的机制，并将其形式化为可分析的数学问题。

Method: 将问题建模为注意力中的极值干扰：决定性证据得分存在上界，而有效干扰项的最大得分随其数量增长。在softmax检索抽象下推导有限样本上界，证明在基准率之上维持固定准确率需要证据间隔按Ω(√log N)增长（N为有效干扰项数，而非原始上下文长度）。分析关联了分数混叠、位置混叠与softmax稀释三种机制，并通过受控实验（嵌入难负例、同格式干扰项、检索门控）加以验证。

Result: 实验显示：存在嵌入难负例时检索准确率随总上下文增长而下降；在固定上下文长度下，同格式条件下观测到的准确率下降幅度最大；检索门控可改善证据使用，但其净收益取决于能否保持证据召回率。

Conclusion: 结果指向若干应对方向：证据瓶颈、抗混叠表示、先检索后推理架构、验证器中介记忆，以及对比式抗投毒训练。

Abstract: Large language models can process increasingly long prompts, yet their ability to locate and use decisive evidence may degrade as irrelevant or confusable context is added. We formulate this phenomenon, which we call context poisoning, as extreme-value interference in attention: the decisive-evidence score is upper-bounded, while the maximum score among effective distractors grows with their number. Under a softmax retrieval abstraction, we derive a finite-sample upper bound showing that maintaining a fixed accuracy target above base rate requires the evidence margin to scale as $Ω(\sqrt{\log N})$, where N denotes the effective distractor count rather than necessarily the raw context length. The analysis connects long-context degradation to score aliasing, positional aliasing, and softmax dilution. Controlled experiments show that retrieval accuracy decreases as total context grows in the presence of embedded hard negatives, that the same-format condition produces the largest observed accuracy drop among the tested distractor constructions at fixed context length, and that retrieval gating can improve evidence use while its net benefit depends on preserving evidence recall. These results motivate evidence bottlenecks, alias-resistant representations, retrieve-then-reason architectures, verifier-mediated memory, and contrastive anti-poison training.

</details>


### [60] [DeepInstructor: An Agentic AI Instructor for Experience-Driven Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22104)
*Rongcan Pei,Fang Guo,Qinglin Qi,Qi Zhu,Yun Luo,Jianhao Yan,Minjun Zhu,Qiujie Xie,Dehong Zheng,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepInstructor，一个将科研想法评估建模为对结构化学术经验推理的智能体框架，基于58,607条同行评审构建经验图，并用ReAct智能体检索分维度证据，实现可追溯评估，同时发布DeepInstruct数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型推动自动化科学发现，研究想法的生成规模空前扩大，瓶颈已从"想法生成"转向"想法评估"。现有评估器主要依赖参数化知识或无结构化检索，缺乏人类导师那种基于经验的推理，判断结果不够可解释、可追溯。

Method: 提出DeepInstructor框架：从58,607条同行评审中构建"经验图"（Experience Graph），并采用基于ReAct的智能体，针对不同评估维度检索具体证据，从而生成可追溯的评估；同时构建DeepInstruct数据集，在新颖性、重要性、可行性三个维度上进行受控的两两比较。

Result: DeepInstructor显著优于现有基线，与人类判断的对齐度Hit@1和Hit@2分别提升24.4%和29.7%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，科学想法评估可以建立在结构化学术经验之上的显式推理之上，为可追溯、可解释的想法评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: As automated scientific discovery advances, Large Language Models (LLMs) can now generate research ideas at an unprecedented scale, shifting the bottleneck from idea generation to idea evaluation. Existing evaluators mainly rely on parametric LLM knowledge or unstructured retrieval, producing judgments that lack the experience-grounded reasoning used by human instructors. To address this, we propose DeepInstructor, an agentic framework that formulates idea evaluation as reasoning over structured scholarly experience. DeepInstructor constructs an Experience Graph from 58,607 peer reviews and employs a ReAct-based agent to retrieve dimension-specific evidence for traceable evaluation. We further introduce DeepInstruct, a dataset with controlled pairwise comparisons across novelty, significance, and feasibility. Experiments show that DeepInstructor substantially outperforms existing baselines, improving Hit@1 and Hit@2 alignment with human judgments by 24.4% and 29.7%, respectively. Our findings suggest that scientific idea evaluation can be grounded in explicit reasoning over structured scholarly experience

</details>


### [61] [Evaluating Fine-Tuned and Base Language Models in Maternal and Vaccination Healthcare for African Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22110)
*Abdulquddus Ajibade,Oluwaseun Odunsi,Iyinoluwa Animasaun,Chioma Nwakanma-Akanno,Oluwasegun Oguntuase,Oluwafunke Akinbuwa,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了尼日利亚母婴健康与疫苗接种场景下LoRA领域微调对LLM性能的影响：MamaBot-Llama全面优于基座模型，而Vax-Llama反而显著退化，说明微调效果高度依赖数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可改善低资源地区的医疗信息传递，但可能输出不准确或不符合当地文化的建议。作者希望验证领域专用微调能否提升尼日利亚母婴健康与疫苗接种场景下模型的安全性、准确性与可信度。

Method: 共评估200个医疗问题（母婴健康与疫苗接种各100个，每领域覆盖5个子领域）。MamaBot-Llama与Vax-Llama分别在超过36,000与9,000条问答对上采用低秩适配（LoRA）进行微调。由两名尼日利亚执业医师使用5点Likert量表独立评分，并以Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行配对比较。

Result: 性能因领域而异。MamaBot-Llama在所有指标上显著优于基座模型，总体提升4.9%（p<.001），临床可信度+7%、医学准确性+5%，关键问题减少50%，临床医生在78%的案例中更偏好该模型。相反，Vax-Llama总体下降5.2%（p<.001），关键问题增加192%，安全性担忧增加400%。

Conclusion: 当基于高质量、临床医生审校的数据时，领域专用微调可提升医疗LLM表现；但数据质量不足时也可能导致性能退化。临床部署前必须进行严格的领域专用验证。（医师评估者已获得知情同意，聊天机器人日志已匿名化。）

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) can improve healthcare information delivery in low-resource settings but may produce inaccurate or culturally inappropriate advice. This study evaluated domain-specific fine-tuning for maternal health and vaccination in Nigeria. Objective: To compare HelpMum's MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama with Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct for accuracy, safety, clarity, contextual appropriateness, and trustworthiness. Methods: We evaluated 200 healthcare questions, 100 each for maternal health and vaccination, across five subdomains per domain. MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation on over 36,000 maternal health and 9,000 vaccination question-answer pairs, respectively. Two Nigerian licensed physicians independently rated responses using a 5-point Likert scale. Paired comparisons used Wilcoxon signed-rank tests. Results: Performance varied by domain. MamaBot-Llama significantly outperformed the base model across all criteria, with a 4.9% overall improvement (p < .001), including gains in clinical trustworthiness (+7%) and medical accuracy (+5%). Critical issues decreased by 50%, and clinicians preferred it in 78% of cases. In contrast, Vax-Llama showed a 5.2% overall decline (p < .001), with critical issues increasing by 192% and safety concerns by 400%. Conclusions: Domain-specific fine-tuning can improve healthcare LLM performance when based on high-quality, clinician-curated data, but may also degrade performance when dataset quality is inadequate. Rigorous domain-specific validation is essential before clinical deployment. Physician evaluators provided informed consent, and chatbot logs were anonymized. Keywords: Large language models; Fine-tuning; Maternal health; Vaccination; Healthcare AI; Low-resource settings; Nigeria; Model evaluation; LoRA; Medical accuracy

</details>


### [62] [Beyond the Text: Verifying That Agent-Written Papers Are Backed by Their Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22111)
*Qiuhong Shen,Benlong Wu,Hanjin Liu,Yuang Qi,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReAgent，一个自动审计框架，用于评估 LLM 智能体生成的研究文档与其配套代码仓库之间的一致性，通过静态审计与动态审计相结合识别硬编码指标、未实现方法、实验证据缺失等不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体已能自主开展研究并产出文档、代码和实验，但报告结论是否真正被实现与执行证据支持尚不明确。现有评审主要评估文本质量，无法可靠发现硬编码指标、未实现方法或未经支持的实验结果等不一致。

Method: ReAgent 从研究文档中构建科学主张的结构化表示，并据此指导仓库分析与证据收集。静态审计检查所声称的方法、实现与实验配置是否在仓库中一致体现；动态审计执行相关实验并收集执行证据以验证实证结论。两者结合可发现单一视角下难以察觉的不一致，如复现了报告数值却偏离所声称方法。最终将证据与审计判定组织为结构化的仓库级审计报告，实现透明的证据可追溯。

Result: 在人工整理的智能体生成研究文档—仓库配对基准上评估 ReAgent，并与代表性静态基线和基于复现的基线进行比较。实验结果表明 ReAgent 能有效识别报告研究结论与其支撑仓库证据之间的不一致。

Conclusion: ReAgent 通过融合静态分析与动态执行证据，为智能体自主研究成果提供了一种可追溯的一致性审计方案，弥补了仅依赖文本质量评审的不足。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly capable of conducting research autonomously, producing research documents alongside the code and experiments that ostensibly support them. Yet whether the reported findings are consistently supported by corresponding implementations and execution evidence remains largely unexplored: existing review practices primarily assess textual quality and cannot reliably identify inconsistencies such as hard-coded metrics, unimplemented methods, or unsupported experimental results. We present ReAgent, an automated auditing framework for assessing the consistency between agent-generated research documents and their associated repositories. ReAgent constructs structured representations of scientific claims from research documents and uses them to guide repository analysis and evidence collection. Static auditing examines whether claimed methodologies, implementations, and experimental configurations are consistently reflected in the repository, while dynamic auditing executes relevant experiments and collects execution evidence to assess empirical findings. By combining static analysis with dynamic evidence, ReAgent identifies inconsistencies that may remain hidden under either perspective alone, such as experiments that reproduce reported numbers while deviating from the claimed methodology. The collected evidence and audit decisions are organized into a structured repository-level audit report, enabling transparent evidence traceability. We evaluate ReAgent on a manually curated benchmark of agent-generated research document--repository pairs and compare it against representative static and reproduction-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that ReAgent effectively identifies inconsistencies between reported research findings and their supporting repository evidence.

</details>


### [63] [Privacy Personalization Trade offs in LLMs: The Impact of Stylometric Signal Reduction on User-Specific Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22112)
*Muhammed Nazmul Arefin,Omar Jamal Hammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过LaMP-7 Twitter基准构建受控框架，比较"基于原始画像"与"基于匿名化画像"两种改写条件下的LLM个性化文本生成，发现匿名化使输出偏好度降至平均13.0%，而语义保持率仍达94.8%，揭示隐私与个性化之间存在明显权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能以高风格保真度生成用户专属文本，但实现个性化所依赖的个人数据常包含人口统计、文化及风格标记，引发文体计量学再识别（stylometric re-identification）的隐私担忧。论文旨在探究削弱可识别风格信号是否会影响LLM文本生成的个性化效果。

Method: 提出一个受控框架，用于在LLM个性化中隔离文体计量信号，基于LaMP-7 Twitter基准开展实验。选取250名用户，对比两种设置：以原始画像为条件的改写，以及以匿名化转换画像（系统性中性化人口统计标识、文化指涉、个人细节与非正式语言线索）为条件的改写。输出由两个独立LLM评审及一项补充人工评估进行评价。

Result: 成对评估显示，基于原始画像的输出与人类撰写的真实文本几乎无法区分，表明现代LLM能以足够保真度复现作者写作风格；相比之下，匿名化画像下模型输出被偏好比例平均仅13.0%，而语义上下文保持率仍高达94.8%。人工评估研究证实了相同模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了明确的隐私—个性化权衡，表明需要发展隐私感知的个性化方法，在保留语义的同时抑制可识别的风格信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to generate user-specific text with high stylistic fidelity. However, the personal data that enables such personalization frequently embeds demographic, cultural, and stylistic markers that raises concerns about stylometric re- identification. This paper investigates whether reducing identifiable stylistic signals affects personalization in text generation by LLMs. We introduce a controlled framework to isolate stylometric signals in LLM personalization using the LaMP-7 Twitter benchmark. Experiments on 250 sampled users compare two settings: paraphrasing conditioned on the original profile and paraphrasing conditioned on an anonymized converted profile in which demographic identifiers, cultural references, personal details, and informal linguistic cues have been systematically neutralized. Outputs are assessed by two independent LLM judges and a complementary human evaluation. Our pairwise evaluation shows that outputs conditioned on original profiles are nearly indistinguishable from human-authored ground truth, indicating that modern LLMs can closely reproduce an author's writing style with sufficient fidelity. In contrast, preference for model outputs with anonymized profiles drops to 13.0% on average, while semantic context preservation remains high at 94.8%. A study with human evaluators confirms the same pattern. These findings reveal a clear privacy-personalization trade-off and highlight the need for privacy-aware personalization methods that retain meaning while suppressing identifying stylistic signals.

</details>


### [64] [An Empirical Cost Attribution of Context-Compression Gateways in Multi-Turn Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22114)
*Luzhuo Chen,Jiayu Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在生产级压缩网关（Paritok）上，对编码智能体（Claude Code、Codex）与前沿LLM（Claude Sonnet、GPT-5）之间的真实多轮会话token账单进行分解，发现三种省token手段（工具schema过滤、内容压缩、历史摘要）的节省机制与速率根本不同，并指出单轮压缩基准的高分与多轮agent成本正交，不能作为省钱论据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设"上下文压缩能省钱"且"公开基准显示激进压缩可保持任务质量"，但这两点并不能推出"压缩文件读取在真实多轮agent中就能省钱"。作者旨在验证这一被默认接受却缺乏实测支撑的推论。

Method: 在生产环境部署压缩网关Paritok，置于编码智能体（Claude Code、Codex）与前沿LLM（Claude Sonnet、GPT-5）之间，将真实会话的token账单拆解为三个独立杠杆：工具schema过滤、文件读取与工具输出的内容压缩、历史摘要。通过受控A/B运行对三者进行隔离测量，并考察非破坏性网关下的字节召回（recall）成本。

Result: 三者的节省速率差异本质性：1) 工具schema过滤每轮固定减少约21K–57K tokens，随轮数N线性增长，是唯一明确且可复现的正向杠杆；2) 内容压缩每轮仅节省约2%的缓存价前缀，但压缩后的读取累积进历史并在后续每轮重发，故累计节省呈二次增长，约3350·N² tokens（实测），约6轮内即超过固定工具过滤的节省，直到被上下文窗口封顶；3) 非破坏性网关允许agent按需拉回原始字节，每次召回仅重发刚被压缩的那一段，成本固定且有界，而非乘性爆炸，重度召回会逐段花掉累计节省。此外，单轮强压缩基准（保留86.5% SWE-bench质量、25.7%压缩率，由网关部署的Paritok-4B达成）与多轮agent成本正交。

Conclusion: 单轮压缩基准的高质量保留不能作为多轮agent省钱的论据；在多轮真实编码会话中，工具schema过滤是唯一稳健的正向杠杆，内容压缩的收益来自其随历史累积的二次增长，非破坏性召回使成本有界。作者据此提炼出一份可操作的"省token投入应投向何处"的配方。

Abstract: Context compression is widely proposed as a way to cut the token bill of LLM coding agents, and public benchmarks report that aggressive compression preserves task-solving quality. These two facts do not imply the third one commonly assumed: that compressing file reads saves money in a real multi-turn agent. We instrument a production compression gateway (Paritok) between coding agents (Claude Code, Codex) and frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet, GPT-5), and decompose the token bill of real sessions into three independent levers: tool-schema filtering, content compression of file reads and tool output, and history summarization. Measured in isolation under controlled A/B runs, the three save at fundamentally different rates. Tool-schema filtering removes a fixed block every turn, roughly 21K-57K tokens on a typical turn; it is linear in the turn count N and the only unambiguously and reproducibly positive lever. Content compression saves only about 2% of the cache-priced prefix per turn, but compressed reads accumulate in history and are re-sent on every later turn, so its cumulative saving grows quadratically, about 3350*N^2 tokens (measured), overtaking the fixed tool-filter saving within roughly 6 turns until the context window caps it. A non-destructive gateway lets the agent pull original bytes back on demand; each recall re-sends exactly the one segment just compressed away, so its cost is fixed and bounded rather than a multiplicative blowup, and heavy recall spends the accumulated saving back one segment at a time. Finally, a strong single-shot compression benchmark - 86.5% of SWE-bench quality retained at a 25.7% compression rate, achieved by the model this gateway deploys (Paritok-4B, reported separately) - is orthogonal to multi-turn agent cost and must not be cited as a cost-saving argument. We distill the results into an actionable recipe for where token-saving effort pays off.

</details>


### [65] [Evaluation Awareness Shifts from Format to Context with Model Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22119)
*Navraj Singh,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究紧凑型语言模型如何检测评估情境（评估意识），并揭示小模型与大模型的机制差异，提出双重通路干预方法来抑制评估意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估意识对模型评估构成前所未有的威胁，但模型检测评估意识的机制尚不明确，且不同规模模型间可能存在机制差异，需要系统探究并寻找有效的缓解方法。

Method: 评估Gemma 3（1B、4B、12B）、Phi-3（Mini、Medium）和Llama-3 8B；采用思维链分析、表征探针和积分梯度归因三种手段；基于发现提出双重通路干预，结合提示净化与激活反引导，同时抑制外部评估触发因素和内部表征。

Result: 较小模型依赖提示格式敏感性来检测评估，较大模型则常依赖高阶推理。在200个高评估意识提示上，双重通路干预实现平均70.58%的行为翻转率，持续优于单一干预。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了紧凑型语言模型中评估意识的发展机制，表明有效缓解需同时处理提示层面和表征层面的信号。

Abstract: Evaluation awareness poses an unprecedented threat to model evaluation, but the mechanisms by which models detect it remain unknown. This study focuses on determining this and identifying contrasting mechanisms between smaller and larger models. While smaller models use the prompt's format sensitivity to detect evaluation, larger models often rely on higher-order reasoning to detect it. We evaluated Gemma 3 (1B, 4B, and 12B), Phi-3 (Mini and Medium), and Llama-3 8B using Chain-of-Thought analysis, representation probing, and Integrated Gradients attribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose a dual-pathway intervention that combines prompt sanitization with activation counter-steering to suppress both external evaluation triggers and their internal representations. Across 200 highly evaluation-aware prompts, our method achieves an average behavioral flip rate of 70.58\%, consistently outperforming either intervention alone. These results provide new insights into how evaluation awareness develops in compact language models and suggest that effective mitigation requires jointly addressing both prompt-level and representation-level signals.Datasets and codebase can be found in this \href{https://github.com/chahal-navi/Evaluation-Awareness-Compact-LLMs/tree/main}{Github Repository.}

</details>


### [66] [Balancing Reasoning and Hardware Constraints in RAG Pipelines for Ukrainian Multi-Domain Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22124)
*Illya Havrylov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个参加UNLP 2026多域文档理解共享任务的系统，结合BM25、BGE-M3与Cross-Encoder重排构建高效混合RAG流水线，并使用4-bit量化的LapaLLM 12B，在严格时间限制下取得Private Score 0.8095，排名第10/15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务要求从乌克兰语PDF语料中抽取精确答案、文档ID和页码，且受限于9小时离线Kaggle执行时限。评估中发现扫描文档的OCR成为严重瓶颈（耗时5-7小时），仅剩约2小时用于500个问题的LLM推理，因此需要构建能在有限资源下稳定完成的资源高效型流水线。

Method: 采用混合检索增强生成（RAG）流水线，检索模块结合BM25、BGE-M3和Cross-Encoder重排；生成模块使用基于llama.cpp在双NVIDIA T4 GPU上运行的4-bit量化LapaLLM 12B模型。为避免参数密集型推理模型（如DeepSeek R1）导致的超时，优先保证流水线稳定性而非多步推理能力。

Result: 系统在隐藏私有测试集上取得Private Score 0.8095，在15支活跃队伍中排名第10。

Conclusion: 在严格时间和硬件约束下，通过资源高效的混合RAG设计与量化小模型，可实现稳定的文档理解流水线；但OCR的串行执行与推理时间限制仍是主要瓶颈，未来需优化扫描文档处理效率以提升整体性能。

Abstract: This paper describes the system submitted to the UNLP 2026 Shared Task on Multi-Domain Document Understanding. The challenge required extracting precise answers, document IDs, and page numbers from a diverse corpus of Ukrainian PDF documents within a strict 9-hour offline Kaggle execution limit. During evaluation on the hidden private test set, optical character recognition (OCR) of scanned documents emerged as a severe bottleneck, consuming 5-7 hours of the total time budget due to sequential single-threaded execution. This overhead strictly limited the remaining time for Large Language Model (LLM) inference to approximately two hours for 500 questions. To guarantee pipeline completion without timeouts, we developed a resource-efficient Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline utilizing BM25, BGE-M3, and Cross-Encoder reranking. Rather than deploying parameter-heavy reasoning models (e.g., DeepSeek R1) which consistently timed out, we utilized a 4-bit quantized LapaLLM 12B model via llama.cpp on dual NVIDIA T4 GPUs. Prioritizing pipeline stability over multi-step reasoning, our system achieved a Private Score of 0.8095, placing 10th out of 15 active teams.

</details>


### [67] [Type-Driven Tokenization for Brahmic Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22125)
*Sai Hemanth Kapila,Rakshika Bagavathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型的标准分词器在婆罗米系文字上会产生畸形文本，因其违反了该类文字特有的正字法约束。本文用 Agda 形式化证明英语正字法为半群而婆罗米正字法为部分半群，并将合法 token 建模为转移系统中的链，导出可证正确的 fixToken 函数，进而实现 SentencePiece 补丁与 Rust 预分词库，消除各印度文字的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流分词器（如 SentencePiece）面向英语等字母文字设计，其核心假设是任意合法 token 可自由拼接。婆罗米系文字（天城文、泰卢固文、泰米尔文、卡纳达文等）属元音附标文字，辅音携带可被依赖标记修改的固有元音，故存在字母文字中不会出现的拼写约束。直接套用现有分词器会破坏这些约束，产生畸形文本。作者旨在揭示这一理论根源并提供可验证的修复方案。

Method: 首先从代数结构上区分两类正字法：英语正字法构成半群（任意两个合法 token 可自由拼接），婆罗米正字法则构成部分半群（并非所有拼接都合法）。在 Agda 中对该区分进行形式化，将合法婆罗米 token 建模为转移系统中的链（chain），并据此推导出可证正确的 fixToken 函数，用于将任意候选 token 扩展至符合正字法边界。随后将形式化推导落地为 SentencePiece 的实用补丁，以及一个独立的基于 Rust 的预分词器库。

Result: 所提出的方法在多种印度文字上消除了先前观察到的分词错误；形式化推导被成功转化为 SentencePiece 补丁与 Rust 独立预分词库，验证了理论到工程实现的可行性。

Conclusion: 分词器在婆罗米系文字上的缺陷根源在于将正字法误设为半群，而实际上应为部分半群。通过 Agda 形式化、转移系统链模型与 fixToken 函数，可从理论上保证修复的正确性，并能有效落地为实际工具，为印度文字的多语言分词提供可靠基础。

Abstract: Standard tokenizers used in large language models produce malformed text when applied to Brahmic scripts. They are a family of abugidas, writing systems whose consonants carry an inherent vowel that dependent marks can modify. They include Devanagari, Telugu, Tamil, Kannada, and others. The underlying issue is that these tokenizers violate orthographic constraints that do not arise in alphabetic scripts like English. We observe that while English orthography forms a \emph{semigroup} (any two valid tokens can be freely concatenated), Brahmic orthography forms a \emph{partial semigroup}: not every concatenation yields a valid string. We formalise this distinction in Agda, model valid Brahmic tokens as chains in a transition system, and derive a provably correct \texttt{fixToken} function that extends any candidate token to respect orthographic boundaries. We then show how this formal derivation translates into a practical patch for SentencePiece as well as a standalone Rust-based pre-tokenizer library, eliminating the observed errors across Indic scripts.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Accuracy and Surface Fluency: Risk-Sensitive Evaluation of LLMs for Legal Clause Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22127)
*Devansh Singh,Sundaraparipurnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向LLM生成合同条款的实证评估研究设计与框架，用CLAUSE与LENS-CRAFT两套体系及"最大严重性原则"，对4个模型在22类条款、34种法律失效模式上进行风险敏感评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被广泛用于起草合同语言，但传统准确率或偏好类评测与法律起草任务严重错配：条款可能文风流畅却遗漏关键除外条款、以不可执行方式分配风险、假定不适用司法辖区或使当事人暴露于监管责任。因此需要转向条款特异、失效模式驱动、风险敏感的评估范式。

Method: 研究采用实证研究设计与框架：评测Claude Haiku 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite、GPT 5.4 Nano、Qwen 3.5 Flash四个模型，覆盖22个合同条款类别与34个具法律动机的失效模式；融合两套框架——CLAUSE（按法律功能与失效目标对提示分类）与LENS-CRAFT（沿九个法律质量维度对输出打分）；以"最大严重性原则"替代维度分数平均，使单一法律决定性缺陷保持可见。论文给出评估协议、分类体系、分析计划与结果报告结构。
