*Mingyang Yu,Xu Yang,Jun Zhang,Xiaolong Wang,Jing Xu,Keqian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PINNsForge：LLM驱动的进化式自动化PINN设计框架，将实际训练中的执行反馈回传LLM，形成"生成–执行–反馈–进化"闭环，在25个PDE基准中24个任务取得最低MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN需在网络表示、采样、损失构建和优化上协同选型，而有效配置在不同PDE间差异显著。现有自动化设计方法虽可搜索候选配置，但训练中揭示的信息主要用于评估而非改进后续设计，导致重复试错、训练预算利用低效。

Method: 提出PINNsForge：由PDE相关先验知识生成多样化候选配置；通过实际训练评估；将高性能设计与累积的执行证据回馈LLM；LLM依据观察到的优化行为，对耦合的PINN设计组件进行精炼、重组与探索，形成持续的生成–执行–反馈–进化循环。

Result: 在25个PDE基准上，与RoPINN、PINNsFormer、PINNsAgent相比，PINNsForge在24个任务上取得最低平均MSE。消融实验显示，移除PDE知识库、执行反馈和进化搜索会使平均MSE分别增至完整方法的3.74×、12.10×和10.10×。

Conclusion: 不同于一次性搜索或仅用于评估的反馈，PINNsForge将训练经验逐步转化为针对目标PDE的更优设计决策，验证了执行反馈与进化搜索在自动化PINN设计中的关键作用。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) require coordinated choices over network representation, sampling, loss construction, and optimization, while effective configurations often vary substantially across partial differential equations (PDEs). Existing automated PINN design methods can search candidate configurations, but information revealed during actual training is still used mainly for evaluation rather than to improve subsequent design, leading to repeated trial-and-error and inefficient use of training budget. We propose PINNsForge, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework for execution-feedback-based automated PINN design. PINNsForge generates diverse candidate configurations from PDE-related prior knowledge, evaluates them through actual training, and feeds high-performing designs together with accumulated execution evidence back to the LLM. Guided by observed optimization behavior, the LLM then refines, recombines, and explores coupled PINN design components, forming a continual cycle of generation, execution, feedback, and evolution. Unlike one-shot search or evaluation-only feedback, PINNsForge progressively converts training experience into improved design decisions for the target PDE. Across 25 PDE benchmarks, PINNsForge achieves the lowest mean MSE on 24 tasks compared with RoPINN, PINNsFormer, and PINNsAgent. Ablation studies further confirm the importance of the PDE knowledge base, execution feedback, and evolutionary search: removing these components increases the mean MSE to 3.74$\times$, 12.10$\times$, and 10.10$\times$ that of the full PINNsForge, respectively.

</details>


### [137] [Spatial-Interactor: Learning Spatial Reasoning through Interaction with the Observable Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23038)
*Kaixiang Yao,Xu Wang,Miao Pan,Hu Xiyue,Weishi Wang,Daniel Dahlmeier,Jintao Chen,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spatial-Interactor 框架，通过交互轨迹训练视觉语言模型（VLM）建模物理世界状态转移，并构建 LSI-108K 数据集与三级课程（L1 被动世界状态转移、L2 主动自状态转移、L3 长时程交互轨迹），结合 SFT 与 On-Policy Distillation（特权自蒸馏）两阶段训练，在多个 VLM 与空间基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理是 VLM 理解并作用于物理世界的关键能力，动态环境下需感知物体运动与视角变化引起的局部状态转移，并在长轨迹上积分以维持更新的空间状态。然而现有 VLM 在这两方面均受限：当前空间训练多聚焦于物体属性与空间关系的静态问答，对状态转移缺乏直接监督；而交互轨迹天然连接“前序观测—动作—后续观测”，可为局部状态转移提供直接监督，完整轨迹还能揭示连续转移间的依赖关系。

Method: 提出 Spatial-Interactor 框架，将学习过程组织为三级课程：L1 被动世界状态转移、L2 主动自状态转移、L3 长时程交互轨迹。基于仿真与真实交互轨迹构建 LSI-108K 数据集，任务与各级目标对齐。采用两阶段训练：先用 SFT 在 L1、L2 上建模局部转移；再用 On-Policy Distillation（OPD）进行特权自蒸馏，由给定片段级转移描述的教师分支监督学生的 on-policy CoT，使学生学会在 L3 长轨迹上积分连续转移。

Result: 在多个 VLM 与空间基准上的实验显示，局部状态转移建模与长时程积分能力均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 以交互轨迹为监督信号、按三级课程组织的训练框架，可有效增强 VLM 对动态环境的局部状态转移感知与长时程空间状态维持能力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is essential for vision-language models (VLMs) to understand and act in the physical world. Reasoning in dynamic environments requires VLMs to perceive local state transitions caused by object motion and viewpoint changes and integrate them over long trajectories to maintain an updated spatial state, yet existing VLMs remain limited in both capabilities. Current spatial training primarily focuses on static questions about object attributes and spatial relations, providing limited direct supervision for state transitions; in contrast, interaction trajectories naturally connect a preceding observation, an action, and a subsequent observation, offering direct supervision for local state transitions, while complete trajectories reveal dependencies among consecutive transitions. We therefore introduce Spatial-Interactor, a framework that trains VLMs to model physical-world state transitions through interaction, organizing this learning process into a three-level curriculum covering L1 passive world-state transitions, L2 active self-state transitions, and L3 long-horizon interaction trajectories. Accordingly, we construct the Learning from Spatial Interaction dataset (LSI-108K) from simulated and real interaction trajectories, with tasks aligned with the objective of each level. Our two-stage training strategy applies Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) to L1 and L2 for local transition modeling, and On-Policy Distillation (OPD) then uses privileged self-distillation: a teacher branch given segment-level transition descriptions supervises the student's on-policy CoT, helping the student learn to integrate consecutive transitions over L3 long trajectories. Experiments across multiple VLMs and spatial benchmarks show consistent gains in local transition modeling and long-horizon integration.

</details>


### [138] [Enforcing Narrative Reliability and Epistemic Pacing in LLM-Driven Detective Games via Structured Knowledge Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23043)
*Parsa Rahmati,Richard Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构化知识树架构与三智能体LLM流水线，用于侦探游戏审讯对话控制，在保证信息按叙事状态逐步披露的同时大幅降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽支持交互游戏中的开放式对话，但其输出具有非确定性，难以维持作者控制权、事实一致性与预设的信息披露顺序。在侦探游戏中该问题尤为突出：过早泄密或虚构细节会破坏玩家推理进程的逻辑。

Method: 设计结构化知识树架构，并配合三智能体LLM流水线：将知识检索、对话生成与回复验证解耦，确保虚拟嫌疑人只披露当前叙事状态所允许的信息。以可玩侦探游戏测试平台《The Interrogation of Adrian Gale》为载体验证。

Result: 通过正式用户研究评估幻觉削减、对作者设定披露顺序的遵循度以及玩家感知的逻辑推进感。结果显示：结构化架构将关键幻觉减少64.78%，并完全杜绝了叙事信息的过早披露。

Conclusion: 严格的机械约束在强制对话揭示方面带来可用性权衡，但系统成功实现了严谨的认知节奏控制，并使玩家对案件侦破进程产生清晰的主观推进感。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) enable open-ended dialogue in interactive games, but their non-deterministic outputs make it difficult to preserve authorial control, factual consistency, and the intended sequence of information disclosure. These challenges are particularly significant in detective games, where premature revelation or fabricated details can undermine the logic of player progression. We present a Structured Knowledge Tree architecture coupled with a tri-agent LLM pipeline for controlling dialogue in an open-ended interrogation game. The system separates knowledge retrieval, dialogue generation, and response verification to ensure that the virtual suspect reveals only information permitted by the current narrative state. We evaluate the approach through The Interrogation of Adrian Gale, a playable detective-game testbed, and a formal user study examining hallucination reduction, adherence to authored disclosure sequences, and perceived logical progression. Our results demonstrate that the structured architecture reduces critical hallucinations by 64.78% and entirely prevents premature narrative disclosure. While the strict mechanical constraints introduced usability trade-offs regarding forced conversational reveals, the system successfully enforces rigorous epistemic pacing and provides players with a clear, subjective sense of progression toward solving the case.

</details>


### [139] [LazyAgent: Demand-Driven Materialization and Physical Optimization of Agentic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23058)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LazyAgent 提出以目标派生的“被需求集合”为核心，用线性时间反向闭包分析替代逐步局部判断，仅在活动目标需要时物化就绪节点，从而在编排型程序上跳过无关工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 运行时先规划后执行，节点一旦就绪即执行，导致程序描述范围超过当前请求时产生大量无关计算。论文希望让执行保持请求特定，避免先启动无关工作，并指出现有公开 benchmark 是 eager 形状、几乎不含未被请求的工作，难以暴露该问题。

Method: LazyAgent 维护一个由目标派生的活“被需求集合”，随执行状态变化从被请求输出刷新反向闭包，仅当活动目标需要时才物化就绪节点；用一次线性时间图分析加常数时间成员测试替代重复局部判断。并证明在请求覆盖整图时与 eager 精确等价。

Result: 在描述超出当前请求所需程序上，LazyAgent 持续优于最强的目标停止 eager 基线：增加一个无关产品使 eager 成本上升 22.5%，LazyAgent 为 0.0%；生产科学工作流节省 42.0% CPU，现场发布门禁（跨四个仓库）节省 51.7% 容器时间；两个第三方测试套件上目标相对输出投影最多节省约 90% 共享步骤，而相同 eager 对照为 0.0%。

Conclusion: LazyAgent 通过“许可”机制（目标相对输出投影）在请求特定执行下避免无关工作，其优势在其他消费者需要该输出或请求本身需要它时消失；排序、复用与剪枝也能省成本但无法替代许可。现有公开基准几乎不含未请求工作，预注册的规划干预未能拓宽它们，因此需要基于常驻程序与序列构建新基准。

Abstract: Current agent runtimes that plan before acting generally execute a step once it becomes ready. We present LazyAgent, a unified execution framework for agent-authored programs organized around a live, goal-derived demanded set. LazyAgent refreshes a backward closure from requested outputs as execution state changes and materializes a ready node only when the active goal requires it. This replaces repeated local judgments with one linear-time graph analysis followed by constant-time membership tests, allowing programs to remain broad while execution stays request-specific. On programs that describe more than the current request needs, LazyAgent consistently outperforms the strongest goal-stopping eager baseline by refusing unrelated work before it starts. Adding one unrelated product raises the eager bill by 22.5% and LazyAgent's by 0.0%. LazyAgent saves 42.0% of measured CPU on production scientific workflows and 51.7% of container time on a live release gate spanning four repositories. We also prove and verify exact equivalence when the request reaches the whole graph, leaving no unrelated work to avoid. Beyond permission, goal-relative output projection saves up to approximately 90% of a shared step on two third-party test suites while the identical eager control saves 0.0%; the advantage disappears when the omitted output has no other consumer or the request needs it. Ordering, reuse, and pruning can also save cost, but do not replace permission. Finally, we show that current public benchmarks are eager-shaped and contain almost no unrequested work. A pre-registered planning intervention did not broaden them. These findings motivate benchmarks built from standing programs and sequences.

</details>


### [140] [FireWorldBench: Benchmarking Complex Physical World Intelligence through Coupled-Field Fire Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23064)
*Qiang Chen,Hao Guo,Huatai Zhu,Tairan Huang,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Keke Huang,Haifeng Li,Yi Chen,Xiu Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FireWorldBench，用于评估多模态大模型与智能体在耦合场火灾动力学中的复杂物理世界智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实物理系统涉及多连续场、潜在因果机制、部分观测与干预敏感动态，现有基准多聚焦物体识别、场景描述和短期预测，难以全面评估复杂物理世界智能。

Method: 以火灾动力学作为耦合场压力测试环境，沿物理能力轴与火灾场景任务轴构建基准，覆盖物理状态理解、时序动力学、因果机制与干预推理；包含520个火灾世界条目（494受控仿真世界、26真实事件组），跨7类环境、47种场景原型，融合结构化文本观测、2D物理场可视化与3D事件级建模，形成9,074个文本-图像交错问答对，含选择题与开放式报告生成。

Result: 构建了大规模、多模态、多任务物理智能评测基准，支持评估模型从部分观测中推断潜在物理状态、解释机制、预测耦合场演化并评估干预后果。

Conclusion: FireWorldBench为复杂物理世界智能提供了具有挑战性的测试平台，推动多模态大模型与智能体从感知描述走向因果与干预理解的物理推理评估。

Abstract: Understanding the physical world requires more than object recognition, scene description, and short-term visual prediction, as real-world physical systems involve multiple continuous fields, latent causal mechanisms, partial observations, and intervention-sensitive dynamics. We propose FireWorldBench, a benchmark for evaluating complex physical world intelligence in multimodal large language models and agents through coupled-field fire dynamics. Fire provides a canonical stress-test environment, where multiple interacting physical fields jointly shape observable states and temporal dynamics. FireWorldBench is organized along two complementary axes, a physical capability axis and a fire scenario task axis, jointly covering physical-state understanding, temporal dynamics, causal mechanisms, and intervention reasoning. The benchmark comprises 520 fire-world entries, including 494 controlled simulation worlds and 26 real-world-aligned event groups, spanning 47 scene archetypes across 7 environment families. These entries combine structured textual observations, multiple 2D physical-field visualizations, and 3D event-level scene modeling, yielding 9,074 text-image interleaved question-answer pairs across choice-based and open-ended report-generation formats. FireWorldBench evaluates whether models can infer latent physical states, explain underlying mechanisms, forecast coupled-field evolution, and assess intervention consequences from multimodal partial observations, providing a challenging testbed for complex physical world intelligence.

</details>


### [141] [Tutoring Large Language Models to be Domain-adaptive, Precise and Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23071)
*Somnath Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"负责任智能"框架，从领域适配、实时伦理对齐、文化多语言安全三方面推进AI的安全、伦理与文化敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在安全、伦理与文化敏感性方面面临严峻挑战，亟需一个系统化框架来构建兼顾领域知识、伦理规范与文化适应性的下一代AI。

Method: （1）利用主动学习与基于图的知识改进专业领域的领域适配以降低幻觉；（2）提出一种解码时对齐机制，实时主动阻断有害文本生成；（3）通过语言特定的引导（steering）尊重多样的语言与社会规范，保障文化及多语言安全。

Result: 论文宣称在三个核心方向取得进展：提升专业领域适配并减少幻觉、增强伦理约束（实时阻断有害生成）、以及实现尊重语言与社会规范的文化与多语言安全。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建语境知识丰富、伦理可靠、文化可适配的下一代AI提供了蓝图。

Abstract: This thesis proposes a framework for "responsible intelligence" to address AI's critical challenges in safety, ethics, and cultural sensitivity. It advances three core areas: First, it improves domain adaptation in specialized fields using active learning and graph-based knowledge to reduce hallucinations. Second, it enhances ethical rigor via a novel decoding-time alignment mechanism that proactively blocks harmful text generation in real-time. Finally, it ensures cultural and multilingual safety through language-specific steering that respects diverse linguistic and social norms. Ultimately, this work provides a blueprint for building next-generation AI that is contextually knowledgeable, ethically sound, and culturally adaptable.

</details>


### [142] [Event Signature Transfer: Model-Agnostic Forecast Scenario Construction from Historical Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23074)
*Karthik Sridhar,Aaditya Jain,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练、模型无关的事件签名迁移算子EST，将历史事件转化为显式预测情景以改进分位数预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测者常能预知事件即将发生，却难以判断其影响形态、规模与时点，需要一种无需重新训练即可注入情景信息的方法。

Method: EST先剔除源事件自身的趋势与季节成分，再将剩余事件签名按比例缩放与时点重排后叠加到原预测上，保持原预测的关联结构，且强度为零时严格退化为原预测；仅读取输出分位数，因此适用于任意分位数预测器，迁移阶段无需训练与模型内部信息。

Result: 在Chronos-2、TimesFM-2.5与Toto-2.0上，覆盖12个真实事件与10个合成情景，人工配置的EST使真实事件样本内WQL降低21.7-90%；在Chronos-2上，12组匹配对比中11组优于协变量条件化、激活编辑与原始回放。

Conclusion: EST能够构建情景，但不估计其发生似然，定位为情景构造算子而非概率推断方法。

Abstract: Forecasters often know an event is imminent but not the shape, size, or timing of its effect. We introduce Event Signature Transfer (EST), a training-free, model-agnostic operator that turns a completed past event into an explicit forecast scenario. EST removes a source event's own trend and seasonality, then scales and retimes the remaining event signature onto a native forecast, preserving the forecast's linked structure and reducing to it exactly at zero strength. Because it reads only output quantiles, EST applies to any quantile forecaster, with no training, no model internals, at transfer time. Across twelve real episodes and ten synthetic scenarios on Chronos-2, TimesFM-2.5 and Toto-2.0, manually configured EST reduces real-episode WQL by 21.7-90\% in-sample. On Chronos-2, it leads eleven of twelve matched comparisons against covariate conditioning, activation editing and raw replay. The operator builds a scenario; it does not estimate its likelihood.

</details>


### [143] [From Inference Engine to Inference Control Plane: Connecting vLLM, llm-d, and the Evolution of Efficient Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23130)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为一篇综述性论文，梳理大语言模型（LLM）推理从"引擎内局部优化"向"分布式控制问题"的范式转变；以 vLLM 与 llm-d 作为互补层次，提出"推理执行规划器"及配套的基准图谱、瓶颈迁移分类学、部署指南与基于 SLO-goodput 的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理正在从单一引擎的局部优化（如算子、显存、批处理）演变为跨集群的分布式控制问题，涉及可复用状态、阶段放置、异构加速器、网络、自动扩缩容、可靠性与服务等级目标（SLO）。然而相关证据分散在同行评审的系统研究、开源实现与生产案例中，缺乏跨来源的整合视角，也缺少对"稀缺资源"迁移这一趋势的系统性刻画。

Method: 采用综合（synthesis）而非新基准的方法：跨同行评审系统研究、开源实现与已记录的生产研究进行证据整合；将推理栈抽象为互补两层的分析框架——模型服务引擎层（PagedAttention、连续批处理、定制内核、量化、并行策略）与推理控制平面层（决定执行的位置、时机与策略）；在此基础上提出"推理执行规划器"（Inference Execution Planner），其决策空间涵盖聚合式与分离式拓扑、KV 来源与传输动作、硬件型号、路由/准入策略以及较慢时间尺度的扩缩容决策。

Result: 论文不产生新的性能数据，所有性能与部署结果均归属原始来源。综合证据表明：现代推理的稀缺资源正从单纯原始 FLOPs，转向受管状态、放置决策、网络搬运、可靠性与决策质量。论文同时给出：按来源组织的基准图谱（source-local benchmark atlas）、瓶颈迁移分类学（bottleneck-migration taxonomy）、实践部署指南、基于 SLO-goodput 的评估框架，以及面向 agentic、多模态、异构与高韧性的推理研究问题。

Conclusion: LLM 推理应被重新定义为分布式控制问题，引擎优化与控制平面优化是互补而非替代关系；以"可行执行计划"（而非仅端点选择）为决策粒度的推理执行规划器，是应对瓶颈迁移与状态/放置/网络/可靠性约束的有效抽象，并为后续研究提供了评估与方向上的统一框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is evolving from an engine-local optimization problem into a distributed control problem involving reusable state, phase placement, heterogeneous accelerators, networking, autoscaling, reliability, and service-level objectives. This paper connects that transition across peer-reviewed systems research, open-source implementations, and documented production studies. It treats vLLM and llm-d as complementary layers: model-serving engines optimize execution through mechanisms such as PagedAttention, continuous batching, kernels, quantization, and parallelism, while an inference control plane can optimize where, when, and under what policy execution occurs across a fleet. The contribution is synthesis rather than a new benchmark; all reported performance and deployment results remain attributed to their original sources. The combined evidence suggests that the scarce resource in modern inference is shifting from raw FLOPs alone toward managed state, placement, network movement, reliability, and decision quality. We propose an Inference Execution Planner that selects feasible execution plans rather than only endpoints, including aggregated versus disaggregated topology, KV source and transfer action, hardware variant, routing/admission policy, and slower scaling decisions. We also provide a source-local benchmark atlas, a bottleneck-migration taxonomy, practical deployment guidance, an evaluation framework based on SLO-goodput, and research questions for agentic, multimodal, heterogeneous, and resilient inference.

</details>


### [144] [CraftBench-UE: Deterministic Evaluation for Coding Agents in Unreal Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23142)
*Shutong Wu,Kevin Calderone,Andy Tsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CraftBenchUE：一个在隔离 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体、在全新工程中重建其提交物并进行确定性构建、资产与运行时检查（不依赖 LLM 评判）的评测框架。基于此构建了涵盖 C++ 源码、Blueprint 资产与编辑器脚本的 70 项任务基准，评测 7 个模型在两种编辑器工具配置下的表现。核心发现：在 10 对“相同玩法、相同运行时测试、仅交付物不同”的任务中，C++ 完成率比 Blueprint 分别高出 30.0 与 42.9 个百分点；且在通过资产检查的按时 Blueprint 提交中，有 42.2% 与 50.0% 未通过运行时断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在游戏引擎中实现玩法功能不仅是写代码：代码能编译运行并不代表正确实现了所要求的玩法。现有评测缺乏针对游戏引擎环境的、可复现且不依赖主观 LLM 评判的自动化验证手段，因此需要一套能真实重建提交物并执行确定性检查的评测体系，以衡量智能体在引擎内完成玩法任务的能力。

Method: 构建 CraftBenchUE 评测框架：在隔离的 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体，将其保存的提交物在全新工程中重建，并施加确定性的构建、资产与运行时检查，全程不使用 LLM 评判。基于该框架设计 70 项任务，覆盖 C++ 源码、Blueprint 资产与编辑器脚本三类交付物。对 7 个模型在两种编辑器工具配置下进行评测，并在 C++ 任务上设置文件与 Shell 基线；同时构造 10 对配对任务——指定相同玩法、使用相同运行时测试，但分别要求以 C++ 和 Blueprint 交付。

Result: 在 10 对配对任务中，C++ 的完成率在两种工具配置下分别比 Blueprint 高出 30.0 和 42.9 个百分点。在该配对集中，按时提交且通过资产检查的 Blueprint 提交里，分别有 42.2% 与 50.0% 未通过显式运行时断言——即满足资产层面要求却未通过所要求的玩法测试。

Conclusion: Blueprint 交付存在显著的“形式合规但功能失效”问题：静态资产检查通过并不等价于玩法正确。评测表明发动机内玩法能力需以运行时验证为准，C++ 与 Blueprint 之间存在明显的完成率差距。作者将随报告发布评测框架、任务基准及轨迹发现。

Abstract: Building gameplay features in a game engine requires more than code, as code that compiles and runs does not necessarily implement the requested gameplay. We introduce CraftBenchUE, an evaluation harness that runs agents in an isolated Unreal Engine environment, reconstructs their saved submissions in fresh projects, and applies deterministic build, asset, and runtime checks without an LLM judge. Based on the harness, we built a benchmark consisting of 70 tasks spanning C++ source, Blueprint assets, and editor scripting. We evaluate seven models under two editor-tool configurations, with a file-and-shell baseline on C++ tasks. We further pair tasks that specify the same gameplay and use the same runtime tests, but require C++ and Blueprint as the deliverables. Across the 10 paired tasks, C++ completion rates exceed Blueprint by 30.0 and 42.9 percentage points in the two tool configurations. Among on-time Blueprint submissions in this paired set that pass asset checks, 42.2% and 50.0% fail explicit runtime assertions. These submissions satisfy asset requirements but fail the required gameplay tests. We will release the harness, task benchmark, and our trajectory findings with the report.

</details>


### [145] [Do Not Trust the Benchmark: Limitations of General LLM Rankings and a Case for Task-Specific Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23201)
*Danial Amin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批评通用LLM排行榜的五大局限，主张评估应披露配置、验证题目与任务完成、报告成本与耗时并明确泛化范围，并以众包平台Isotanta为例讨论共享排名与任务特定评估的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数日益影响大语言模型的开发、营销与选型，但总分数只有在关联被测系统、所含题目与评估条件时才具可解释性；通用排行榜的排名容易被误用为选型依据。

Method: 观点性（perspective）分析，梳理通用LLM排名的五项相互关联的局限，并结合已记录的案例说明这些问题需要不同对策；随后以众包基准平台Isotanta作为贡献题目与重复评估的实践示例进行讨论。

Result: 识别出五类局限：被测系统与公开可用系统的差异；外部评估中的商业激励与依赖；基准饱和、有缺陷的测试与数据污染；模型利用评分程序；通用分数与用户任务相关性有限。提出改进的评估程序要求，并指出更大的题池可提升任务覆盖、重复采样可提升估计稳定性，但均不保证效度或个性化。

Conclusion: 论文区分了平台的当前共享排名与所提出的任务特定、用户自供评估；核心论点是模型选择需要关于预期工作任务表现的证据，而非通用排行榜上的高位排名。

Abstract: Benchmark scores increasingly influence the development, marketing, and selection of large language models (LLMs). Yet an overall score is interpretable only in relation to the system tested, the questions included, and the conditions of evaluation. This perspective examines five connected limitations of general LLM rankings: differences between evaluated and publicly available systems; commercial incentives and dependencies in external evaluation; benchmark saturation, defective tests, and data contamination; models exploiting scoring procedures; and the limited relevance of general scores to users' tasks. Documented cases illustrate why these problems require different responses. I argue for evaluation procedures that disclose the tested configuration, validate questions and successful task completion, report performance alongside cost and execution time, and make the scope of generalization explicit. I then discuss \textbf{Isotanta}, a crowdsourced benchmarking platform, as a practical example of contributed questions and repeated evaluation. A larger question pool may improve task coverage, while repeated sampling can improve the stability of estimates on that pool; neither guarantees validity or personalization. The paper distinguishes the platform's current shared ranking from proposed task-specific and user-provided evaluations. Its central argument is that model selection requires evidence about performance on the intended work, not simply a high position on a general leaderboard.

</details>


### [146] [Expansion Counts under Standard A* Tie-Breaking Strategies on the Final Plateau](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23293)
*Alex Fukunaga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了A*算法中9种同$f$值节点的破平策略，证明在一致启发式下任意两策略间存在正代价实例使扩展差距任意大；并给出单位代价情形下低$h$/高$h$的互补极值结果及加权评估函数$f_\alpha=g+\alpha h$（$0\le\alpha<1$）消除破平敏感性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A*中具有相同$f$值的节点的扩展顺序由破平策略决定，进而影响最终$f$层上被扩展的状态集合。然而不同破平策略的性能差异是否普遍存在、差距能否任意大、以及能否通过启发式权重消除这种敏感性，此前缺乏系统刻画，因此本文开展该问题的理论研究。

Method: 以9种标准破平策略为对象，在一致启发式假设下构造正代价实例并进行成对比较分析；进一步设计参数化的单位代价网格实例，比较low-$h$＋FIFO与LIFO的扩展数比值；随后在单位代价搜索且非目标节点$h>0$、启发式值在目标附近精确的设定下，分析low-$h$与high-$h$的扩展行为；最后引入$f_\alpha=g+\alpha h$评估函数，考察权重$\alpha$对破平敏感性的影响。

Result: 在一致启发式下，任意两种破平策略之间均存在正代价实例，使各自优于对方且加性扩展差距可任意大；参数化单位代价网格实例显示low-$h$＋FIFO与LIFO的扩展数比值无界。单位代价搜索中（非目标$h>0$、目标附近启发式精确）得到互补极值结果：low-$h$在完美区域内从同一配置出发最小化剩余扩展数，而high-$h$在每个$h=1$的最终平台状态均为目标前驱时最大化总扩展数。对于$f_\alpha=g+\alpha h$，当非目标$h>0$时，任意启发式权重$0\le\alpha<1$均消除破平敏感性，所有破平策略扩展相同的状态集合。

Conclusion: 破平策略在A*中可导致任意大的扩展差异，故其选择具有实质性影响；但在单位代价、非目标$h>0$且目标附近启发式精确的条件下，扩展数极值由low-$h$/high-$h$呈现互补特征；引入权重$\alpha<1$的评估函数$f_\alpha=g+\alpha h$可完全消除破平敏感性，为实践中规避破平依赖提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In the A* search algorithm, the tie-breaking strategies for nodes with the same $f$-value determines which states A* expands on the final $f$-layer. For nine standard tie-breaking strategies, we show that under a consistent heuristic, every pair has positive-cost instances favoring each strategy over the other by an arbitrarily large additive expansion gap. A parameterized unit-cost grid example also gives unbounded expansion-count ratios between low-$h$ with FIFO and LIFO. In unit-cost search with $h > 0$ at non-goals, exact heuristic values near the goal lead to complementary extremal results: low-$h$ minimizes the number of remaining expansions from a common configuration within the perfect region, while high-$h$ maximizes the total number of expansions when every final-plateau state with $h=1$ is a goal predecessor. Finally, with the evaluation function $f_α = g + αh$, when $h>0$ at non-goals, every heuristic weight $0 \leq α<1$ eliminates tie-breaking sensitivity, and all tie-breaking strategies expand the same set of states.

</details>


### [147] [TicTacBench: Benchmarking Timing Closure Capabilities of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23363)
*Bowei Wang,Zhigang Fang,Zhijie Yang,Renzhi Chen,Shanshan Li,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TicTacBench 基准评估编码智能体在 RTL 时序收敛（post-PnR）能力，并提出 TicTacSkill 方法提升收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RTL 基准主要评估功能正确性与 PPA，忽略了智能体在时序收敛方面的能力，导致该关键工程能力缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建 TicTacBench：含 30 个多样化任务，每个任务提供次优 RTL 设计、真实时序约束、功能等价性验证与时序报告，在布局布线后（post-PnR）评估；使用 8 个前沿 LLM 驱动编码智能体进行 300+ 次运行；并分析失败类别，据此提出 TicTacSkill，引导智能体遵循标准时序收敛流程。

Result: 最佳智能体仅能收敛 53.3% 的任务，平均 ADP 恶化 7.18%、EDDP 改善 8.83%；TicTacSkill 将时序收敛率提升 9%。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在 RTL 设计上进步显著，但时序收敛能力仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led to the emergence of coding agents capable of performing complex engineering tasks, including register-transfer level (RTL) design and optimization. Existing RTL benchmarks mainly evaluate functional correctness and performance, power, and area (PPA) of the generated RTL designs, leaving agents' ability for \emph{timing closure} under-evaluated. We propose TicTacBench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate coding agents' capabilities for RTL-level timing closure under post-place-and-route (post-PnR) evaluation. TicTacBench contains 30 diverse tasks, each provided with a suboptimal RTL design, realistic timing constraints, functional equivalence verification, and timing reports. With over 300 runs of coding agents driven by 8 frontier LLMs, we find that even the best agent can only close 53.3\% of tasks with 7.18\% area-delay product (ADP) degradation and 8.83\% energy-delay-squared product (EDDP) improvement on average. We identify common failure categories that explain why agents fail to close timing. Then we propose TicTacSkill, a new method that guides agents to follow standard timing-closure procedures and improves the Timing Closure Rate by 9\%. These results suggest that while coding agents have made significant progress in RTL design, their timing-closure capability still has substantial room for improvement.

</details>


### [148] [Leaky-integrator reconstruction: taming error accumulation in recursive differenced time-series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23378)
*Zijiang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 leaky-integrator reconstruction：一种免训练方法，用于消除递归差分预测的误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归差分预测（先预测一步差分再累加积分）是应对非平稳性的常用手段，但其在非线性模型递归多步 rollout 中会发散，336 步误差可达正常预测器的数倍。

Method: 诊断：将累加求和视为单位圆上带极点的离散积分器，解释递归 rollout 发散原因。修复：用泄漏积分器 H(z)=1/(1-γz^{-1})（γ<1）将极点移入单位圆内，理论上可界定累积误差方差；重建时使用单一固定 γ=0.9，无需重训，仅需两行改动。

Result: 在所有测试的神经架构上均收缩各预测步长误差；七个发散架构、二十个数据集上平均增益从 H=24 的 ~3% 增至 H=96 的 23%、H=192 的 37%、H=336 的 51%（跨架构 43-74%；使用 oracle pole 达 78%）。

Conclusion: 在不存在病态的情形（稳定或联合预测器已达不可约速率）下可证明惰性，因此是安全、通用的默认方案。

Abstract: We introduce leaky-integrator reconstruction, a training-free method that cures the error accumulation of recursive differenced forecasting. Our first contribution is diagnostic: predicting one-step changes and integrating them by cumulative summation, the standard remedy for non-stationarity, is a discrete integrator with a pole on the unit circle, and we show this makes recursive rollout of a nonlinear model diverge, its 336-step error reaching several times that of a well-behaved forecaster (normalised MAE 1.6-3.8 versus about 0.8) across every neural architecture tested. Our second, central contribution is the fix: move the pole inside the unit circle with a leaky integrator H(z) = 1/(1 - gamma z^-1), gamma < 1, which provably bounds the accumulated error variance. Applied at reconstruction time with a single fixed gamma=0.9 (no retraining, a two-line change to any deployed one-step or foundation-model forecaster), it shrinks error at every horizon, the mean gain over seven diverging architectures and twenty datasets growing from ~3% at H=24 to 23% at H=96, 37% at H=192 and 51% (43-74% across those architectures) at H=336 (78% with an oracle pole). Crucially, it is provably inert where no pathology exists (stable or joint predictors already at the irreducible rate), making it a safe, general default.

</details>


### [149] [AgentBetta: Verification-Driven Adaptive Configuration of an AI Nano-Agent through Selective Expansion and Verified Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23512)
*Md. Ashraful Babu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估了 AgentBetta——一个自适应 AI Nano-Agent 框架，将模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆与算力资源等要素表示为可执行配置，并通过验证驱动的诊断、选择性扩展与反事实收缩进行动态更新，在验证成功率、资源配置效率与配置缺陷识别上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体通常以预定义配置部署，但不同任务对模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆和计算资源的需求差异很大；固定的"全量供给"配置容易造成能力与资源浪费，因此需要一种能够按任务自适应调配能力的机制。

Method: AgentBetta 将上述因素建模为可执行配置，通过三个环节更新配置：（1）验证驱动的诊断（configuration-deficiency diagnosis），识别配置缺口；（2）选择性扩展（selective expansion），仅调整相关维度；（3）基于验证的反事实收缩（counterfactual contraction），在成功后移除冗余能力。评估区分受控机制验证与外部智能体对比，并在 AB-ConfigBench 基准上测试。

Result: 在 AB-ConfigBench 上，AgentBetta 达到 91.38% 的验证成功率，相比全量配置将上下文中位数从 64,000 字符降至 8,000 字符，工具暴露中位数从 5 个降至 0 个；配置缺陷诊断的 macro-F1 为 0.819，各评估维度精确率为 1.000，选择性扩展避免了对无关维度的改动；成功后收缩在 56.41% 的单维收缩探针中保持了验证结果，说明部分成功配置仍含可移除能力。外部评估显示自适应配置可改善验证任务完成与能力暴露之间的平衡，但结果随基准与智能体族变化；跨族复现未重现主骨干的准确率排序，专用系统在部分任务域仍具优势。

Conclusion: 应将 AgentBetta 理解为一种调节能力分配与推理开销的"配置自适应机制"，而非专用智能体架构的通用替代方案；其价值在于按任务动态平衡能力供给与资源消耗，且效果依赖具体基准与智能体族。

Abstract: Large language model agents are typically deployed with predefined configurations, although the required model capability, context, tools, permissions, memory, and computational resources can vary substantially across tasks. This study develops and evaluates AgentBetta, an adaptive AI Nano-Agent framework that represents these factors as an executable configuration and updates them through verification-driven diagnosis, selective expansion, and verification-based counterfactual contraction. The evaluation distinguishes controlled mechanism validation from external agent comparisons. On the AB-ConfigBench benchmark, AgentBetta achieved 91.38% verified success while reducing median context allocation from 64,000 to 8,000 context characters and median tool exposure from five tools to zero compared with the fully provisioned configuration. The configuration-deficiency diagnosis achieved a macro-F1 score of 0.819 with precision of 1.000 across the evaluated dimensions, and selective expansion avoided unnecessary changes to unrelated configuration dimensions. Post-success contraction preserved verification outcomes in 56.41% of evaluated one-dimension contraction probes, indicating that some successful configurations contained removable capability under the tested conditions. External evaluations indicate that adaptive configuration can improve the balance between verified task completion and capability exposure; however, the results vary across benchmarks and agent families. In particular, the cross-family replication did not reproduce the primary-backbone accuracy ordering, and specialized systems remained advantageous for certain task domains. These results support interpreting AgentBetta as a configuration-adaptation mechanism that regulates capability allocation and inference expenditure rather than as a universal replacement for specialized agent architectures.

</details>


### [150] [Are Human-Aligned Models Models of Humans? A Turing-Test Gap in Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23640)
*Suqin Yuan,Runqi Lin,Muyang Li,Guanzhe Hong,Jindong Gu,Lei Feng,Chris Russell,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分"对齐人类偏好"与"对齐人类行为"，指出偏好对齐即使在偏好与回复均来自人类的情况下也会降低模型的类人性，并将此现象命名为"图灵测试差距"（Turing-test gap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍将基于人类反馈的对齐等同于"与人类对齐"，但人们偏好的回复未必是他们自己会给出的回复。论文旨在揭示偏好对齐与行为对齐之间的区分，纠正"偏好对齐自然带来类人性"这一隐含假设。

Method: 从理论与实证两方面入手：先推导偏好对齐保持人类回复分布所需的限制性条件，再检验真实人类偏好是否满足该条件，并考察在不同强度与方向的偏好加权下人类回复似然的变化，同时验证标准DPO下的表现。

Result: 偏好对齐仅在某个严格条件下才能保持人类回复分布，而实证上未发现一致证据表明真实人类偏好满足该条件；人类回复似然的损失随偏好加权强度增加而上升，且与加权方向无关；该差距在标准DPO下同样出现。

Conclusion: 类人性应被视为对齐的一个显式维度，而不能假定其会随偏好对齐自动获得。

Abstract: Human-feedback alignment has made language models useful assistants and is commonly described as aligning them with humans. However, the responses people prefer from an AI need not be the responses they themselves would give. We distinguish alignment with human preferences from alignment with human behavior, and show that alignment with human preferences can make model behavior less human-like even when both preferences and responses come entirely from humans. We call this the Turing-test gap. We show that preference alignment preserves the human response distribution only under a restrictive condition, and find no consistent evidence that real human preferences satisfy it. Empirically, the loss of human-response likelihood increases with the strength of preference weighting, regardless of its direction, and the gap also appears under standard DPO. These results establish human-likeness as an explicit dimension of alignment rather than something assumed to follow from preference alignment.

</details>


### [151] [PhysAI-Bench: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Agentic Decision-Making in Autonomous UAV-Centric Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23695)
*Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Merouane Debbah,Abderrahmane Lakas,Manu Perumkunnil,Norbert Tihanyi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhysAI-Bench，一个面向 Physical AI 自主系统（如 UAV）智能体决策能力的基准，包含 10,178 个从无人机任务对话轨迹中自动提取的标准化决策实例，并基于两阶段协议评测了 29 个基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI 与基础模型在 UAV 等自主系统中的应用快速发展，但现有基准多聚焦物理感知、直觉物理、具身导航与协作推理，缺乏对可靠自主性所需的"智能体决策"能力的评估。

Method: 构建 PhysAI-Bench：从自主 UAV 任务对话轨迹中自动抽取 10,178 个标准化决策实例，每个实例保留任务上下文、时序依赖、物理约束、MCP 工具调用、A2A 交互、传感器观测以及 AI 原生 6G 网络条件（时延、丢包、吞吐、边缘负载、网络切片）。仅暴露决策前的信息以避免未来事件泄漏，逼近在线决策。评测 29 个基础模型，采用两阶段协议：先在人验的 35 实例开发集上，从零样本/三样本/五样本三种提示与四种温度的 12 种组合中筛选模型特定配置（各跑 3 次）；随后冻结配置，在 500 个固定且情节不相交的实例集上各跑 3 次。

Result: GPT-5.3 取得最高准确率 52.00%，其次是 GPT-5.2（49.40%）与 Grok 4.5（49.07%）。Few-shot 提示总体提升性能，而温度影响有限。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Physical AI 中可靠的智能体决策仍是未解难题。数据集已开源：https://github.com/maferrag/physai-bench。

Abstract: Recent advances in Physical AI have accelerated the use of foundation models in autonomous systems such as unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), which must perceive, reason, plan, and act in dynamic environments. Existing benchmarks assess physical perception, intuitive physics, embodied navigation, and collaborative reasoning, but rarely evaluate the agentic decision-making required for reliable autonomy. We introduce \textit{PhysAI-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating this capability. It contains 10,178 standardized decision instances automatically extracted from conversational traces of autonomous UAV missions. Each instance preserves mission context, temporal dependencies, physical constraints, Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls, Agent-to-Agent (A2A) interactions, sensor observations, and AI-native 6G network conditions, including latency, packet loss, throughput, edge load, and network slicing. We expose only information preceding each decision, preventing future-event leakage and approximating online decision-making. We evaluate 29 foundation models using a two-stage protocol. We select model-specific configurations from 12 combinations of zero-, three-, and five-shot prompting and four temperatures, tested in three runs on a 35-instance, human-verified development set. We then freeze each selected configuration and evaluate it in three runs on a fixed, episode-disjoint set of 500 instances. GPT-5.3 achieves the highest accuracy (52.00%), followed by GPT-5.2 (49.40%) and Grok~4.5 (49.07%). Few-shot prompting generally improves performance, while temperature has limited influence. The results demonstrate that reliable agentic decision-making in Physical AI remains an open challenge. The dataset is available at https://github.com/maferrag/physai-bench

</details>


### [152] [ScholarStack: Layered Research Asset Orchestration and Cross-Task Reuse for Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23735)
*ScholarSeed AI Team,Ao Zhang,Caoqinwei Gong,Guanglei Wang,Haifan Zhang,Hanwei Zhang,Jiayi Sheng,Jihai Zhang,Kai Ying,Liyun Dai,Tingyu Zhu,Wei Chen,Wei Luo,Wenkai Fang,Xiaoyu Qiu,Xue Jiang,Yi Wang,Yuan Cao,Zheng Yu,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScholarStack——一种分层研究资产框架，将论文集合编译为可复用、带版本、保留溯源的三级资产（论文级陈述、领域级组织、跨论文证据综合），并通过统一接口按任务所需证据粒度返回视图；在四类任务族、十个任务设置上验证，跨论文证据任务质量提升显著，且所有测量任务的查询时 token 成本均下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体多围绕单一任务组织，导致同一批论文被反复检索、切分与解读，前一任务积累的理解难以复用于后续任务，形成孤立的文档处理流程，缺乏可累积的共享基础设施。

Method: 提出分层研究资产框架 ScholarStack：将论文集合编译为三个互补层级的可复用、版本化、保留溯源的资产——（1）源锚定的论文级陈述，（2）领域级组织，（3）证据锚定的跨论文综合；通过统一访问接口，按各任务所需证据粒度返回任务特定视图，并保留研究条件、来源可追溯性与验证状态。在四类任务族（共十个任务设置）上实例化，在匹配基座模型下将使用编译资产的智能体与任务特定基线进行对比。

Result: 质量增益集中于需要跨论文证据的任务，如多论文问答与文献综述生成；在每一个被测量的任务上，查询时 token 成本均下降，原因是资产一次编译、跨任务复用。

Conclusion: 分层研究资产可作为科学智能体的共享基础设施，推动基于文献的辅助从孤立的文档处理转向累积的、证据锚定的工作流。

Abstract: Scientific agents support a range of literature-based research tasks, such as retrieval, question answering, evidence-grounded generation, and claim assessment. Most existing systems, however, are organized around individual tasks: the same papers are repeatedly retrieved, segmented, and interpreted, and the understanding built in one task is difficult to reuse in the next. We present ScholarStack, a layered research asset framework that compiles a paper collection into reusable, versioned, and provenance-preserving assets at three complementary levels: source-grounded paper-level statements, domain-level organization, and evidence-grounded cross-paper syntheses. A common access interface returns task-specific views at the evidence granularity each task requires, preserving study conditions, source traceability, and verification status. We instantiate the framework on four task families spanning ten task settings, comparing agents that use the compiled assets with task-specific baselines under matched base models. Quality gains concentrate on tasks that require cross-paper evidence, such as multi-paper question answering and literature review generation, and query-time token cost falls on every task where it is measured, with assets compiled once and reused across tasks. These results suggest that layered research assets can serve as shared infrastructure for scientific agents, shifting literature-based assistance from isolated document processing toward cumulative, evidence-grounded workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [PRQuant: Permutation Residual Quantization for Low-Overhead Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22106)
*Peiran Wang,Anqi Wang,Jiaying Zhao,Huiwen Yang,Zhenyu Ming,Rongqian Wang,Yiwu Yao,Kun Tian,Xin Yao,Gong Zhang,Fan Yang,Zhongyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRQuant：一种免训练、低开销的量化框架，通过通道重排与静态权重侧残差补偿，将散乱残差补偿转为规则的尾部增广GEMM，降低延迟并提升低比特量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性层低比特量化的精度常被少量离群值主导。现有方法（平滑、旋转、残差补偿）虽可缓解，但往往给权重引入新的精度瓶颈，且多为在线实现，带来沉重执行开销。

Method: PRQuant（Permutation Residual Quantization）为免训练、低开销框架。在AWQ式缩放后，识别对权重量化误差贡献最大的输入通道，将其置换到连续尾部块，并离线构建对应残差权重子张量；推理时该连续结构使激活侧无需昂贵的在线聚集操作，将散乱残差补偿转为规则的尾部增广GEMM。

Result: 有效降低down-projection重建误差。消融显示平滑与残差补偿是数值改进的主要驱动，置换提供稳定的边际数值收益，并实现硬件友好的连续布局、消除动态聚集开销。在五个下游基准上平均精度优于默认MXFP4及所评估的PTQ基线，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507上分别较MXFP4提升1.24和0.55。

Conclusion: PRQuant通过通道重排与静态残差补偿相结合，在免训练、低开销下同时改善量化精度与推理延迟，整体优于MXFP4及所评估的PTQ基线。

Abstract: Accuracy of Low-bit quantization of linear layers is often dominated by a small number of outliers. Although existing methods, such as smoothing, rotation, or residual-based approaches, may mitigate this problem, they often introduce new accuracy bottlenecks to weights. Besides, most of these techniques are implemented as online approaches, which can result in heavy execution overheads. To address the afore-mentioned issues, We propose PRQuant (Permutation Residual Quantization), a training-free and low-overhead framework that combines channel reorganization with static weight-side residual compensation. After AWQ-style scaling, PRQuant identifies the input channels that contribute most to weight quantization error, permutes them into contiguous tail blocks, and constructs their residual weight sub-tensors offline. During inference, this contiguous structure enables the activation side to use tail blocks seamlessly without the expensive online gathering operation, and turns scattered residual compensation into a regular tail-augmented GEMM, substantially reducing latency. Experiments demonstrate that PRQuant effectively reduces down-projection reconstruction error. Ablation studies confirm that smoothing and residual compensation are the primary drivers of numerical improvement, while permutation provides a consistent marginal numerical benefit and, more importantly, enables a hardware-friendly contiguous layout that eliminates dynamic gathering overhead. Overall, PRQuant outperforms default MXFP4 and the evaluated PTQ baselines in average accuracy across five downstream benchmarks, improving over MXFP4 by 1.24 and 0.55 on Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, respectively.

</details>


### [154] [Generalized Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22107)
*Huizi Cui,Zongbo Han,Chenggong Ding,Naichuan Xiao,Jialong Yang,Jingdong Chen,Guangyu Wang,Qinghua Hu,Changqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于大规模合成多模态数据集训练的通用多模态基础模型，可处理任意模态组合与任意预测任务，在18个数据集上无需任务适配即达到竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型只能处理预定义模态和单一任务，难以快速适配新下游应用，因此需要一种不依赖特定模态、能编码可迁移多模态关联模式的统一融合模型。

Method: 基于因果结构形式化建模现实多模态数据的生成过程，构建大规模合成多模态数据集；提出在合成数据生成任务上训练的学习范式，学习可迁移的多模态关联；推理时通过上下文示例激活恰当的关联。

Result: 在覆盖12种模态、11类预测任务的18个真实数据集上，无需任务特定适配即取得与专用模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 统一的通用多模态基础模型可行，不依赖特定模态、通过合成为驱动学习可迁移多模态关联，可泛化到任意模态组合与任意预测任务。

Abstract: Making prediction with multimodal data is widely used in diverse scenarios. Existing multimodal fusion models, once deployed, can only handle predefined modalities (e.g., vision, text and audio) and single tasks, making it difficult to quickly adapt to new downstream applications. Therefore, a natural yet rather aggressive question arises, whether there exists a general multimodal fusion model that can be applied to arbitrary modality combinations and arbitrary prediction tasks. We argue that a unified multimodal fusion model should not depend on specific modalities and instead encode transferable patterns of multimodal correlation. To this end, we propose a simple and effective learning paradigm based on training over the generation of large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse causal structures that formally characterize the generative processes of multimodal data in real world. Building on this framework, we propose the generalized multimodal foundation model, a unified foundation model for generalized multimodal learning. By constructing large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse correlation patterns, our model encodes transferable multimodal correlations during training and activates appropriate associations through in-context examples during inference. Extensive experiments on 18 real-world datasets spanning 12 modalities and 11 prediction tasks demonstrate that our model achieves competitive performance with specialized models without task-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [155] [Correcting Learning-based Perception for Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22108)
*Yan Miao,Hussein Darir,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两步式ML感知状态估计修正策略：离线利用感知契约的原像刻画不确定性，运行时用风险启发式从不确定估计中选取状态驱动控制决策，在多个视觉ACC场景中显著提升安全性且干预轻微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传感器的工作边界较为明确，而基于机器学习的感知模块"何时有效、何时失效"的边界难以刻画。感知错误会导致下游控制出现不安全或过度保守的行为，因此需要一种能够刻画并修正ML感知不确定性的方法。

Method: 两步策略：第一步离线计算，利用感知契约（perception contracts）的原像来刻画ML状态估计所产生的不确定性；第二步在运行时使用风险启发式，从不确定的估计集合中选择特定状态来驱动控制决策。基于多种视觉自适应巡航控制（ACC）模块，在不同天气与道路场景下进行大规模仿真评估，感知模块采用Yolo与LaneNet。

Result: 在45个原本因Yolo与LaneNet感知导致安全违规的ACC场景中，运行时感知修正使73%的场景恢复了安全性；剩余27%无法恢复的场景源于感知契约原像的构造不完全符合规范。该方法并不过度保守：被修正场景平均仅带来2.8%的完成时间增加，干预程度较轻。

Conclusion: 所提出的运行时感知修正策略能有效提升ML感知驱动控制系统的安全性，同时保持较低的时间代价和轻微的干预，但其效果受限于感知契约原像构造的合规性。

Abstract: Learning-enabled perception is important in many autonomous systems. Unlike traditional sensors, the boundary where ML perception does or does not work is poorly characterized. Incorrect perception can lead to unsafe or overtly conservative downstream control actions. In this paper, we propose a two-step strategy for correcting ML-based state estimation. First, an offline computation is used to characterize the uncertainties resulting from the ML module's state estimation, using preimages of perception contracts. Second, at runtime, a risk heuristic is used to choose particular states from the uncertain estimates to drive the control decisions. We perform extensive simulation-based evaluation of this runtime perception correction strategy on different vision-based adaptive cruise controllers (ACC modules), in different weather conditions, and road scenarios. Out of 45 ACC scenarios where the original perception-based control system using Yolo and LaneNet led to safety violations, in 73% of the scenarios, our runtime perception correction preserved safety; our method wouldn't be able to recover 27% of the scenarios where the construction of the preimages of perception contracts is not fully conformant. Further, our runtime perception correction strategy is not overly conservative---on the average only a 2.8% increase in completion time is experienced in the corrected scenarios, with mild interventions.

</details>


### [156] [A Shared Learning Rate Is Not a Neutral Control in Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22109)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 选择性on-policy蒸馏中"共享学习率"并非中性控制：在GSM8K+LoRA下所有选择性臂随学习率波动（最高17.7pp），dense却基本平稳，导致dense-vs-selective结论在相邻学习率间从10.1pp变为5.1pp；作者称之为"selector-rate entanglement"，并建议报告臂×率矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献在比较不同selector时，统一使用单一共享学习率，并默认该学习率是中性控制。本文质疑这一前提，旨在检验学习率是否会与选择性监督发生交互，从而使selector比较结论依赖于被视作"背景参数"的学习率设置。

Method: 在GSM8K上以Qwen2.5-1.5B为学生、7B为教师做LoRA微调，扫描8档学习率网格，比较dense监督与多种选择性臂（随机5%子集、total-variation selector、teachability selector），并检验成对显著性。通过比较AdamW更新幅度与梯度范数分离"选择本身"与"步长"的影响。进一步做预注册的frozen-scoring消融（用初始学生打分，criterion、budget、on-policy rollout不变，每格12个种子）。另在全量微调（1e-6至1e-5）及MATH-500上复现。

Result: LoRA下dense监督统计上平稳（波动1.8pp，p=0.26），而各选择性臂随学习率显著变化：随机5%为5.4pp、total-variation为6.7pp、teachability达17.7pp。dense-vs-selective差距在lr=1e-4为10.1pp、在5e-5为5.1pp（2.0倍差异）；六个selector成对显著性中有两个在相邻学习率间翻转且无排名反转。AdamW更新幅度与学习率偏差仅2.2%，尽管梯度范数跨臂相差15.5倍，说明根源在selection而非step size。frozen-scoring消融显示live scoring额外增加3.79±1.69pp的学习率敏感性（p=0.035），frozen臂仍显著纠缠（p=0.015），即反馈回路加剧而非导致该现象。全量微调下模式放大：dense波动19.8pp、selective达49.5pp，结论从发表操作点的+3.6pp（不显著）变为高一档的+34pp（p=0.005）。MATH-500上LoRA未复现学习率依赖，但选择性训练约10pp的代价复现。

Conclusion: 学习率与选择性监督存在系统性交互（selector-rate entanglement），使dense-vs-selective及selector间的比较结论高度依赖学习率这一通常被忽略的参数。作者建议以报告"臂×学习率矩阵"而非单列共享学习率结果，作为selector比较的前提条件。

Abstract: Selective on-policy distillation trains a student only at the token positions a selector scores highest, and the literature compares selectors under a single shared learning rate--a control chosen to be neutral. We show it is not. Under LoRA on GSM8K (Qwen2.5-1.5B student, 7B teacher), across an 8x learning-rate grid, dense supervision is statistically flat (swing 1.8 pp, p=0.26) while every selective arm moves with the rate: 5.4 pp for a random 5% subset, 6.7 pp for a total-variation selector, up to 17.7 pp for a teachability selector. Consequently the dense-versus-selective verdict reads 10.1 pp at lr=1e-4 but 5.1 pp at 5e-5--a 2.0x difference decided by a parameter the protocol treats as scenery--and two of six pairwise significance calls between selectors flip between adjacent rates without any rank inversion. We call this selector-rate entanglement and trace it to selection itself rather than step size: AdamW update magnitudes track the rate to within 2.2% despite 15.5x gradient-norm differences across arms. A preregistered frozen-scoring ablation (selection scored by the initial student; criterion, budget, and on-policy rollouts unchanged; 12 seeds per cell) shows live scoring adds 3.79+/-1.69 pp of rate sensitivity (p=0.035) while the frozen arm remains significantly entangled (p=0.015): the feedback loop aggravates the phenomenon rather than causing it. Under full fine-tuning at the rates this literature actually uses (1e-6 to 1e-5) the pattern grows: dense itself swings 19.8 pp, the selective arm 49.5 pp, and the verdict ranges from a non-significant +3.6 pp at the published operating point to +34 pp (p=0.005) one notch hotter. On MATH-500 the rate dependence does not reproduce under LoRA, scoping that result, while the ~10 pp cost of selective training does. We prescribe reporting the arm x rate matrix, not a shared-rate column, as a precondition for selector comparisons.

</details>


### [157] [Toward Fairness in Machine Learning Models for Predicting Treatment Retention and Premature Discontinuation in Medication for Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22113)
*Tongnian Wang,Carolina Vivas-Valencia,Cici Bauer,Yanmin Gong,Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了预测阿片类药物使用障碍药物治疗（MOUD）保留与提前脱落的机器学习模型的算法公平性，发现模型在总体性能可接受时仍存在亚组间性能差距，偏见缓解可缩小但无法完全消除这些差距，且存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOUD治疗中保留率与完成率长期偏低，促使研究者采用机器学习模型预测保留情况并识别提前脱落风险患者。然而，这些模型在不同患者群体间的公平性尚未得到充分探索，引发其在治疗决策支持中应用的担忧。因此，需要系统评估算法公平性并考察偏见缓解技术的有效性。

Method: 使用横断面治疗事件数据集-出院记录（TEDS-D），涵盖2015至2019年间美国出院个体的治疗事件。针对接受门诊MOUD的个体，训练四种机器学习模型预测提前治疗脱落及保留超过180天。在种族、族裔、年龄和性别定义的患者亚组中评估总体性能与亚组错误率，并辅以模型解释分析。进一步评估偏见缓解技术及其对公平性和预测性能的影响。

Result: 机器学习模型在MOUD结局预测中，即使总体预测性能看似可接受，仍会表现出亚组层面的性能差距；偏见缓解技术可以缩小这些差距，但无法完全消除，且伴随权衡（公平性与预测性能之间的取舍）。

Conclusion: 研究强调了公平性感知评估与亚组性能透明报告的重要性，为MOUD治疗场景中风险分层与护理优先级排序的机器学习模型的负责任、情境敏感使用提供了实践见解。

Abstract: Persistent low retention and completion rates in medications for opioid use disorder (MOUD) have driven the use of machine learning (ML) models to predict retention and identify patients at risk of premature discontinuation. However, the fairness of these models across patient populations remains largely unexplored, raising concerns about their application in treatment decision support. This study systematically assesses algorithmic fairness in ML models for predicting MOUD retention and premature discontinuation and investigates the effectiveness of bias mitigation techniques. Using the cross-sectional Treatment Episode Data Set-Discharges (TEDS-D), which includes treatment episodes for individuals in the U.S. discharged between 2015 and 2019, we trained four ML models to predict premature treatment discontinuation and retention beyond 180 days among individuals receiving outpatient MOUD. We evaluated overall performance and subgroup-level error rates across patient subgroups defined by race, ethnicity, age, and sex, complemented by model explanation analyses. We further assessed bias mitigation techniques and their effects on both fairness and predictive performance. Our findings demonstrate that ML models for MOUD outcome prediction can exhibit subgroup-level performance gaps even when overall predictive performance appears acceptable and that bias mitigation can reduce, but not fully eliminate, these gaps without trade-offs. By demonstrating the importance of fairness-aware evaluation and transparent reporting of subgroup performance, this study provides practical insights for the responsible and context-sensitive use of ML models for risk stratification and care prioritization in MOUD treatment settings.

</details>


### [158] [ZoAQ: Adaptive Zeroth-Order Querying via Query-Reuse Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22115)
*Yangyang Feng,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZoAQ 是一种基于查询复用的自适应零阶优化方法，利用历史函数评估同时指导更新与查询决策，无需额外验证查询即可实现自适应查询分配，在多类任务上显著减少查询开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZOO）依赖函数评估估计更新，扰动查询是主要成本。固定预算每步消耗相同查询数，效率低；而自适应控制器虽可节省查询，却常需额外 oracle 调用来验证估计器可靠性，抵消了收益。因此需要一种不自带额外验证开销的自适应查询分配机制。

Method: 提出 ZoAQ：以查询复用为核心，不丢弃每步后的历史评估，而是让其同时服务于下一次更新和"是否继续查询"的决策，从而在不增加验证查询的前提下实现自适应查询分配。理论分析刻画了这种一致性（agreement）何时能识别支持下降的更新，并引导控制器设定足够的查询预算。

Result: 在合成目标上，相对于固定基线（1.2M 查询）减少 43–48% 查询；黑盒攻击中，MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 分别以平均 320 和 625 次查询达到 100% 成功率；在四种 OPT 微调设置下，相对固定 K=4 节省 43–46% 前向评估，任务内准确率变化范围为 -0.018 至 +0.010。

Conclusion: 通过复用已有查询同时支持更新与查询决策，ZoAQ 实现了无额外验证成本的自适应查询分配，在合成优化、黑盒攻击与大模型微调中均稳定降低查询量且保持性能，验证了查询复用作为自适应控制信号的有效性。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization (ZOO) estimates updates from function evaluations, making perturbation queries a primary cost. Fixed budgets spend the same number of queries at every step, while adaptive controllers may offset their savings by using additional oracle calls to test estimator reliability. We introduce ZoAQ, an adaptive ZOO method built around query reuse. Rather than discarding past evaluations after each step, ZoAQ makes them useful for both the next update and the decision to query further. This enables adaptive query allocation without extra validation queries. Our analysis characterizes when this agreement identifies an update that supports descent and guides the controller to a sufficient query budget. On synthetic objectives, ZoAQ reduces queries by 43-48% relative to fixed baselines using 1.2M queries. In black-box attacks, it reaches 100% success with 320 and 625 average queries on MNIST and CIFAR-10, respectively. Across four OPT fine-tuning settings, ZoAQ saves 43-46% forward evaluations relative to fixed K=4, with accuracy changes within tasks ranging from -0.018 to +0.010.

</details>


### [159] [LE4Mob: Towards Inductive, Distance-Aware and General-Purpose Location Embedding for Human Mobility Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22117)
*Xinglei Wang,Stephen Law,Zichao Zeng,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LE4Mob——一种归纳式、距离感知、地理衍生的位置嵌入框架，用于人类移动建模，在下一位置预测与通勤流生成任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置嵌入依赖移动观测数据，无法表示未见位置，且对地理距离的保持约束较弱，导致跨数据集和跨移动任务的复用性受限。

Method: LE4Mob扩展了对比式语言—位置预训练，并引入距离感知正则化目标，促使嵌入空间保留空间关系；其基于地理上下文预训练，可生成未见位置的嵌入，且不依赖下游移动任务的监督信号。

Result: 在个体级下一位置预测和群体级通勤流生成两类任务、多个数据集与多个研究区域上，LE4Mob均优于强基线，在归纳设置以及下游模型直接依赖位置嵌入间交互时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 距离感知、地理衍生的位置表示可作为人类移动建模的可复用基础，具有良好的归纳性与跨任务迁移性。

Abstract: Location representations provide mobility models with fundamental information about the spatial position, functional characteristics, and relationships of places. However, existing embeddings are often dependent on mobility observations, unable to represent unseen locations, and weakly constrained to retain geographic distance. This limits their reuse across datasets and mobility tasks. To address these limitations, we propose LE4Mob, an inductive, distance-aware, and geography-derived location embedding framework for mobility modelling. LE4Mob extends contrastive language-location pre-training while introducing a distance-aware regularisation objective that encourages the embedding space to preserve spatial relationships. Pre-trained from geographic context, LE4Mob can encode rich spatial-semantic information and generate embeddings for unseen locations inductively. Its independence from downstream mobility task supervision also makes it transferable across different mobility tasks. We evaluate LE4Mob on individual-level next location prediction and population-level commuter flow generation. Experiments across multiple datasets and study areas show that LE4Mob outperforms strong baselines, with particular advantages in inductive settings and when downstream models rely directly on interactions between location embeddings. These findings demonstrate the potential of distance-aware, geography-derived location representations as reusable foundations for human mobility modelling.

</details>


### [160] [Success Leaves Detours: Learning Executable Walkthroughs for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22120)
*Kaijie Chen,Chenyu Fang,Liang Yan,Bo Li,Bo Zhang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trace 框架，从稀疏奖励轨迹中归纳出紧凑、状态条件化且可验证的"可执行演练流程"（Walkthrough）。它利用奖励与持久状态变化检测进展锚点、传播信用并估计动作前置条件，再通过反向依赖切片提取依赖一致的动作链，剔除无关循环与绕路。在 J-TTL、WebShop、ScienceWorld 上，Trace 优于 8 个基线，平均 AUC 与 Final-3 分别提升 30.0% 和 40.5%，且推理 token 更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自进化智能体依赖复用过往经验，但稀疏奖励轨迹中充斥着失败、循环与绕路；而常见摘要又丢失了执行所必需的状态条件与动作依赖关系，导致经验难以直接复用。作者因此研究从稀疏奖励轨迹中归纳"可执行演练流程"这一问题。

Method: 核心观察是：延迟信用能识别与进展相关的动作，却无法判断这些动作是否产生了后续动作所必需的事实。据此提出 Trace：一是从奖励和持久状态变化中检测进展锚点；二是传播信用以识别有价值的转移；三是通过跨回合的成功与失败证据估计动作前置条件；最后进行反向依赖切片，将所需事实回溯至其产生者，抽取依赖一致的动作链，同时去除无关循环与绕路。所得 Walkthrough 编码入口条件、有序的状态—动作—效果步骤以及完成与失败谓词。

Result: 在 J-TTL、WebShop、ScienceWorld 三个环境上，使用三种开源 LLM 进行实验，Trace 一致优于 8 个测试时学习与记忆基线。相比最强基线，平均 AUC 提升 30.0%，Final-3 提升 40.5%，同时使用更少的推理 token。

Conclusion: 长时程交互更受益于状态条件化的可执行流程，而非完整轨迹或抽象摘要。Walkthrough 形式支持经验复用、中间状态恢复与程序化验证。

Abstract: Test-time self-evolving agents improve by reusing past experience, yet sparse-reward trajectories contain failures, loops, and detours, while summaries often omit the state conditions and action dependencies needed for execution. We study executable Walkthrough induction from sparse-reward trajectories: extracting compact, state-conditioned, and verifiable procedures. Our key observation is that delayed credit identifies actions associated with progress but cannot determine whether they produce facts required by later actions. We propose Trace, a credit-guided, dependency-grounded framework that compiles noisy trajectories into executable Walkthrough Memory. It detects progress anchors from rewards and persistent state changes, propagates credit to identify valuable transitions, and estimates action prerequisites from cross-episode success and failure evidence. Backward dependency slicing then traces required facts to their producers, extracting dependency-consistent action chains while removing irrelevant loops and detours. The resulting Walkthroughs encode entry conditions, ordered state--action--effect steps, and completion and failure predicates, supporting reuse, intermediate-state resumption, and programmatic verification. Experiments on J-TTL, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three open-source LLMs show that Trace consistently outperforms eight test-time learning and memory baselines. Compared with the strongest baseline, it improves average AUC and Final-$3$ by $30.0%$ and $40.5%$, respectively, while using fewer inference tokens. These results show that long-horizon interaction benefits more from state-conditioned executable procedures than from complete trajectories or abstract summaries.

</details>


### [161] [Modelling daily activity patterns from mobile phone location data via deep representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22121)
*Xinglei Wang,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Zichao Zeng,Stephen Law,James Haworth,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督模型ACE，将活动模式挖掘形式化为表征、聚类与解释一体化流程，利用预训练城市嵌入结合访问时间与停留时长，通过Transformer建模停留序列，实现无标签移动定位数据的活动模式识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动采集的手机定位数据虽能提供大规模、纵向的人类移动观测，但无法直接揭示活动目的。访问地点的功能特征可提供情境信息，然而其与活动目的之间的关系在混合用途城市环境中仍不确定，因此需要一种无需活动目的标签即可刻画活动模式的方法。

Method: 将活动模式挖掘概念化为表征、聚类与解释的集成过程；提出Activity Chain Encoder（ACE）用于表征阶段。ACE为自监督模型，融合预训练城市嵌入与访问时间、停留时长，采用Transformer建模停留的序列组织，并通过掩码活动建模与身份引导对比学习进行训练，无需确定性活动目的标签。学到的日表征聚合为用户级画像后进行聚类，并结合时序—功能模式与人口普查人口统计背景进行解释。

Result: 应用于伦敦的手机应用定位数据并与三种代表性方法比较，ACE支持识别出六个差异化的工作日活动模式群体，各群体具有不同的日节律、城市功能情境与人口统计关联。

Conclusion: 这些互补证据进一步支持构建有实证基础的活动模式人物画像，为从无标签手机定位数据中提取具有行为意义的人群模式提供了整体路径；全流程源代码已公开。

Abstract: Passively collected mobile phone location data provide large-scale, longitudinal observations of human mobility but do not directly reveal activity purposes. The functional characteristics of visited locations offer useful contextual information, yet their relationship with activity purpose remains uncertain, particularly in mixed-use urban environments. We conceptualise activity pattern mining as an integrated process of representation, clustering, and interpretation, and propose the Activity Chain Encoder (ACE) for the representation stage. ACE is a self-supervised model that combines pre-trained urban embeddings with visit timing and duration and uses a Transformer to model the sequential organisation of stays. It is trained using masked activity modelling and identity-guided contrastive learning without requiring deterministic activity purpose labels. Learned daily representations are aggregated into user-level profiles, clustered, and interpreted through temporal-functional patterns and Census-derived demographic context. Applied to mobile phone app location data from London and compared with three representative methods, ACE supports the identification of six differentiated weekday activity-pattern groups characterised by distinct daily rhythms, urban functional contexts, and demographic associations. These complementary forms of evidence further support the development of empirically grounded activity-pattern personas, establishing a holistic route for deriving behaviourally meaningful population patterns from unlabelled mobile phone location data. The source code for the entire analytical pipeline developed in this study is publicly available at https://github.com/xlwang233/ACE.

</details>


### [162] [Rank Portability Does Not Imply Feasibility Portability: Target-Specific Evaluation of Joint Hardware Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22122)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验"跨设备架构排序可迁移即可用代理设备做目标端模型选择"的假设，发现排序可迁移性与联合延迟-能耗可行性可迁移性、目标端决策支持是三个不同的评估对象；跨设备评估应报告哪些目标环境真正支持所声称的工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨设备硬件评估常默认：只要架构排序能在设备间迁移，就可用代理设备支持目标端模型选择。该假设在联合延迟-能耗可行性这一多目标、硬约束场景下缺乏系统检验，可能导致代理复用带来高风险误判。

Method: 在两个公开架构族上进行压力测试。NAS-Bench-201上考察跨设备排序相关性与目标可比的可行集重叠；用忠实复现的AdaProxy诊断检验边界失败是否源于适配能力弱；用精确有限样本分裂共形分析刻画证据瓶颈。随后在HW-GPT-Bench的13台设备、10,000个GPT架构上复现，以RTX3080为代理设备，在匹配的联合约束下测量目标延迟SRCC与代理复用违规风险。

Result: NAS-Bench-201上跨设备排序相关性中等，可行集重叠不完整；AdaProxy显著改善延迟排序，说明边界失败并非单纯源于适配弱；有限单侧90%阈值需至少9个校准观测。GPT复现中，相对RTX3080，目标延迟SRCC为0.951–0.996，但匹配联合约束下代理复用违规风险高达33.3%–100%。

Conclusion: 排序可迁移性、可行性可迁移性与目标端特定决策支持是相互独立的评估对象，高排序相关性并不保证可行性可迁移。跨设备评估应明确报告哪些目标环境真正支持所声称的工作点，而非仅以代理设备的排序结果替代目标端决策依据。

Abstract: Cross-device hardware evaluation often assumes that if architecture rankings transfer across devices, a proxy device can support target-side model selection. We stress-test this assumption for joint latency-energy feasibility across two public architecture families. On NAS-Bench-201, cross-device rank correlations are moderate, while target-comparable feasible-set overlap remains incomplete. A faithful AdaProxy diagnostic substantially improves latency ranking, showing that the observed boundary failures are not simply due to weak adaptation. Exact finite-sample split-conformal analysis also exposes an evidence bottleneck: a finite one-sided 90% threshold requires at least nine calibration observations. We then replicate the phenomenon on 10,000 GPT architectures across 13 HW-GPT-Bench devices. Relative to an RTX3080 proxy, target latency SRCC ranges from 0.951 to 0.996, yet proxy-reuse violation risk ranges from 33.3% to 100% under matched joint constraints. These results show that rank portability, feasibility portability, and target-specific decision support are distinct evaluation objects. Cross-device evaluations should therefore report which target environments actually support the operating point being claimed.

</details>


### [163] [StationPDE: Station-Oriented Surface PDE Learning for Multi-Station Multivariate Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22123)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StationPDE，一种面向气象站的表面偏微分方程学习模型，通过构建地形感知连续场并分解物理演化，结合数据驱动扩散分支与自适应路由，实现多站多变量天气预报，在Weather2K和MeteoNet上平均降低约9.6%的MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多站天气预报模型仅学习离散站点间统计依赖，缺乏显式物理演化；而基于PDE的气象模型虽具可解释物理动力学，却依赖连续场和上层大气变量，这些在仅含地面站点数据中不可得。因此需要弥合两类方法之间的差距。

Method: StationPDE从离散站点观测构建地形感知的连续表面场，并将其物理演化分解为表面风输送与上层大气推断：表面风输送显式演化可观测气象变量，上层推断以可学习水平扩散近似缺失的上层变量影响；同时并行数据驱动扩散分支捕捉互补运动模式，并通过自适应路由器融合两路预测进行站点级多变量预报。

Result: 在Weather2K和MeteoNet数据集上，StationPDE持续优于最先进基线，相比最强基线平均降低约9.6%的MSE。代码见https://github.com/hnu-vis/StationPDE。

Conclusion: StationPDE以站向表面PDE学习弥合了统计模型与物理模型之间的差距，在不依赖连续场与上层变量的条件下实现可解释的物理演化建模，并取得一致性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-station multivariate weather forecasting aims to forecast future weather variables at multiple weather stations from historical surface observations. Existing station forecasting models learn statistical dependencies among discrete stations, but lack explicit physical evolution. Meanwhile, PDE-based weather models provide interpretable physical dynamics, yet require continuous fields and upper-air variables unavailable in surface station data. To bridge this gap, we propose StationPDE, a station-oriented surface PDE learning model. StationPDE constructs a terrain-aware continuous surface field from discrete station observations and decomposes its physical evolution into surface wind transport and upper-air inference. Surface wind transport explicitly evolves observable weather variables, while upper-air inference uses learnable horizontal diffusion to approximate the missing influence of unavailable upper-air variables. A parallel data-driven diffusion branch captures complementary motion patterns, and an adaptive router integrates the two forecasts for station-level multivariate forecasting. Experiments on Weather2K and MeteoNet show that StationPDE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing MSE by about $9.6\%$ on average compared with the strongest baseline. Code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/hnu-vis/StationPDE.

</details>


### [164] [SolarFlowRefiner: Refinement-Aware Flow Matching for Surface Solar Radiation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22126)
*Udbhav Srivastava,Antonita Racheal,Yiheng Chen,Runlong Yu,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SolarFlowRefiner，一种 refinement-aware 的流匹配框架，通过将精化目标反传至生成器采样器，实现生成与校正联合优化，用于从粗分辨率 ERA5 与卫星通道重建高分辨率 SSR 场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率地表太阳辐射（SSR）对光伏预报和电网调度至关重要，但物理一致的重分析产品（如 ERA5）分辨率过粗，无法刻画局地云致变率。单个 ERA5 格点内可能同时存在日照区与云影区，缺失的高分辨率订正具有空间锐利性和内在模糊性。一阶段预测器易产生过平滑，而事后精化（post-hoc refinement）存在阶段间不匹配：生成器独立优化，但其输出决定了精化器的初始状态。

Method: 面向 ERA5 辐射变量与共配准卫星通道的多源降尺度任务，设计条件 FlowMatch 生成器，先预测相对于上采样 ERA5 基线的归一化订正。随后在“当前 FlowMatch 输出与目标残差之间”的预测条件状态上训练精化器，使其暴露于生成器产生的结构化误差。进一步将精化目标通过 FlowMatch 采样器反向传播，使生成与校正针对最终重建结果联合优化。

Result: 在按天分块的 ERA5–SolarCube 基准上，方法相较独立生成与事后精化均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: SolarFlowRefiner 为降尺度提供了将生成式预测器与迭代校正器耦合的通用策略，可作为更广泛框架使用。

Abstract: High-resolution surface solar radiation (SSR) is important for solar forecasting and grid operation. However, physically consistent reanalysis products are too coarse to resolve localized cloud-driven variability. In this paper, we study a multisource downscaling task that reconstructs high-resolution SolarCube SSR fields from coarse ERA5 radiative variables and co-registered satellite channels. The task is challenging because a single ERA5 grid cell may contain both sunlit and cloud-shadowed regions. As a result, the missing high-resolution correction can be spatially sharp and inherently ambiguous. One-stage predictors often oversmooth these structures. Post-hoc refinement also introduces a stage-wise mismatch: the generator is optimized independently, even though its output determines the refiner's initial state. We introduce SolarFlowRefiner, a refinement-aware flow-matching framework for SSR downscaling. A conditional FlowMatch generator first predicts a normalized correction to an upsampled ERA5 baseline. The refiner is then trained on prediction-conditioned states between the current FlowMatch output and the target residual. This exposes the refiner to the structured errors produced by the generator. The refinement objective is also backpropagated through the FlowMatch sampler, allowing generation and correction to be jointly optimized for the final reconstruction. Experiments on a day-blocked ERA5--SolarCube benchmark show consistent improvements over standalone generation and post-hoc refinement. More broadly, SolarFlowRefiner provides a general strategy for coupling generative predictors with iterative correctors.

</details>


### [165] [Helix-FNO: Spectral-Domain Operator Learning Coupled with a High-Fidelity Mechanistic Model for Fast Surrogate Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22129)
*Jiabao Zhao,Chuwei Wang,Jinxi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Helix-FNO 教师-学生架构，用 32 态机理教师生成高保真数据，训练傅里叶神经算子学生直接学习解算子，实现毫秒级推理、比机理模型快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺度处理过程的机理仿真模型是唯一可信、可外推的物理化学动力学描述，但运行速度过慢，无法支撑现代决策引擎在 5 分钟决策节奏下所需的大量前向评估。传统代理建模往往只学习单一配置下的单一解，难以泛化到新的进水profile、控制设置或厂区布局。

Method: 提出 Helix-FNO 教师-学生架构：教师为 32 状态机理模型，通过拉丁超立方与基于不确定性的主动学习筛选输入场-解对，覆盖边界与超载工况；学生为傅里叶神经算子（FNO），在谱域学习解算子本身而非单个解。文中给出算子形式化、谱卷积定义、加权蒸馏损失与主动学习准则，并分析截断傅里叶展开相对于参数化解流形光滑度的逼近误差。

Result: 算例研究在精度、数据集效率与推理时延上，将 Helix-FNO 与物理信息网络和数据驱动循环代理进行对比，并在速度-精度帕累托前沿上定位各方法。所得算子在毫秒级推理下比机理教师快三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该算子化代理具备大规模候选筛选与在线决策支持所需的能力，是支撑 5 分钟决策节奏的可行技术路径。

Abstract: Mechanistic simulation models of full-scale treatment processes remain the only trustworthy, extrapolative description of the underlying physico-chemical dynamics, yet their runtime is far too slow to support the thousands of forward evaluations that a modern decision engine requires at a 5-minute decision cadence. The standard remedy-surrogate modelling-often produces a network that learns a single solution for a single configuration, so it generalises poorly to new influent profiles, control settings or plant layouts. This paper presents Helix-FNO, a teacher-student architecture that couples a thirty-two-state mechanistic teacher with a Fourier neural operator (FNO) student. The teacher supplies a high-fidelity dataset of input-field-to-solution pairs, curated by Latin-hypercube and uncertainty-based active learning to cover the boundary and overload regimes that matter in practice; the student learns, in the spectral domain, the solution operator itself rather than any single solution, thereby moving from learning one instance to learning an entire family of equations. We give the operator formulation, the spectral convolution definition, the weighted distillation loss and the active-learning criterion, and we analyse the approximation error of a truncated Fourier expansion with respect to the smoothness of the parametric solution manifold. An illustrative study compares Helix-FNO against a physics-informed network and a data-driven recurrent surrogate on accuracy, dataset efficiency and inference latency, and places the methods on a speed-accuracy Pareto front. The resulting operator is three orders of magnitude faster than the mechanistic teacher at millisecond inference, which is precisely the capability required for massive candidate screening and online decision support.

</details>


### [166] [Hierarchical Bayesian optimization of an aircraft-based multi-agent system-of-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22130)
*Paul Saves,Thierry Lefebvre,Nathalie Bartoli,Jasper Bussemaker,Nikolaos Kalliatakis,Nabih Naeem,Prajwal Prakasha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层贝叶斯优化框架，利用高斯过程元建模处理系统之系统（SoS）架构设计中的离散选择、条件依赖与异构变量，并在野火抑制多智能体系统案例中验证了其搜索效率与鲁棒性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂SoS架构设计依赖高保真多物理场、多学科仿真，但仿真计算成本高且存在评估失败风险，直接优化不可行；传统代理模型方法难以应对SoS固有的离散架构选择、条件依赖和异构设计变量，亟需可扩展的建模与优化方法。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯优化框架，以高斯过程元模型（meta-modeling）为核心代理模型，对离散架构选择、条件依赖结构以及异构设计变量进行分层表征，从而在有限仿真预算下组织并求解SoS架构优化问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，相较于常规代理模型优化方法，分层形式化表述提升了搜索效率与鲁棒性，能够在有限仿真预算下探索规模大、结构多样的设计空间；框架成功应用于COLOSSUS项目中的航空多智能体野火抑制系统，协调具有互补角色的异构空中平台。

Conclusion: 该工作为SoS架构设计与模型探索提供了可扩展方法，是可转移至航空、可持续出行与韧性系统设计的实用方法论；作为分层贝叶斯优化在真实SoS问题上的首批实践示范之一，兼具方法学与实践推进意义。

Abstract: Developing innovative system architectures increasingly relies on advanced modeling and optimization techniques to frame the architecting process and define the corresponding computational problems. For complex System-of-Systems (SoS), high-fidelity multiphysics and multidisciplinary simulations are essential for capturing detailed behaviors. However, their computational expense and the risk of evaluation failures make direct optimization challenging. To overcome these limitations, surrogate-based approaches, like Bayesian optimization, have emerged as effective tools for managing expensive, black-box simulation tasks. This work introduces a hierarchical Bayesian optimization framework that leverages Gaussian process meta-modeling to handle discrete architectural choices, conditional dependencies, and heterogeneous design variables inherent to SoS problems. Results show that the hierarchical formulation improves search efficiency and robustness compared to conventional surrogate-based methods, enabling the exploration of large and structurally diverse design spaces with limited simulation budgets.
  We apply the approach to an aircraft-based multi-agent system for wildfire suppression, a use case developed within the EU-funded COLOSSUS project that illustrates how SoS principles can coordinate heterogeneous aerial platforms with complementary roles, supporting both sustainable mobility and emergency response missions. Our framework provides a scalable methodology for SoS architecting and model exploration, offering transferable insights for applications in aviation, sustainable mobility, and resilience-oriented system design. By combining hierarchical representations with surrogate-based optimization, this work is among the first practical demonstrations of hierarchical Bayesian optimization applied to real-world SoS problems, advancing both methodology and practice.

</details>


### [167] [Weak Ties, Strong Signals: Efficient Training Data Detection in Diffusion LLMs via Independent Token Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22145)
*Hongyao Yu,Tianqu Zhuang,Ziyuan Xu,Hao Fang,Jiaxin Hong,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中的训练数据成员推断问题，指出已有基于随机掩码的token级检测方法因掩码token间依赖而产生非负结构性估计误差，并提出独立token采样（ITS）框架以弱化依赖、提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLMs在去噪过程中可能泄露训练数据，但检测这类使用行为存在困难：dLLMs缺乏自回归架构高效的单次概率分解，现有方法只能用随机掩码在有限查询预算下获得可计算的token级检测信号，却无法控制掩码token之间的依赖，从而掩盖微弱的记忆信号。

Method: 论文证明token级近似会引入非负的结构性估计误差，并用掩码token间的累积条件互信息（CMI）刻画该误差。基于"可靠检测需要内部依赖弱的掩码token集合"这一结论，提出独立token采样（ITS）：使用注意力导出的成对依赖代理来近似CMI感知的选择准则，避免直接估计token组合CMI的高昂成本；并引入多样性促进策略以提升多轮采样中的token覆盖度，从而聚合出受依赖误差影响更小的token级信号。

Result: 在多个数据集上，ITS在不同模型和数据集上均持续优于现有最优基线，在ArXiv数据集上AUC提升0.18，并在有限查询预算下保持强性能。

Conclusion: 掩码token间的依赖是dLLM成员推断中token级检测误差的关键来源，通过注意力代理选择内部依赖弱的掩码token并结合多样性采样，可在有限查询预算下显著提升检测可靠性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive models, yet they may expose sensitive training data during denoising. Detecting such usage is challenging because dLLMs lack the efficient one-pass probability decomposition of causal architectures. Existing methods rely on random masking to obtain tractable token-wise detection signals under limited query budgets, but fail to control dependencies among masked tokens. We demonstrate that this token-wise approximation introduces a non-negative structural estimation error, which is theoretically characterized by the cumulative conditional mutual information (CMI) among masked tokens and can obscure subtle memorization signals. This insight suggests that reliable detection requires masked token sets with weak internal dependency. To avoid the prohibitive cost of directly estimating CMI over token combinations, we propose \textit{Independent Token Sampling} (ITS), a query-efficient framework that uses an attention-derived pairwise dependency proxy to approximate the CMI-aware selection criterion. ITS further incorporates a diversity-promoting strategy to improve token coverage across sampling rounds, yielding aggregated token-wise signals that are less affected by dependency-induced approximation error. Experiments on multiple datasets show that ITS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different models and datasets, achieving an AUC improvement of 0.18 on the ArXiv dataset while maintaining strong performance under limited query budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/Chrisqcwx/DLLM-MIA .

</details>


### [168] [GRRR: The Geometry of Reshaping, Rotation, and Routing in Decoder LLM post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22146)
*Jianing Qi,Hao Tang,Zhigang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究LLM后训练如何相对预训练权重改变参数，在预训练矩阵的SVD框架中分解权重更新，发现对角分量（奇异值重塑）通常可移除而保留大部分后训练增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后训练（SFT与RL）究竟在何种几何意义上改变大语言模型权重，厘清增益来源于奇异值重塑、方向耦合旋转还是超出原非零SVD核心的新通路。

Method: 在12条包含SFT与RL的后训练链上，将每个权重更新表示在预训练矩阵的SVD框架中，分离出三类几何上不同的分量：对角值（重塑奇异值）、非对角值（旋转预训练输入与输出方向间的耦合）、零空间值（在原非零SVD核心之外路由）。

Result: 在数学评测集上，移除对角分量通常能保留大部分后训练增益。

Conclusion: 后训练增益主要由对预训练通路的重新配置与扩展承载，而非显著改变预训练模型的奇异值。

Abstract: We study how post-training changes the weights of Large Language Models (LLMs) relative to their pretrained weights. Across 12 post-training chains with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), we express each weight update in the pretrained matrix's singular value decomposition (SVD) frame. This decomposition separates the changes of three geometrically distinct components: diagonal values, which reshapes singular values; off-diagonal values, which rotates the coupling between pretrained input and output directions; and null-space values, which routes outside the matrix's original nonzero SVD core. On a math evaluation suite, we find that removing the diagonal component usually preserves most of the gains from post-training. These results suggest that post-training gains are carried primarily by reconfiguring and extending pretrained pathways rather than by substantially changing singular values of pre-trained models.

</details>


### [169] [SafeTune: A Unified Faithful Library for Auditing and Repairing Safety Drift in Fine-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22153)
*Pratinav Seth,Saisab Sadhu,Anshul Kaushal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeTune是一个开源库，统一了微调大模型安全性漂移的四类干预范式（事后权重恢复、安全约束微调、基于梯度的遗忘、推理时引导），并提供可解释性、评测与部署工具，支持配置驱动的模块化工作流与受控对比，并在金融、医疗场景中验证其漂移刻画与分层缓解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大语言模型会导致"安全性漂移"，但现有应对方法分散在互不兼容的实现、生命周期阶段与评测协议中，难以采用与横向比较，缺乏统一的框架与工具支撑。

Method: 提出SafeTune库：将四类干预范式（事后权重恢复、安全约束微调、梯度遗忘、推理时引导）统一到一致配置驱动的工作流中，同时保留各范式所需的特定输入与干预点；采用模块化注册机制，可扩展新方法、基准、评判器、模型与微调领域，无需重构流水线；并通过受控对比实验与金融、医疗部署案例研究进行验证。

Result: SafeTune能够刻画安全性漂移，在常见拒答行为与能力评测上评估各可行干预的效果，并支持校准式或分层式缓解策略；在金融与医疗场景的案例研究中展示了其部署实用性。
