Conclusion: SafeTune提供了一个统一、可扩展、配置驱动的开源平台，弥合安全漂移缓解方法在实现、阶段与评测上的碎片化问题，便于方法采用与公平比较，并为实际部署中的分层安全缓解提供支持。

Abstract: Methods for addressing safety drift in fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) are scattered across incompatible implementations, lifecycle stages, and evaluation protocols, making them difficult to adopt and compare. We introduce SafeTune, a source-available library that unifies four intervention paradigms: post-hoc weight recovery, safety-constrained fine-tuning, gradient-based unlearning, and inference-time steering, alongside shared interpretability, evaluation, and deployment utilities. SafeTune provides a consistent configuration-driven workflow while preserving the distinct inputs and intervention points each paradigm requires. Its modular registry supports new methods, benchmarks, judges, models, and fine-tuning domains without redesigning the surrounding pipeline. We demonstrate SafeTune through controlled comparisons and finance and medical deployment case studies, showing how it characterizes safety drift, evaluates feasible interventions on common refusal-behavior and capability evaluations, and supports calibrated or layered mitigation.

</details>


### [170] [A Comparative Framework for Evaluating Foundation Models on Tabular Data: A Case Study in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22154)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个比较性评估框架，从六个临床维度对表格基础模型（TFM）打分排名，并通过两个医疗用例验证其在不同临床情境下产生不同模型排序，同时给出45个TFM的架构分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是临床实践中最常见的数据格式，涵盖实验室结果、用药记录、诊断编码和患者人口统计信息。随着表格基础模型数量与种类激增，" clinician或数据科学家应当为某任务选择哪个模型、原因何在"变得难以回答。既有综述仅编目模型能力，缺少按照真实应用特定需求进行结构化比较的方法。

Method: 提出比较性评估框架，沿六个具有临床意义的维度对表格基础模型进行打分与排序：对新数据集的泛化能力、患者隐私保护效果、所需数据量、随数据集与特征空间增长的扩展性、对临床医生的可解释性、跨患者亚组的公平性。每个维度细分为可测量子成分，子成分组可选择性组合为补充性复合评分（超级指标），以提供跨维度诊断视图。框架应用于两个医疗用例：铁缺乏筛查与心衰预测。同时提供按底层架构组织的45个TFM分类法。

Result: 在两个医疗用例中，同一组指标依据各临床情境中最重要因素的不同，导致不同的模型排序，展示了框架的实用性；并形成涵盖45个表格基础模型、按架构组织的分类参考。

Conclusion: 该框架为临床与数据科学从业者在真实临床需求下选择表格基础模型提供了结构化、可诊断的比较依据，并通过架构分类法帮助研究者与从业者驾驭这一快速扩展的领域。

Abstract: Tabular data is the most common format in clinical practice, encompassing laboratory results, medication records, diagnostic codes, and patient demographics. As foundation models for tabular data have grown in number and variety, a practical question has become harder to answer: which model should a clinician or data scientist actually choose for a given task, and why? Existing surveys catalogue what these models can do, but they stop short of providing a structured way to compare them against the specific demands of a real application. We introduce \system{}, a comparative evaluation framework that scores and ranks tabular foundation models (TFMs) across six clinically meaningful dimensions: how well a model generalizes to new datasets, how effectively it protects patient privacy, how much data it needs to perform well, how it scales with growing datasets and feature spaces, how interpretable its predictions are to clinicians, and how fairly it performs across patient subgroups. Each dimension is broken down into measurable sub-components, and groups of sub-components can optionally be combined into supplementary compound scores, called super-metrics, that provide a diagnostic view of how a model performs across several dimensions simultaneously. To show how the framework works in practice, we apply it to two healthcare use cases, screening for iron deficiency and predicting heart failure, demonstrating how the same set of metrics leads to different model rankings depending on what matters most in each clinical context. We also provide a taxonomy of 45 TFMs organized by their underlying architecture, which serves as a reference for researchers and practitioners looking to navigate this rapidly expanding field.

</details>


### [171] [From Latent Biomarkers to Clinical Rules: Embedding-Guided Rule Mining and Attribution-Based Translation for Interpretable Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22155)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Hamed Ayoobi,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出四步流程方法，在FT-Transformer隐空间中挖掘决策规则并翻译回临床可测量特征，在六个公开数据集上验证，五个数据集上翻译规则优于原始特征规则，平均AUROC提升0.04–0.23。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持工具需兼顾预测准确性与可解释性。基于规则的模型提供透明性，但直接从原始临床测量中推导的规则可能遗漏多变量交互产生的模式，因此需在保留可解释性的同时捕捉更复杂的预测模式。

Method: 提出四步流程：1）在FT-Transformer隐空间中挖掘决策规则；2）将能持续区分患者群体的嵌入维度视为潜在生物标志物；3）用小决策树挖掘规则；4）通过梯度-输入显著性和CLS注意力归因将选定规则翻译为可测量临床特征。在六个公开临床与人群健康数据集、四个嵌入维度上进行评估。

Result: 翻译规则在六个数据集中的五个优于原始特征规则，平均AUROC增益为0.04至0.23。在心脏病数据集上，隐空间规则达到0.98 AUROC，但翻译后降至0.72，表明高性能隐规则并非总能由简单原始特征条件表示。

Conclusion: 隐空间规则发现可揭示预测模式，并能翻译为临床可测量、可供临床医生评估的特征，但翻译过程可能损失部分预测性能。

Abstract: Clinical decision support tools are most useful when accurate predictions are accompanied by understandable explanations. Rule-based models provide transparency, but rules derived directly from raw clinical measurements may miss patterns arising from interactions between multiple variables. We present a four-step pipeline that mines decision rules in the latent space of an FT-Transformer and translates them back into measurable clinical features. Embedding dimensions that consistently separate patient groups are treated as latent biomarkers, rules are mined using small decision trees, and selected rules are translated using gradient-input saliency and CLS attention attribution. We evaluate the framework on six public clinical and population health datasets at four embedding dimensions. Translated rules outperformed raw-feature rules in five of six datasets, with mean AUROC gains ranging from 0.04 to 0.23. On the heart disease dataset, embedding-space rules reached 0.98 AUROC, but translation reduced this to 0.72, showing that high-performing latent rules cannot always be represented by simple raw-feature conditions. These results show that latent-space rule discovery can uncover predictive patterns while translating them into clinically measurable features that can be evaluated by clinicians.

</details>


### [172] [The Limits of Speculation: Bounding Speculative Decoding in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22156)
*Aidar Amankulov,Denis Mamatin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 MoE 推测解码中的“投机预算选择”建模为离线随机最短路（SSP）问题，并构建基于反事实模拟的诊断性 Oracle，揭示被拒绝候选在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中形成严格线性边界，该边界由边际成本与期望进展之比的必要条件支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型中的推测解码面临验证成本随输入而变化（输入依赖的专家加载）导致的不稳定问题，缺乏对验证成本物理机制的深入理解，也缺少为自适应在线启发式设计提供理论依据的严格参考。

Method: 将推测预算选择形式化为参考序列上的离线随机最短路（SSP）问题；构建诊断性 Oracle，通过反事实模拟计入 MoE 验证成本；在 Qwen3-Coder 与 EAGLE-3 配对上进行决策分析，并在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中观察决策规律。

Result: 被拒绝的候选在 Delta Space 中形成严格的线性边界，表明复杂的全局优化在局部受一个必要条件支配，即边际成本与期望进展之比（ΔE[Cost]/ΔE[a]）的平衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解 MoE 推测解码的验证成本机制提供了“物理学”视角，并为未来自适应在线启发式的设计提供了严格的数学参考点。

Abstract: Speculative decoding in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models faces the problem of unstable verification cost caused by input-dependent expert loading. To study the physics of this process, we formulate speculation-budget selection as an offline Stochastic Shortest Path (SSP) problem over reference sequences and build a diagnostic Oracle that uses counterfactual simulation to account for MoE verification cost. A detailed analysis of the Oracle's decisions on the Qwen3-Coder and EAGLE-3 pairing, in the space of marginal deltas (Delta Space), shows that rejected candidates form a strict linear boundary. This result demonstrates that a complex global optimization is locally governed by a necessary condition balancing marginal cost against expected progress ($\frac{Δ\mathbb{E}[Cost]}{Δ\mathbb{E}[a]}$), providing a rigorous mathematical reference point for designing future adaptive online heuristics.

</details>


### [173] [PAGE: Partition-Aware Gated KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22157)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将KV缓存驱逐重构为逐输入的准入决策，提出无标签标量（prefill注意力中top-k头一致性的早晚期下降）来预测输入属于"容量受限类"还是"稀释倾向类"，并据此构建PAGE门控：下降大时启用驱逐器，否则保留完整缓存。PAGE把容量受限情形下的危害率从0.75降至0.026（29倍），将99%→0%的崩溃变为稳定89%，且无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache驱逐方法只决定保留哪些token，而不决定是否应当驱逐；因此基准均值会掩盖一类输入——在这些输入上压缩会使准确率从99%骤降到0%，即压缩在这些输入上是灾难性的。作者希望以逐输入的方式识别并规避这类风险。

Method: 把驱逐重新定义为逐输入的准入决策，并发现输入可划分为容量受限类（任何预算下驱逐都具灾难性）与稀释倾向类（驱逐安全甚至有益）。提出单一无标签标量：prefill注意力中成对top-k头一致性的早晚期下降量，可在解码前预测类别。PAGE对该下降量设阈值：下降大则应用任意基础驱逐器，否则保留完整缓存；无需训练、无需准确率标签。跨四种架构族标量对输入排序一致，约100条无标签输入的小规模试点即可为新架构族重校准阈值。

Result: 作为安全防护，PAGE在容量受限regime下将危害率从0.75降至0.026（29倍降低），覆盖四种驱逐器、四个模型、两个基准，把99%→0%的崩溃转化为稳定的89%，且无需重训练驱逐器。在驱逐本已安全之处门控保持惰性；其保护的容量受限类仅占输入的少数且可识别，因此收益是针对性安全增益而非平均增益。压缩实现为1.8–3.4倍（均值2.9倍），相对标称16倍预算，静态配置下在batch 16时趋近1倍；在同等内存下训练过的驱逐器更优。

Conclusion: PAGE是逐输入安全防护而非压缩器：它通过无标签、免训练的准入门控避免灾难性驱逐，换取针对性安全增益，但实际压缩率有限，且在同内存条件下不及专门训练的驱逐器。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction methods decide which tokens to keep but not whether to evict at all, so a benchmark mean can hide a class of inputs on which compression drives accuracy from 99\% to 0\%. We reframe eviction as a per-input admission decision and show that inputs separate into a capacity-bound class, where eviction is catastrophic at every budget, and a dilution-prone class, where eviction is safe or beneficial. A single label-free scalar computed from prefill attention, the early-to-late drop in pairwise top-$k$ head agreement, predicts this class before any decoding. PAGE thresholds this drop: it applies any base evictor when the drop is large and retains the full cache otherwise, with no training and no accuracy labels. The drop orders inputs by eviction safety consistently across four architecture families, and a per-model unlabeled pilot of about 100 inputs recalibrates the threshold for a new family. Used as a safeguard, PAGE cuts the harm rate on the capacity-bound regime from 0.75 to 0.026, a 29 $\times$ reduction, across four evictors, four models, and two benchmarks, turning a 99\% to 0\% collapse into a flat 89\% without retraining the evictor. The gate is inert wherever eviction is already safe, and the capacity-bound class it protects is a small, identifiable minority of inputs, so the benefit is a targeted safety gain rather than an average one. PAGE is a per-input safeguard, not a compressor: realized compression is $1.8 - 3.4 \times$ (mean 2.9$\times$) against a nominal 16$\times$ budget and decays toward unity by batch 16 under static provisioning, and a trained evictor wins at matched memory.

</details>


### [174] [StepKV: Step-Aware KV Cache Compression for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22158)
*Boyu Feng,Jiahong Liu,Yifan Li,Wenhao Yu,Zexuan Qiu,Yuliang Sun,Ming Shen,Xiang Li,Quanyu Dai,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StepKV提出以"推理步骤"为基本保留单元的KV缓存压缩方法，通过将缓存条目关联到生成步骤并结合步骤效用与token级显著性进行全局排序，在多步智能体推理中实现更优的效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存随上下文线性增长，增加存储与解码成本。多步LLM智能体中，查询会扩展为推理、工具交互与检索观测的轨迹。现有剪枝方法将缓存视为扁平token流，按新近度或注意力显著性排序，造成"压缩单元"与"推理单元"错配：token级剪枝删除单个条目，而智能体中的有用信息常以重要性不均且延迟出现的推理步骤组织，导致早期观测或中间决策虽新近注意力低却对后续证据综合至关重要，作者称此为"推理连续性中断"（Reasoning Continuity Disruption）。

Method: StepKV将推理步骤视为一等保留单元：把缓存条目关联到其生成步骤；从轨迹派生信号估计步骤效用；将步骤效用与token级显著性结合；据此对可剪枝token进行全局排序，并在目标预算下保留得分最高的条目。

Result: 在多跳问答与长时程网页推理任务上，StepKV在低KV预算下仍能维持精度，而token级基线方法精度急剧下降，提供了更稳健的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: StepKV为智能体KV缓存压缩提供了以步骤为中心的视角，通过联合考虑token级与推理步骤级信息，缓解推理连续性中断问题，适用于多步智能体推理场景。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference, but the cache grows linearly with context length, increasing storage and decoding costs. KV cache compression mitigates this cost by retaining only a subset of cached tokens. This challenge is particularly important for multi-step LLM agents, where a query expands into trajectories of reasoning, tool interactions, and retrieved observations. Existing pruning methods typically treat the cache as a flat token stream and rank tokens by recency or attention saliency. This creates a mismatch between the unit of compression and the unit of reasoning: token-level pruning removes individual entries, whereas useful information in multi-step agents is often organized into reasoning steps with uneven and delayed importance. Consequently, an early observation or intermediate decision may receive little recent attention yet remain essential for later evidence synthesis. We term this failure mode Reasoning Continuity Disruption.These observations motivate KV cache compression that jointly considers token- and reasoning-step-level information. StepKV addresses this goal by treating reasoning steps as first-class retention units. It associates cache entries with their generating steps, estimates step utility from trajectory-derived signals, and combines this utility with token-level saliency. The resulting scores globally rank prunable tokens, from which StepKV retains the top-scoring entries under a target budget. StepKV thus provides a step-centric perspective for agent KV cache compression. Across multi-hop QA and long-horizon web reasoning tasks, StepKV sustains accuracy under low KV budgets where token-level baselines degrade sharply, offering a more robust efficiency-accuracy trade-off for multi-step agent inference.

</details>


### [175] [Uncertainty and Business-Aware Remaining Useful Life Estimation for Semiconductor Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22160)
*Davide Frizzo,Francesco Borsatti,Gian Antonio Susto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合深度学习序列模型与同时分位数回归的预测性维护框架，实现不确定性感知的RUL估计与风险感知维护决策；在PHM18离子铣削数据上，S4D表现最优并显著降低业务成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造各组件高度耦合，单个部件失效可能中断整条产线，因此需尽早识别高风险资产；同时维护规划须在突发故障与过早中断设备寿命之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建预测性维护框架，融合深度学习序列模型与同时分位数回归（SQR）进行不确定性感知的剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计；在2018 PHM数据挑战赛（PHM18）离子铣削数据上对比多种架构，包括基于状态空间模型（SSM）的架构。

Result: 评估采用预测指标与业务指标（意外故障UB、未利用寿命UL及成本加权目标）。对角状态空间模型（S4D）在各分位数上给出最优RUL估计，相比预防性维护（PvM）基线显著降低业务成本，避免系统性的过早干预。

Conclusion: 结果支持在半导体生产中采用不确定性感知、成本敏感的维护规划。

Abstract: Semiconductor manufacturing relies on tightly interconnected components, so early identification of the assets most likely to fail is essential to prevent a single breakdown from disrupting the entire production pipeline. Maintenance planning must therefore balance unexpected failures against prematurely interrupted operating life. We present a Predictive Maintenance (PdM) framework combining Deep Learning (DL) sequence models and Simoultaneous Quantile Regression (SQR)
  for uncertainty-aware Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation and risk-aware maintenance decisions. Several architectures are compared on ion-milling data from the 2018 PHM Data Challenge (PHM18), including architectures based on State Space Models (SSM), using prediction and business metrics: Unexpected Breaks (UB), Unexploited Lifetime (UL), and a cost-weighted objective. Diagonal State Spaces (S4D) delivers the best Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimates across quantiles and, relative to Preventive Maintenance (PvM) baselines, substantially lowers business cost by avoiding systematically early interventions. The results support uncertainty-aware, cost-sensitive maintenance planning in semiconductor production.

</details>


### [176] [TARGet: Topology-Aware Fusion-based Radio Frequency Circuit Functional Modeling using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22165)
*Soroosh Noorzad,Sebastian Bodero,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源、拓扑感知的射频电路建模方法 TARGet，融合 GNN 与子电路连接感知网络，在两层级（子电路与整体拓扑）建模，显著提升数据效率与精度，并实现零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ML 电路建模方法存在三大局限：每换一种拓扑（哪怕增删单个元件）就需重新采集训练数据；忽略电路拓扑信息，难以刻画复杂元件交互；依赖全连接网络与扁平特征表示，导致训练数据需求量大。

Method: TARGet 在两个层级建模电路：子电路层面以 S 参数表示刻画子电路行为（而非单个元件），形成可复用的 RF 构建块行为抽象；整体层面显式引入电路拓扑信息以支持跨拓扑学习。方法采用新颖的融合架构，整合图神经网络（GNN）与子电路连接感知神经网络，以提升数据效率。

Result: 在多种 RF 电路拓扑上，TARGet 预测误差低于 1%，训练数据需求相比 SOTA 方法最多减少 35.5 倍；在严格 1% 误差阈值下，预测精度比 SOTA 高 9.7 倍。留出的匹配网络评估显示对未见子电路拓扑的零样本迁移，NMAE 最多降低 45%。

Conclusion: TARGet 通过拓扑感知与子电路级 S 参数抽象及 GNN 融合架构，在射频电路建模中同时实现高精度、高数据效率与跨拓扑泛化能力，且开源可用。

Abstract: Automatic synthesis of analog and Radio Frequency (RF) circuits is an emerging area that requires an efficient circuit modeling method. In recent years, Machine Learning (ML) solutions have played a promising role in this regard. However, many existing ML approaches require separate training data for each circuit topology, even when a single circuit component is added or removed. In addition, they overlook circuit topology information, which limits their ability to capture complex component interactions. Furthermore, they rely on fully connected neural networks with flat feature representations, which require substantial amounts of training data. In this work, we propose an open-source topology-aware RF circuit modeling method, TARGet. Our model considers the circuit at two levels: sub-circuits and the overall circuit topology. At the sub-circuit level, TARGet leverages S-parameter representations to capture sub-circuit behavior rather than relying on individual circuit components, providing a reusable behavioral abstraction for RF building blocks. Moreover, TARGet explicitly incorporates circuit topology information into the model, enabling it to learn across multiple topologies. TARGet introduces a novel fusion-based architecture that integrates Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and sub-circuit connectivity-aware neural networks to improve data efficiency. Experimental evaluation across multiple RF circuit topologies demonstrates that TARGet achieves sub-1% prediction error while reducing the required training data by up to 35.5x compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. Furthermore, TARGet achieves 9.7x higher prediction accuracy under a strict 1% error threshold relative to SOTA models. A held-out matching-network evaluation further demonstrates zero-shot transfer to an unseen sub-circuit topology, where TARGet reduces NMAE by up to 45%.

</details>


### [177] [Clustering-Based Collective Anomaly Detection in IoT Systems: A Graph Neural Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22166)
*Dalila Khettaf,Djamel Djenouri,Zeinab Rezaeifar,Youcef Djenouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UGCAD，一种无监督图集体异常检测框架，利用变分图自编码器学习图表示并增强聚类，实现IoT网络流量中的集体异常检测，在CICIoT2023和ToN-IoT数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备广泛部署导致网络流量激增，恶意攻击机会增多，亟需有效异常检测。现有研究多聚焦点异常，集体异常检测相对缺乏探索，且多数方法依赖组标签或成员信息等先验知识。

Method: UGCAD直接作用于图结构数据，无需组标签或成员信息先验知识。采用变分图自编码器（VGAE）学习图表示，用其增强聚类算法实现节点有效分组；先聚合并精炼被判定为正常的簇，再应用异常分数检测集体异常。

Result: 在CICIoT2023和ToN-IoT网络数据集上的大量实验表明，UGCAD在聚类和集体异常检测（CAD）两方面均有效；与多种传统及最先进的基于聚类的CAD方法比较，UGCAD在准确检测集体异常上更具优势。

Conclusion: UGCAD为IoT网络流量中的无监督集体异常检测提供了有效方案，克服了对先验组标签的依赖，性能优于现有基于聚类的CAD方法。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Internet of Things (IoT) technology has led to the widespread deployment of smart, interconnected devices across a range of domains. However, this expansion has also resulted in a substantial increase in network traffic, creating more opportunities for malicious actors to launch cyberattacks and compromise sensitive information, thereby increasing the need for effective anomaly detection. The state-of-the-art in anomaly detection has predominantly focused on point anomalies. In contrast, the detection of collective anomalies remains relatively under-explored in the literature. In this paper, we introduce Unsupervised Graph Collective Anomaly Detection (UGCAD), a novel frame- work designed to identify collective anomalies in IoT network traffic. Unlike many existing methods, UGCAD operates on graph-structured data without any prior knowledge of group labels or membership. It leverages a variational graph autoencoder (VGAE) to learn the graph representation, which is subsequently used to enhance a clustering algorithm for effective grouping of nodes. To detect collective anomalies, clusters identified as normal are first aggregated and refined, after which anomaly scores are applied to detect collective anomalies. Extensive experiments conducted on the CICIoT2023 and ToN-IoT network datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of UGCAD in both clustering and collective anomaly detection (CAD). Furthermore, comparative evaluations against several traditional and state-of-the-art clustering-based CAD approaches confirm the superiority of UGCAD in accurately detecting collective anomalies.

</details>


### [178] [Role-Aware Morgan Fingerprints for Reaction Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22167)
*Chinmay Mirji,Prashant Shekhar,Foram Madiyar,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MFP：基于角色感知Morgan指纹的反应产率预测方法，将各组分指纹按化学角色聚合并与差异特征拼接成定长描述符，输入前馈回归器，精度高且训练快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测可减少试错实验、加速条件筛选。现有基于GNN或Transformer编码反应SMILES的方法预处理开销大，且输入格式不一致时性能易崩溃，需要更轻量、稳健且可复现的基线方法。

Method: MFP对每个反应组分计算基于计数的环形指纹（Morgan指纹），按化学角色（反应物、试剂、产物）聚合，再与对转化敏感的差异特征结合，构成定长反应描述符，送入前馈神经网络回归器。采用统一预处理与评估协议，在Suzuki-Miyaura和Buchwald-Hartwig基准上与YieldBERT（含/不含数据增强）和GNAN对比，并进行复杂度分析与指纹半径、折叠向量长度消融。

Result: Suzuki-Miyaura上R2=0.878，Buchwald-Hartwig上R2=0.969，训练速度比图或Transformer方法快一个数量级。复杂度分析表明MFP将表示成本集中于一次性预处理，消除图方法每轮的消息传递开销。消融显示半径2、nBits=2048在两个数据集上取得精度、速度与跨划分稳定性的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: MFP是一个有效、可复现且高效的反应产率预测基线，兼顾精度与训练效率，适合作为该任务的轻量级对照方法。

Abstract: Predicting reaction yield from molecular structure and reaction context can cut experimental trial-and-error and speed up condition screening in synthetic chemistry. Recent methods for this task use learned representations such as graph neural networks or Transformer encoders over reaction SMILES (Simplified Molecular Input Line Entry System), but these approaches carry heavy preprocessing overhead and can break when input formatting is inconsistent. We propose MFP, a reaction yield prediction method built on role-aware Morgan fingerprints where count-based circular fingerprints are computed for each reaction component, aggregated by chemical role (reactant, reagent, product), and combined with transformation-sensitive difference features into a fixed-length reaction descriptor fed to a feed-forward neural regressor. We test MFP against state of the art methods such as YieldBERT (with and without data augmentation) and GNAN (graph neural network) on the Suzuki-Miyaura and Buchwald-Hartwig benchmarks using a shared preprocessing and evaluation protocol. MFP reaches R2 = 0.878 on Suzuki-Miyaura and R2 = 0.969 on Buchwald-Hartwig while training an order of magnitude faster than graph- or Transformer-based alternatives. A formal complexity analysis confirms that MFP folds all representation cost into a one-time preprocessing step, removing the per-epoch message-passing overhead that graph methods carry. An ablation over fingerprint radius and folded vector length shows that radius-2 representations at nBits =2048 give the best balance of accuracy, speed, and cross-split stability on both datasets. These results establish MFP as an effective, reproducible, and efficient baseline for reaction yield prediction.

</details>


### [179] [Multiple latent orderings better predict language model preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22170)
*Aviral Chawla,William H. W. Thompson,Jean-Gabriel Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LLM 偏好中的循环不一致性并非采样噪声，而是多个内部一致的潜在偏好排序聚合的结果，并用噪声增强混合 Bradley-Terry (MBT) 模型验证了这一观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被要求做出价值判断与选择，而观察到的选择常呈现非传递性：模型可能偏好 A>B、B>C，却又偏好 C>A。现有工作将此类不一致视为围绕单一潜在排序的采样噪声，作者认为这一假设值得质疑，需要更合理的解释框架。

Method: 首先从理论上证明观察到的结构不一致在任意单调链接函数下均无法由单一排序解释；随后提出噪声增强混合 Bradley-Terry (MBT) 模型，通过重复成对比较推断潜在偏好分量，在七个模型、四个任务上进行验证。

Result: 在七模型四任务中，排序混合模型比单一效用模型更好地解释结构性不一致；聚合偏好常掩盖潜在的偏好异质性；Moral Machine 两难案例研究表明，在聚合排序上不一致的模型仍可能共享潜在偏好分量。

Conclusion: LLM 体现的是多元（plural）偏好。将 LLM 偏好视为单一函数的对齐与评估流程，存在对不同用户可能各自认同的一致排序进行平均化的风险。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed in settings where they are asked to make value judgments and choices. These observed choices often exhibit intransitivity: A model may prefer item $A$ to $B$ and $B$ to $C$, while also preferring $C$ to $A$. Existing work that models LLM preferences treats such inconsistencies as sampling noise around a single latent ordering. We instead propose that intransitivity reflects the aggregation of multiple latent, internally consistent orderings. We first show that observed inconsistencies cannot be explained by a single ordering under any monotone link function. We then introduce a noise-augmented mixture Bradley-Terry (MBT) model that infers latent preference components from repeated pairwise comparisons. Across seven models and four tasks, a mixture of orderings often explains structural inconsistencies better than single-utility models. We find that aggregate preferences often hide underlying preference heterogeneity. A case study on Moral Machine dilemmas shows that models which disagree on aggregate orderings can still share latent components. Together, these results suggest that LLMs reflect plural preferences. Alignment and evaluation pipelines that treat LLM preferences as a single function, therefore, risk averaging over coherent orderings that different users may endorse differently.

</details>


### [180] [Industrial Kinematic Trajectory Model (IKTM): Coordinate-Free Autoregressive Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22173)
*Max Amiri,David Eyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无坐标的工业车辆轨迹生成模型IKTM，用速度与航向变化的运动学序列表征运动，在一站点训练、三站点零样本测试，转弯率分布匹配度显著优于先前马尔可夫模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景（港口、矿区、机场）的移动性仿真需求广泛，但现有轨迹模型依赖绝对坐标、路网token或语义分区等站点特定表征，难以适用于非结构化工业地形。

Method: IKTM以运动学序列（速度与航向变化）表征运动，不含绝对空间参照；采用带概率混合头的自回归因果Transformer，在先前无坐标马尔可夫模型基础上引入深度序列建模与显式时长条件信号。

Result: 单站点训练、三未见过站点零样本评估：四站点均值JS散度在oracle时长协议下约0.035–0.050 bits，完全零样本先验时长协议下约0.032–0.042 bits，均高于采样噪声下限（≤0.0049 bits）。配对比较中oracle长度值比先前马尔可夫模型重实现低5.3–6.0倍。终止为时长条件而非空间条件：100%试验停止，长度跟踪误差+0.0±0.0 s（100/100精确命中）。

Conclusion: 无坐标运动学表征结合深度序列建模与时长条件，可实现跨站点零样本的工业轨迹生成，且优于先前的马尔可夫基线。

Abstract: Mobility simulation supports logistics, safety, and communications planning in industrial environments such as ports, mines, and airports. Existing trajectory models, however, rely on absolute coordinates, road-network tokens, or semantic zones: representations that are site-specific and not well suited to unstructured industrial terrain. We introduce the Industrial Kinematic Trajectory Model (IKTM), a coordinate-free trajectory generator that represents industrial vehicle motion through kinematic sequences (speed and heading change) with no absolute spatial reference. IKTM uses an autoregressive causal transformer with probabilistic mixture heads and extends our prior coordinate-free Markovian model with deep sequence modelling and an explicit duration-conditioning signal. Trained on one site and evaluated zero-shot on three unseen sites, it matches the small-turn shape of the empirical turn-rate distributions of held-out telematics; across all four sites, the per-site mean Jensen-Shannon divergence over 100 sampling seeds spans approximately 0.035-0.050 bits under an oracle-length duration-target protocol and approximately 0.032-0.042 bits under a fully zero-shot prior-length protocol, with similar ranges whether out-of-distribution (OOD) duration targets are drawn from each held-out site's empirical length distribution or from the Site A prior. Both protocols stay above the metric's sampling-noise floor (<=0.0049 bits). Paired by site, the oracle-length values are 5.3-6.0x lower than those of a re-implementation of our prior Markovian model under the same 1 Hz protocol. Termination is duration-conditioned rather than spatial: rollouts stop on 100% of trials with a length-tracking error of +0.0 +/- 0.0 s against the sampled target (100 of 100 exactly on target; N=100, T=0.2, untouched in-distribution test split).

</details>


### [181] [Contrastive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22175)
*Bonnie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对比世界模型（Contrastive World Models），用Deep InfoMax式下界替代像素重建目标，提升世界模型对视觉干扰的鲁棒性与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素重建的世界模型在视觉复杂环境中易受无关信息主导，模型被分散注意力，忽略对规划和控制真正相关的信息。

Method: 在Dreamer基础上，将标准世界模型目标中的观测重建替换为类Deep InfoMax的互信息下界，最大化状态-动作序列与未来观测局部patch特征之间的互信息，促使状态表示保留对未来有预测性的信息，无需重建视觉无关细节，并移除像素解码器。

Result: 在三种视觉复杂度递增的小规模实验中：默认设置下与Dreamer及动量预测基线持平；引入干扰物或自然视频背景后显著优于两者；同时因移除像素解码器而训练更高效。

Conclusion: 对比式、基于infomax的目标是构建对视觉干扰因素鲁棒的世界模型的原则性且有前景的方向，有助于将基于模型的强化学习智能体迁移到真实世界。方法通用性强，仅需状态-动作序列与未来观测。

Abstract: World models trained via pixel reconstruction can struggle in visually complex environments, where irrelevant information dominates the objective and distract the model from information relevant to planning and control. We present Contrastive World Models, an approach for learning latent dynamics models without pixel reconstruction. Building on Dreamer, we replace observation reconstruction in the standard world model objective with a Deep InfoMax-like lower bound that maximizes the mutual information between state-action sequences and local patch features of future observations, encouraging state representations to retain information that is predictive of the future without requiring the model to reconstruct visually irrelevant details. We evaluate our approach in small-scale experiments across three settings of increasing visual complexity. Our method matches Dreamer and a momentum prediction baseline in the default setting, and substantially outperforms both once distractors or natural video backgrounds are introduced, while also training more efficiently by removing the pixel decoder entirely. Our approach is general and makes minimal assumptions beyond access to state-action sequences and future observations. These results suggest that contrastive, infomax-based objectives are a principled and promising direction for building world models that are robust to visual nuisance factors, a property particularly relevant for transferring model-based RL agents to the real world.

</details>


### [182] [OpenBlock: Constructive and Verified Content Generation for Adaptive Tile-Matching Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22177)
*Jiang Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自适应消除类益智游戏平台，核心为双轨内容生成架构：始终可用的规则生成器与可选的学习生成器共享同一验证关口（穷举顺序放置搜索），保证学习轨不会削弱规则轨的可构造性保证。结合自博弈强化学习放置智能体与逐形状可放置性监督，诊断出游戏主导失败模式是高填充度下长条块大量失去合法位置，并通过大规模仿真与14天线上灰度发布验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除类三消/拼块游戏拥有数亿玩家，但决定每回合呈现何种块的内容生成算法均为专有，且该品类缺乏研究自适应难度的开放平台。同时需要厘清晚期失败究竟由内容难度还是棋盘状态退化导致，并量化学习式生成在真实在线环境中的收益。

Method: 提出双轨内容生成架构：确定性规则生成器（始终可用）与可选学习生成器，二者共同受一个验证关口约束，该关口通过穷举顺序放置搜索确认每个交付块集完全可放置，从而保证学习轨不降低规则轨的构造可行性保证。采用自博弈强化学习放置智能体，并以辅助任务将逐形状可放置性暴露给共享表示，用于诊断主导失败模式。开展23.4万余局自博弈、受控仿真、头对头消融，以及14天线上灰度发布（48,000名玩家，样本比例校验并做CUPED调整）。

Result: 自博弈智能体在234,000+局中达到35.6%胜率（中位分4,200）。受控仿真显示，当棋盘填充率达70–75%时，33–56%的长条块无合法放置位；而获胜与失败对局的出块难度分布统计上不可区分，表明晚期失败由棋盘状态退化而非内容难度驱动。消融表明逐形状可放置性监督（而非聚合难度特征）带来表示收益。14天在线灰度中，相对仅用规则轨，第1日留存提升1.8个百分点，单次会话时长提升7%。

Conclusion: 棋盘状态退化而非内容难度是晚期失败的主因，逐形状可放置性监督是表示增益的关键来源；双轨架构在验证关口约束下既保持可构造性保证，又能在实际在线环境中带来非对称收益（留存与会话时长提升）。该平台为消除类游戏自适应难度研究提供了开放基础。

Abstract: Tile-matching puzzle games serve hundreds of millions of players, yet the content-generation algorithms that decide which pieces to present at each turn remain proprietary, and no open platform exists for studying adaptive difficulty in this genre. We present an adaptive tile-matching platform whose central algorithmic contribution is a dual-track content-generation architecture: a deterministic rule-based generator that is always available, and an optional learned generator, both subject to a common verification gate that establishes, by exhaustive sequential-placement search, that every delivered piece set is fully placeable so the learned track can never degrade the constructive-feasibility guarantee of the rule track. A self-play reinforcement-learning placement agent, supervised by auxiliary tasks that expose per-shape placeability to shared representations, is used to diagnose the game's dominant failure mode: at high board fill, long-bar pieces lose the majority of their legal placements. Across 234,000+ self-play episodes the agent reaches a 35.6\% win rate (median score 4,200), and controlled simulation shows that at board fill rates of 70--75\%, 33--56\% of long-bar pieces have no legal placement, while spawn difficulty distributions are statistically indistinguishable between won and lost games---evidence that board-state degeneration, not content difficulty, drives late-game failure. Head-to-head ablations show that per-shape placeability supervision---not aggregate difficulty features---drives the representation gain, and a 14-day online gray rollout (48,000 players; sample-ratio verified, CUPED-adjusted) lifts day-1 retention by 1.8 percentage points and session duration by 7\% over the rule track alone, quantifying the neural track's asymmetric upside in live play.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Task Completion: Training Capable and Safe Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22178)
*Zeyu Kang,Zhenyun Yin,Yang Zhang,Shan He,Shanzhe Lei,Yanjiu Zhong,Xinquan Chen,Yuhong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE方法，通过联合后训练赋予计算机使用智能体（CUA）条件化安全策略：正常任务照常完成、遇风险可安全绕行、无安全路径则拒绝；配套SCOPE-Gen合成数据管线与SATraj-OS轨迹数据集，基于Qwen3.5-9B训练后SCOPE-RL在OSWorld达54.17%任务成功率、OS-BLIND达64.30%攻击规避率，综合能力-安全得分58.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA后训练仅以任务成功为目标，无法诱导可靠的安全行为。可靠的CUA应具备条件化策略：对常规良性任务正常执行；遇到环境风险时在存在安全完成路径的情况下规避风险并继续；当目标本身有害或不存在安全路径时明确拒绝。需要同时优化任务执行能力与安全决策。

Method: 1) 提出SCOPE（Safety and Capability Optimization for Policy Execution），联合后训练任务执行能力与安全感知决策；2) 提出SCOPE-Gen自动管线，合成可验证的能力任务，并在保持原始目标不变的前提下转换为配对的环境风险变体；3) 构建SATraj-OS轨迹数据集，包含能力示范、安全继续、显式拒绝三类轨迹；4) 训练上先用监督微调学习三类轨迹，再用在线强化学习提升任务完成度；基座模型为Qwen3.5-9B。

Result: SCOPE-RL在OSWorld上任务成功率54.17%，在OS-BLIND上攻击规避率64.30%，综合能力-安全得分58.80%，为所评估智能体中最佳。消融显示两类安全监督作用不对称但互补：拒绝轨迹贡献大部分攻击规避增益，而风险处理轨迹在相当攻击规避水平下能保留更高任务效用。

Conclusion: 任务成功导向的后训练不足以产生可靠安全行为，需以条件化策略同时优化能力与安全；通过SCOPE-Gen合成配对风险数据、SATraj-OS提供三类轨迹监督，并结合SFT与在线RL，可在能力与安全间取得更优折中，且拒绝与风险处理两类监督互补，为CUA安全后训练提供可复现的数据与方法框架。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have made rapid progress in completing complex tasks through graphical user interfaces, yet post-training centered on task success alone does not induce reliable safety behavior. A reliable CUA must condition its execution on risk: it should complete ordinary benign tasks, avoid environmental hazards and continue when a safe completion path remains, and refuse when the goal is harmful or no safe path exists. To learn this conditional policy, we develop Safety and Capability Optimization for Policy Execution (SCOPE), which jointly post-trains a CUA for task-execution capability and safety-aware decision making. To provide aligned training data for this joint objective, we further introduce SCOPE-Gen, an automated pipeline that synthesizes verifiable capability tasks and converts them into paired environment-risk variants while preserving their original goals. Using the resulting tasks, we construct SATraj-OS, a trajectory dataset comprising capability demonstrations, safe continuations, and explicit refusals. SCOPE first learns from all three trajectory types through supervised fine-tuning and then further improves task completion through online reinforcement learning. Starting from Qwen3.5-9B, SCOPE-RL achieves a 54.17% task success rate on OSWorld and a 64.30% attack-avoidance rate on OS-BLIND, yielding the best aggregate capability--safety score of 58.80% among the evaluated agents. Ablations reveal asymmetric but complementary roles for the two forms of safety supervision: refusal trajectories account for most of the attack-avoidance gain, whereas risk-handling trajectories preserve greater task utility at comparable attack-avoidance levels.

</details>


### [184] [Gaussian Process Decorrelation for Spatiotemporal Deep Learning-Based Snow Water Equivalent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22182)
*Colin Fenster,Adrienne Marshall,Soutir Bandyopadhyay,Daniel McKenzie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对SNOTEL网络的雪水当量（SWE）预测问题，提出先以高斯过程线性变换去除空间相关性，再用LSTM学习各站时间信号，并结合保形预测量化不确定性，性能优于多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国西部积雪融水对农业灌溉和市政饮用水至关重要，准确的积雪预测对水资源政策与管理意义重大。SNOTEL站点提供的SWE日观测在空间和时间上均具强相关性，如何跨站点预测未来SWE是一个关键且具挑战性的问题。

Method: 采用基于高斯过程的线性变换对SWE数据去空间相关，使各站点残差在空间上近似独立；随后在去相关后的数据上训练LSTM神经网络，以学习每个站点的纯净时间信号；并引入保形预测（conformal prediction）为SWE预报构建无分布假设的预测区间。

Result: 将空间与时间分量分离的建模策略在预测精度上优于多个基线模型；保形预测能够为SWE预报提供有效的无分布不确定性量化，形成面向时空数据的预测区间框架。

Conclusion: 该框架可支撑次季节尺度的SWE积累预测，并适用于利用未来数据外推SWE，同时为构建大尺度、时空完整的SWE分布图提供了方法与动机基础。

Abstract: In the Western United States, snowmelt is essential to the agricultural industry in addition to being a key source of municipal drinking water. Consequently, accurate snowpack forecasting is critical for water policy and management. Automated Snow Telemetry (SNOTEL) stations provide accurate daily measurements of snow water equivalent (SWE) that exhibit strong correlations in space and in time. We tackle the problem of predicting future SWE values across the SNOTEL network.
  Specifically, we use a Gaussian Process-based linear transformation to remove spatial correlations before training a long short-term memory (LSTM) neural network on the decorrelated SWE data. This approach allows the LSTM to learn a clean temporal signal at each station. We show that this separation of spatial and temporal components yields better predictive success than multiple baseline models.
  Furthermore, we incorporate conformal prediction to quantify uncertainty in the resulting SWE forecasts, providing a distribution-free approach to illustrate a potential framework for establishing predictive intervals for spatiotemporal data. Together, accurate point forecasts and distribution-free uncertainty quantification provide a framework for SWE accumulation forecasting on subseasonal scales or projecting SWE with future data while motivating and supporting future work in predicting a large-scale, spatiotemporally complete SWE map.

</details>


### [185] [CleanScore: Black-Box Benchmark Audits with Negative Controls and Sensitivity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22183)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CleanScore——一种仅用打分输出的黑盒基准审计方法，通过为每题构造公开形式与两个独立改写的全新形式，并以私有负控题库和已注册的受控暴露实验区分"曝光"与"普通形式失配"，报告公开形式优势的区间而非结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准成绩可能反映真实能力，也可能反映对题目的先前曝光，而多数模型的训练数据未知，因此需要一种在训练数据不可见条件下可操作的污染审计手段，并明确该类审计结论的边界。

Method: 方法为黑盒审计：(1) 每题作为父项，包含一个公开形式与两个独立撰写的全新形式，保持数字、事实与答案不变；(2) 输出公开形式优势的区间估计；(3) 使用带显式"传输半径"的私有负控题库分离曝光与普通形式失配；(4) 已注册的受控暴露实验检测植入曝光，并在全新形式暴露下保持不报警；(5) 对五个开源模型、200 道 GSM8K 与 200 道 ARC-Challenge 题目进行已注册审计；(6) 用已注册正控界定零结果的解释范围。

Result: 审计未发现与曝光一致的准确率优势，将表层形式膨胀上界约束在 5 个百分点以内。正控显示：泄露某一题会使模型在从未见过的改写上获得的提升几乎与泄露原措辞相当，即 52%–110% 的效果对改写审计不可见；在 ARC 上植入的 49 点优势对应可观测差距仅为 -0.020，且约 20 点在改写题干与选项后仍存活，跨四个训练随机种子一致。

Conclusion: 表层形式零结果所能排除的污染程度远小于基于措辞的污染审计所暗示的程度；CleanScore 提供了区间化的、可注册复现的审计框架，但负结果需在正控所界定的范围内谨慎解读。

Abstract: Public benchmark scores may reflect skill, prior exposure to the questions, or both, and for most models the training data are unknown. We present CleanScore, a black-box audit using scored outputs only. Each benchmark question becomes a parent item with one public form and two independently written fresh forms preserving its numbers, facts and answer. The audit reports an interval for the public-form advantage rather than a verdict, and a private negative-control bank with an explicit transport radius separates exposure from ordinary form mismatch. A registered controlled-exposure experiment detects planted exposure and stays quiet under fresh-form exposure. A registered audit of five open models on 200 GSM8K and 200 ARC-Challenge items finds no exposure-consistent advantage, bounding surface-form inflation below five points. Registered positive controls then bound what such a null can mean. Leaking an item raises accuracy on paraphrases the model never saw almost as much as on the leaked wording, leaving 52% to 110% of the effect invisible to a paraphrase audit. On ARC a planted 49-point advantage shows an observable gap of -0.020, and about 20 points survive rewriting stem and options, across four training seeds. A surface-form null bounds far less than the phrase contamination audit implies.

</details>


### [186] [DPTM-DT: Dual-Pretrained Transformer Multitask Representation Learning for Drug-Target Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22184)
*Ge Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双预训练Transformer框架DPTM-DT进行多任务药物-靶标预测，在Davis与KIBA上取得最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物-靶标关系预测模型存在三方面不足：药物或蛋白表征不完整；跨模态交互建模较浅；将亲和力回归与相互作用分类分开训练，而二者实为同一药物-靶标对紧密相关的不同视角。

Method: DPTM-DT融合GROVER分子图嵌入、ESM蛋白语言模型嵌入与CTD理化描述符，通过双向跨模态注意力交换药物-靶标信息，并共享同一对表征完成连续亲和力回归、高亲和力二分类与六级亲和力分类三个任务（含门控融合与双靶标表征）。

Result: 在Davis与KIBA上覆盖随机80/20与DeepDTA标准划分。随机80/20下Davis MSE/CI为0.193/0.917，KIBA为0.120/0.918；二分类AUPR/MCC分别为0.727/0.654与0.798/0.689；六分类Macro-F1/Top-2分别为0.800/0.932与0.815/0.962。标准划分趋势一致。消融显示双靶标表征、门控融合与跨模态注意力均有贡献。

Conclusion: DPTM-DT在回归、二分类与多分类三种设定下整体优于对比方法，验证了多任务共享表征与双预训练跨模态建模的有效性；代码与补充材料已公开。

Abstract: Drug-target relation prediction supports candidate screening, drug repositioning, and mechanism analysis. Existing models often use incomplete drug or protein representations, model cross-modal interactions shallowly, or train affinity regression and interaction classification separately, although these tasks describe closely related views of the same drug-target pair. This paper presents DPTM-DT, a dual-pretrained Transformer framework for multitask drug-target prediction. DPTM-DT combines GROVER molecular graph embeddings, ESM protein language-model embeddings, and CTD physicochemical descriptors, then exchanges drug-target information through bidirectional cross-modal attention. A shared pair representation is used for continuous affinity regression, high-affinity binary classification, and six-level affinity classification. Experiments on Davis and KIBA cover random 80/20 and DeepDTA-style standard splits. On the random 80/20 split, DPTM-DT achieves MSE/CI values of 0.193/0.917 on Davis and 0.120/0.918 on KIBA. It also reports binary AUPR/MCC values of 0.727/0.654 and 0.798/0.689, and six-class Macro-F1/Top-2 values of 0.800/0.932 and 0.815/0.962 on Davis and KIBA, respectively. Across the reported regression, binary classification, and multiclass classification settings, DPTM-DT achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods. Results under the standard split show the same relative trend. Ablations indicate that dual target representation, gated fusion, and cross-modal attention each contribute to the final performance. Code and supplementary materials are available at: anonymous.4open.science/r/DPCM-DT-74E0.

</details>


### [187] [Adaptive Physics-Informed Neural Networks for the Blasius Boundary-Layer Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22185)
*Mehari Fentahun Endalew,Xiaoming John Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个自适应PINN框架求解Blasius边界层方程，结合梯度范数自适应损失加权、非均匀与残差驱动配点、以及Adam–L-BFGS序贯优化，实现了高精度壁面剪应力预测，并指出现有加权损失不足以衡量物理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN虽为无网格求解微分方程的方法，但其性能高度依赖损失加权、配点位置与优化策略；本文旨在针对Blasius边界层方程提升PINN的精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用梯度范数自适应损失加权、非均匀与残差驱动配点布置、Adam–L-BFGS序贯优化，构成自适应PINN训练框架；在架构[1,100,100,1]上进行代表性运行，并开展四种架构的全训练对比研究，同时与独立数值边值解及已有PINN结果对比。

Result: 代表性运行预测f''(0)=0.3320762918，与基准0.332057336215相比绝对误差1.896×10⁻⁵，最终加权损失6.789×10⁻⁸；流函数、速度与剪切剖面与独立数值解吻合良好。四架构对比中两层隐藏层模型壁面剪应力误差最小（1.629×10⁻⁶），最深网络加权目标最小但壁面剪应力误差显著更大；相较已有PINN值0.33165，误差约降低21.5倍。

Conclusion: 联合自适应训练框架可在Blasius问题上达到高精度，但加权损失本身不足以识别物理上最准确的PINN；由于自适应组件被联合应用，其各自贡献无法从当前结果中分离，需通过受控消融实验单独评估。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free approach
  for solving differential equations, but their performance can depend
  strongly on loss weighting, collocation placement, and optimization
  strategy. This study develops an adaptive PINN framework for the Blasius
  boundary-layer equation using gradient-norm-based adaptive loss
  weighting, nonuniform and residual-based collocation, and sequential
  Adam--L-BFGS optimization. In the representative run using the
  architecture $[1,100,100,1]$, the model predicts
  $f''(0)=0.3320762918$, compared with the high-accuracy benchmark
  $0.332057336215$, giving an absolute error of
  $1.896\times10^{-5}$. The final weighted loss is
  $6.789\times10^{-8}$, and the predicted stream-function, velocity, and
  shear profiles agree closely with an independent numerical
  boundary-value solution. A separate full-training architecture study
  shows that the two-hidden-layer model achieves the smallest wall-shear
  error among the four tested architectures, $1.629\times10^{-6}$,
  whereas the deepest network attains the smallest weighted objective
  but a substantially larger wall-shear error. Compared with the
  previously reported PINN value $f''(0)=0.33165$, the representative
  run reduces the wall-shear error by approximately a factor of $21.5$.
  The results show that the combined adaptive training framework can
  achieve high accuracy for the Blasius problem and that weighted loss
  alone is insufficient for identifying the most physically accurate
  PINN. Because the adaptive components are applied jointly, their
  individual contributions cannot be isolated from the present results
  and would require a controlled ablation study for separate assessment.

</details>


### [188] [Correlation-Guided Flow Matching with Annealed Masking for Spatial Transcriptomics Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22187)
*Yupei Zhang,Hao Chen,Li Pan,Chao Li,Xiaohan Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CorrFlow，一种相关性引导的流匹配框架，通过退火掩码流匹配与基因图正则优化显式建模基因-基因依赖，从组织学图像预测空间转录组，在12个数据集上取得最佳平均PCC与HPCC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组（ST）测序成本高，促使从组织学图像预测ST。现有生成式方法虽能建模条件分布与随机性，但通常将基因视为独立预测目标，忽略生物系统中固有的基因-基因相互作用，导致难以保持有生物学意义的共表达模式。作者认为基因间相互作用（反映共享通路与调控机制）对生成数值准确且生物学一致的ST谱至关重要。

Method: CorrFlow 采用两个互补机制建模基因依赖：(1) 退火掩码流匹配策略，按与时间步相关的退火调度逐步掩码基因子集，促使模型基于剩余基因推断被掩码基因，实现联合条件建模而非逐基因边缘估计；(2) 基因图正则优化方案，融合 STRING 数据库先验知识与 WGCNA 估计的数据驱动共表达构建基因亲和图，在预测表达中同时施加局部一致性与全局平滑性约束。

Result: 在12个数据集上的大量实验中，CorrFlow 在所评估方法中取得最佳平均 PCC 与 HPCC，产生生物学上更一致的ST预测。

Conclusion: 显式建模基因-基因依赖是提升组织学图像到ST预测的关键；CorrFlow 通过相关性引导的流匹配与基因图正则，兼顾数值准确性与生物学连贯性，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) provides spatially resolved gene expression profiling but remains expensive, motivating the prediction of ST from histology images. Generative models have emerged as a mainstream paradigm for ST prediction due to their ability to model the conditional distribution of gene expression and capture its inherent stochasticity. However, these methods typically treat genes as independent prediction targets and overlook the intrinsic gene-gene interactions in biological systems, which limits their ability to preserve biologically meaningful co-expression patterns. We argue that gene-gene interactions, which reflect shared pathways and regulatory mechanisms, are essential for generating numerically accurate and biologically coherent ST profiles. In this paper, we propose CorrFlow, a correlation-guided flow matching framework for histology-to-ST prediction that explicitly models gene-gene dependencies through two complementary mechanisms. First, we introduce an annealed masked flow matching strategy, where subsets of genes are progressively masked following a timestep-dependent annealing schedule, encouraging the model to infer masked genes conditioned on the remaining genes and promoting joint conditional modeling beyond per-gene marginal estimation. Second, we devise a gene graph-regularized optimization scheme that integrates prior knowledge from the STRING database and data-driven co-expression estimated by WGCNA to construct a gene affinity graph, which enforces both local consistency and global smoothness in the predicted expression. Extensive experiments across 12 datasets show that CorrFlow achieves the best average PCC and HPCC among evaluated methods, leading to more biologically coherent ST predictions.

</details>


### [189] [WildfireSpreadBench: The Metric Decides the Model in Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22191)
*Arin Gopakumar,Marco Pannozzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在WildfireSpreadTS数据集上以统一评估流程对比五种判别式模型与一种生成式模型，发现平均精度（AP）与阈值相关指标（F1、IoU）给出的模型排名不一致，AP偏好预测面积过大、实际不可用的模型，因此单用AP会误导野火预测模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火次日蔓延预测可为疏散边界与隔离带规划提供依据，但模型多以AP评估；AP汇总所有阈值下的表现，而实际操作必须选定单一阈值，二者目标不一致，可能导致评估结论与实际业务需求脱节。

Method: 在WildfireSpreadTS数据集上，用共享评估流程和两种输入配置，基准测试五种判别式架构与一种生成式模型；同时报告AP及阈值相关指标F1、IoU，并比较输入通道由7扩至23的影响。

Result: 模型排名随AP或F1/IoU而变化。AP最高的模型标记面积是实际过火面积的4至5倍，在F1与IoU上仅列六者第五；召回导向最强的模型标记面积达16至23倍。可用性更好的模型AP低24至37。模型可归为过度预测、均衡、欠预测三类，仅靠AP无法区分。输入通道由7增至23，AP平均仅变0.03，而架构间差异为0.21至0.24。

Conclusion: 仅用AP评估会偏向预测结果不适合实际野火业务预报的模型；应采用阈值相关指标或结合多指标评估，以选出更具操作可用性的模型。

Abstract: Machine learning is being increasingly used to predict where active wildfires will burn the following day, helping inform evacuation boundaries and containment lines. Most models are evaluated using Average Precision (AP), which summarizes performance across all decision thresholds, although acting on a forecast requires choosing one. We benchmarked five discriminative architectures and one generative model on WildfireSpreadTS using a shared evaluation pipeline and two input configurations. We found that model rankings varied depending on whether performance was measured by AP or by threshold-dependent metrics like F1 and IoU. The highest-AP model flagged 4 to 5 times the area that burned and ranked fifth of six on F1 and IoU, and the most recall-heavy model flagged 16 to 23 times. Models with more usable predictions had AP scores 24 to 37 lower. Across architectures, we identified three distinct prediction profiles: over-predicting, balanced, and under-predicting, which AP alone could not distinguish. Expanding the input from 7 to 23 channels changed AP by 0.03 on average, against a 0.21 to 0.24 spread across architectures. These results show AP alone can favor models whose predictions are poorly suited for operational wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [190] [SegTSim: A Big Data Driven Segmented Temporal Simulation Framework for Heterogeneous Multivariate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22192)
*Xinhang Li,Chenxi Geng,Yujia Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SegTSim分段时序仿真框架，整合弹性建模、自适应min-gating、动态产能迁移、汇率传播与深度集成验证，在美日汽车贸易2015–2025年约1.3万条年度数据上验证：25%扰动下日本进口额下降20.4%至9.3亿美元，各输出变量在1000次集成推理中变异系数均低于3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质多元时间序列存在分段特异性非线性动态，单一整体式预测架构难以刻画，需要分段建模与多因素耦合的仿真框架。

Method: SegTSim框架，包含分段弹性建模、自适应min-gating、动态产能迁移优化、多因子数据融合与汇率传导，以及深度集成验证流程；数据源为USITC美日汽车贸易2015–2025年约13000条年度记录。

Result: 25%扰动情景下，日本进口量下降20.4%至0.93 billion USD；1000次集成推理中各输出变量变异系数均低于3.5%。

Conclusion: 分段时序仿真结合集成验证可在大数据贸易场景下提供稳定的扰动响应估计，SegTSim对分段非线性动态具有适用性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multivariate time-series systems exhibit segment-specific nonlinear dynamics that challenge monolithic forecasting architectures. We propose SegTSim, a big-data-driven segmented temporal simulation framework that integrates segment-specific elasticity modeling with adaptive min-gating, dynamic production relocation optimization, multi-factor data fusion with exchange-rate propagation, and a deep ensemble validation pipeline. The framework is validated on US--Japan automotive trade data from USITC repositories spanning 2015 to 2025, comprising approximately 13000 annual records. Under a 25% perturbation scenario, Japanese import volume declines by 20.4% to 0.93 billion USD, while all output variables maintain coefficients of variation below 3.5% across 1000 ensemble inference runs.

</details>


### [191] [A Pinch of SFT, A Dash of RL: When Reinforcement Learning Helps Long-Horizon Advertising Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22194)
*Aakash Kolekar,Sahika Genc,Bunyamin Sisman,Shahriar Shariat,Shree Vandana Kachroo,Avishek Saha,Qianli Wu,Ari Singer,Benoit Dumoulin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究企业分析智能体中SFT与RL的配比问题，提出用教师支持度与奖励可观测空间将特征划入三种机制（模仿、提升、发现），并据此做针对性训练路由。在18个后续实验中诊断预测了15个观测轨迹；针对性SFT+RL在广告主技能上取得多项显著提升，并降低泄漏、节省43%的增量RL算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析智能体需要跨分布式业务数据完成长程工具调用任务（检索、推理、API调用、代码执行及对中间观测的适应）。SFT能校准工具语法与教师支持下的行为，RL可探索演示之外的奖励可获得行为；但若统一施加RL，会扰动已被SFT校准好的技能。因此需要研究在生产镜像beta API下如何平衡SFT与RL。

Method: 基于受控实验中检查点轨迹可回溯地分出三种机制：Imitation（SFT已捕获可靠教师行为）、Lift（两阶段均有帮助）、Discovery（有用且奖励可观测的行为超出可靠教师支持范围）。作者将此前瞻性使用，利用教师支持度与奖励可观测的剩余空间，将特征路由至四种处理：仅SFT、SFT后RL、增加RL分配、或继续环境开发。随后开展18个特征特异实验验证，并在GPT-OSS 120B上以广告主技能为对象，与前沿Control对比评估；另做SME审计与共享奖励/优化下的统一vs针对性匹配比较。

Result: 诊断在18个后续实验中预测了15/18的观测轨迹。GPT-OSS 120B上，针对性SFT后RL在8个广告主技能中对7个产生正向点估计；5项正向增益的配对95%置信区间不含零，1个技能出现有置信度支持的回归。最大增益为non-disclosure（+11.27点；95% CI [+9.72, +12.82]）。SME审计显示，相比SFT，针对性RL将标准泄漏从11.8%降至2.9%、对抗泄漏从22.9%降至6.8%，同时保持可操作性（86.2%降至85.7%）。在共享奖励与优化的匹配比较中，针对性RL将七技能平均delta从+1.62提升至+3.57，同时减少43%的增量RL算力。

Conclusion: 按教师支持度与奖励可观测剩余空间对特征进行差异化路由，可前瞻性判断何时仅用SFT、何时叠加RL、何时加大RL投入或优先补充环境，从而在获得更大且统计可支持的收益的同时，降低泄漏并显著节省增量RL算力。

Abstract: Enterprise analytics agents solve long-horizon tool-use problems over distributed business data, requiring retrieval, reasoning, API calls, code execution, and adaptation to intermediate observations. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) calibrates tool syntax and teacher-supported behavior, whereas reinforcement learning (RL) can explore reward-supported behaviors beyond demonstrations; applied uniformly, however, RL can perturb already-calibrated skills. We study how to balance SFT and RL under production-mirroring beta APIs. We observe that, in our controlled experiment, checkpoint trajectories retrospectively separated into three regimes: Imitation, where SFT captured reliable teacher behavior; Lift, where both stages helped; and Discovery, where useful reward-observable behavior lay outside reliable teacher support. We leverage this prospectively, using teacher support and reward-observable headroom to route features to SFT only, SFT then RL, increased RL allocation, or further environment development. Across 18 subsequent feature-specific experiments, the diagnostic predicted 15/18 observed trajectories. On GPT-OSS 120B, targeted SFT then RL produced positive point estimates on 7/8 advertiser skills relative to a frontier Control; five positive gains had paired 95% confidence intervals excluding zero, while one skill had a confidence-supported regression. The largest gain was non-disclosure (+11.27 points; 95% CI [+9.72, +12.82]). A separate SME audit surfaced that targeted RL reduces standard leakage from 11.8% to 2.9% and adversarial leakage from 22.9% to 6.8% relative to SFT while preserving actionability (86.2% to 85.7%). In a matched uniform-versus-targeted comparison with shared rewards and optimization, targeted RL improved the seven-skill mean delta from +1.62 to +3.57 while using 43% less incremental RL compute.

</details>


### [192] [EvoRank: LLM-Guided Evolution of Multi-Objective Learning-to-Rank Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22196)
*Rayhan Patel,Shabaz Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoRank：LLM 引导的进化式自动排序工程师，可发现完整 LTR 流水线，并在多目标电商搜索任务上以约 10 美元成本稳定超越调优后的 LambdaMART，同时提出“余量门”以事先预测进化搜索是否值得投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标电商排序中，完整 Learning-to-Rank 流水线（特征、模型、损失、集成）的设计高度依赖人工经验；直接用 LLM 驱动进化搜索虽具吸引力，但其收益可能来自适应度噪声而非真实泛化，导致算力与资金被浪费。因此需要先量化搜索空间余量与噪声的关系，再决定是否投入。

Method: 提出 EvoRank：一个开放式自主排序工程师，采用 LLM 引导的进化循环，联合发现特征、模型、损失函数与集成策略；在 Expedia ICDM 2013 数据上以相关性、转化、营收为目标。同时设计两阶段方法：先仅进化训练目标，再通过“迁移审计”在留出数据上重新评分冠军个体以剔除适应度噪声；核心判据为“搜索空间余量相对于适应度噪声”的可测比值，并封装为 headroom gate，在 LLM 花费前预测收益。

Result: 三次独立运行均在 50 次迭代内收敛（约 10 美元），所得可解释流水线在 6 万条留出查询上优于 Optuna 调优的 LambdaMART；优势在全量数据规模下仍存在，并进入原竞赛前 6%。第一轮仅进化训练目标时，选择折上看似有效，但迁移审计显示增益几乎全部为适应度噪声；注入领域知识或更丰富诊断反馈均未改变迁移结果。

Conclusion: 决定成败的量可在事前测量：搜索空间余量相对于适应度噪声。作者将其封装为 headroom gate，用于在投入 LLM 之前判断进化循环是否划算，并公开系统、审计工具及失败模式与防护规则目录，便于团队迁移到自有排序栈。

Abstract: We present EvoRank, an open autonomous ranking engineer: an LLM-guided evolutionary loop that discovers complete Learning-to-Rank pipelines (features, models, losses, ensembles) for multi-objective e-commerce search. On the Expedia ICDM 2013 dataset, with relevance, conversion, and revenue as competing objectives, three independent runs each converge within 50 iterations (about ten dollars) on interpretable pipelines that beat an Optuna-tuned LambdaMART on 60k held-out queries, an advantage that persists at full data scale and places in the top 6 percent of the original competition. A first campaign, evolving only training objectives, builds the central design rule: it appeared to work on its selection fold (the small dataset it uses to pick winners) while a transfer audit, re-scoring winners on held-out data, showed the gains were almost entirely fitness noise (the randomness of its own scoring), and neither seeded domain knowledge nor richer diagnostic feedback changed what transferred. The deciding quantity is measurable in advance: search-space headroom relative to fitness noise. We package this as a headroom gate that predicts, before any LLM spend, whether the loop will pay off, and we release the system, the auditing tools, and a catalog of failure modes with their guardrails, so teams can apply the procedure to their own ranking stacks.

</details>


### [193] [Dissecting Hierarchical Reasoning Models: A Mechanistic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22197)
*Leo Raphael Rodrigues,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对分层推理模型（HRM）在数独、迷宫与ARC-AGI-2任务上进行机制可解释性分析，发现其本质是在题目特定的解状态上做约束感知的迭代精化：循环模块关键，但高低层状态贡献随任务检查点与推理阶段变化，且不存在紧凑、因果重要的特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRM是一类有代表性的基于分层Transformer的潜在空间推理模型，变体众多，但其内部推理机制与所编码信息尚不清楚；同时现有机制可解释性技术多面向显式token空间，是否适用于潜在空间、递归式推理模型存疑，因此需要系统刻画HRM的工作机制。

Method: 在Sudoku、Maze、ARC-AGI-2上比较HRM与含/不含循环模块的Transformer基线；对循环状态做因果干预；使用线性探针并结合随机方向消融；使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）及特征消融；并设置带步内BPTT的数独对照实验。

Result: 循环模型优于单遍基线，而单状态循环Transformer与HRM相当；状态干预显示高层与低层状态的因果贡献随任务特定检查点和推理阶段而变化；选定任务变量可从HRM循环状态中线性解码，但消融探针方向与随机对照效果相当；SAE消融引发更大的行为变化，然而排名靠前的SAE特征在较大消融规模或跨任务时相对同规模随机子集并无稳定优势，该现象在带步内BPTT的数独对照中同样存在。

Conclusion: HRM实质上是在题目特定的解状态上执行约束感知的迭代精化，不同层级组件的功能贡献存在差异，但模型并不依赖紧凑且因果重要的特征集；这凸显了研究不同工作机制的必要性，以及为潜在空间递归推理模型开发更适配的机制可解释性技术的紧迫性。

Abstract: We study Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM), a representative hierarchical Transformer-based latent reasoning model with many variants, on Sudoku, Maze, and ARC-AGI-2. We mechanistically understand how HRM reasons and what information it encodes. Our analyses compare HRM against Transformer baselines with and without recurrent modules, apply causal interventions on recurrent states, and utilize linear probes against random-direction ablations, as well as sparse autoencoders with feature ablations. Our results reveal several key findings: recurrent models outperform one-pass baselines, while single-state recurrent Transformers are comparable to HRM. State interventions further show that the causal contributions of the high- and low-level states vary across task-specific checkpoints and inference stages. Selected task variables are linearly decodable from the recurrent states in HRM, yet ablating probe directions produce effects comparable to random controls. SAE ablations yield larger behavioral changes than probe-direction ablations. However, top-ranked SAE features show no stable advantage over size-matched random subsets at larger ablation sizes or across tasks; the same pattern persists in a Sudoku control with within-step BPTT. Together, we characterize that HRM is essentially implementing constraint-aware iterative refinement on a puzzle-specific solution state, in which the functional contributions of components at different levels vary without relying on a compact, causally important feature set. These results highlight the necessity of studying the different working mechanisms and the importance of developing mechanistic interpretability techniques better suited for latent-space, recursive reasoning models.

</details>


### [194] [Improving Parameter Utilization by Sharing Neural Experts Across Layers in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22199)
*Dian Jiao,Jiaxin Duan,Shuai Zhao,Jiabing Leng,Yiran Zhang,Feng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CS-MoE，一种跨层专家共享的Transformer架构，通过引入全局共享专家池缓解层间参数冗余，在仅激活55%参数的情况下取得比同规模稠密模型更低的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大模型存在层间参数冗余，即功能变换在不同深度上被重复学习，导致参数利用效率低下。现有MoE架构以层隔离专家作为每个Transformer块的终点，难以跨层共享，无法有效解决该冗余。

Method: 提出CS-MoE架构：在保留层独立专家的同时，使各层可并发访问一个集中式、全局共享的专家池（Global Experts Sharing机制），从而支持对token级参数激活与计算量（FLOPs）的弹性控制。

Result: CS-MoE在仅激活55%参数时，困惑度低于等规模稠密Transformer；性能随激活专家数增加单调提升；在固定FLOPs预算下扩大共享池，可逼近消耗更多FLOPs的MoE模型；并在计算成本与模型容量之间形成灵活的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: CS-MoE通过跨层全局专家共享有效降低层间冗余，提供计算成本与模型容量间的可调权衡，是计算受限场景下的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models often suffer from inter-layer parameter redundancy, where functional transformations are redundantly learned across network depths. We propose CS-MoE, a novel Transformer architecture featuring cross-layer expert sharing to address this inefficiency. Deviating from the widely used Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture that terminates each Transformer block with layer-isolated experts, CS-MoE combines layer-independent experts with concurrent access to a centralized, globally shared expert pool. This \textit{Global Experts Sharing} mechanism enables elastic control over token-level parameter activation and computational consumption (FLOPs). Experiments demonstrate that CS-MoE achieves lower perplexity than equal-scale dense Transformers while activating only 55\% of parameters. Furthermore, its performance scales monotonically with an increased number of activated experts and approaches MoE counterparts that consume more FLOPs by expanding the shared pool with a fixed FLOPs budget. CS-MoE also establishes a flexible Pareto frontier between computational cost and model capacity, offering an efficient alternative for computation-constrained environments.

</details>


### [195] [Prediction of Nonlinear Oscillations in a Jumping Quarter-Car Model Using Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22205)
*Masahisa Watanabe,Shiva Dixit,Nirmal Punetha,Swati Chauhan,Manish Dev Shirimali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了回声状态网络（ESN）作为数据驱动方法，对非光滑“跳跃四分之一车模型”进行非线性动力学预测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能驾驶（自主控制与高级驾驶辅助系统）需要可靠的车辆动力学预测。农用与工程用越野车辆因轮胎-路面间歇性失接触，易出现分岔与混沌等非线性行为；此类动力学同时包含光滑非线性与失接触导致的不连续切换，传统建模预测困难。

Method: 采用储备池计算，具体为回声状态网络（ESN），对跳跃四分之一车模型进行数据驱动预测。仅使用少量空间点的时序数据训练储备池，并在周期与混沌区间内评估其重构分岔图、相空间吸引子与时间轨迹的能力。

Result: 训练后的储备池定性地复现了通往混沌的倍周期分岔路径，并捕捉了周期与混沌吸引子的几何结构。

Conclusion: 储备池计算可作为实际非光滑车辆系统中非线性动力学的可行数据驱动预测器。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of vehicle dynamics is essential for smart driving applications such as autonomous control and advanced driver-assistance systems. Off-road vehicles used in agricultural and construction settings are particularly prone to nonlinear behavior, including bifurcations and chaotic motion arising from intermittent loss of tire--road contact. Predicting such dynamics is challenging because it requires resolving both smooth nonlinearities and the discontinuous switching associated with contact loss. In this work, we investigate the feasibility of reservoir computing (RC) -- specifically an echo state network (ESN) -- for data-driven prediction of a jumping quarter-car model. The reservoir is trained on time-series data from a small number of points and evaluated on its ability to reconstruct bifurcation diagrams, phase-space attractors, and time trajectories across periodic and chaotic regimes. The trained reservoir qualitatively reproduces the period-doubling route to chaos, captures the geometric structure of periodic and chaotic attractors. These results demonstrate that reservoir computing is a feasible data-driven predictor of nonlinear dynamics in a practical, non-smooth vehicle system.

</details>


### [196] [The Effect of Quantization on Clinical Benchmarks: Accuracy and Safety Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22216)
*Leonard Twagirayezu,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估5个7-8B模型在FP16/GPTQ-INT8/GPTQ-INT4下的临床表现：INT8普遍安全，INT4退化严重且依赖模型与任务，安全与准确性鲁棒性相互独立，恢复方法需按任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化可使大模型部署于资源受限的临床边缘设备，但其对临床准确性与安全性的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 在FP16、GPTQ-INT8、GPTQ-INT4三种精度下评测5个7-8B模型，覆盖MedQA、MedMCQA、Med-HALT、风险分层的HealthBench样本及MedSafetyBench五个基准；联合变化量化位宽、模型族与临床任务类型，并引入显式风险分层与安全指标；另测试临床校准替换与QLoRA微调两种恢复方法。

Result: INT8 GPTQ普遍安全（最大退化-1.9%~1.9%）；INT4退化明显且依赖模型：临床微调的BioMistral-7B在MedMCQA降19.7%，超过任何通用模型；MedMCQA比MedQA对INT4更敏感，Med-HALT基本不受影响；HealthBench急诊风险子组中Qwen2.5-7B在INT4下降26.8%；MedSafetyBench上模型族影响大于精度（FP16拒答率10.2%-74.9%），Qwen2.5-7B(-17.8%)与Meditron-7B(-28.3%)存在明显INT4安全退化，而Qwen2.5-7B同时是准确性最鲁棒的模型；两种恢复方法均呈MedMCQA回升而MedQA进一步退化的相同权衡。

Conclusion: INT8可广泛安全用于临床部署；INT4安全性须按模型、按任务评估；临床微调不带来压缩鲁棒性；准确性与安全性鲁棒性相互独立；安全对齐主要由指令微调而非临床领域适配决定；恢复策略需任务特定验证，不能假定普遍有效。

Abstract: Quantization enables deployment of large language models on resource-constrained clinical edge devices, but its effect on clinical accuracy and safety remains understudied. We evaluate five 7-8B parameter models at FP16, GPTQ-INT8, and GPTQ-INT4 precision across five benchmarks: MedQA, MedMCQA, Med-HALT, a risk-stratified sample of HealthBench, and MedSafetyBench. The study jointly varies quantization bit width, model family, and clinical task type, with explicit risk stratification and safety measures. INT8 GPTQ is universally safe (max. degradation -1.9%-1.9%), while INT4 degradation is substantial and model-dependent: BioMistral-7B, clinically fine-tuned, loses 19.7% on MedMCQA, more than any general-purpose model, showing clinical fine-tuning does not confer compression robustness. MedMCQA degrades more than MedQA under INT4; Med-HALT is largely unaffected. On HealthBench's emergency-risk subgroup, Qwen2.5-7B degrades by 26.8% under INT4, suggesting high-risk scenarios are disproportionately vulnerable to compression. On MedSafetyBench, the model family dominates over precision (refusal rates range 10.2%-74.9% at FP16), though Qwen2.5-7B (-17.8%) and Meditron-7B (-28.3%) show substantial INT4 safety degradation; notably, Qwen2.5-7B is simultaneously the most accuracy-robust model, demonstrating that accuracy and safety robustness are independent properties. We additionally test two recovery methods, clinical calibration substitution and QLoRA fine-tuning, both producing the same trade-off: MedMCQA recovers while MedQA further degrades, indicating recovery strategies require task-specific validation rather than being assumed universally beneficial. These findings indicate INT8 is broadly safe for clinical deployment, while INT4 safety must be assessed per-model and per-task, and that safety alignment is determined primarily by instruction tuning rather than clinical domain adaptation.

</details>


### [197] [UniGIO: Unified Generative Global In-situ Weather Modeling from Spatiotemporal Incomplete Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22217)
*Songru Yang,Zili Liu,Tao Han,Ben Fei,Lei Bai,Chang Liu,Zhengxia Zou,Xiangyang Ji,Wanli Ouyang,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UniGIO，一个面向全球原位观测（GIO）的生成式框架，可直接从原生不完整的站点观测数据建模全球原位天气动态，统一预测、插补与生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球原位观测（GIO）能提供卫星格点数据无法覆盖的局部、瞬态天气动态记录，对数值天气预报、防灾减灾和农业等领域至关重要。但GIO存在严重的时空不完整性，现有方法往往需要等待完整且"AI-ready"的数据，并引入额外误差，损害了实时准确的原位天气建模。

Method: UniGIO通过从被掩码标注的已观测数据生成缺失数据，在任意缺失率下统一了预测、插补和生成。具体地：使用Observation Mixer和Event Aligner在站点和区域层面捕获缺失与观测之间的互补性，将离散观测扩散到天气过程天然跨多站点延展的连续空间；用Adaptive Temporal Mixer建立含模式漂移的时间依赖；用Mixture-of-Experts结构追踪混沌局部天气系统中的极端事件；在解码器中通过Local Refiner适配平稳与极端事件。

Result: 在目前最大的全球站点天气数据集Weather-5K上进行的大量实验表明，UniGIO达到SOTA性能，在准确性、保真度和极端事件捕获上分别取得11%、12%和5%的优势。

Conclusion: UniGIO为GIO网络中的天气建模提供了一种新颖的整体性解决方案，可直接从不完整观测出发实现统一的天气建模。

Abstract: Global In-situ Observation (GIO) provides fine-scale, direct records of the global weather system from sparse point stations, making it an indispensable source for capturing localized and transient dynamics beyond the reach of satellite gridded data, and playing a critical role in key fields such as numerical weather prediction, disaster prevention, and agriculture. However, GIO exhibits strong spatiotemporal incompleteness, severely impairing accurate and real-time in-situ weather modeling. Unlike existing methods waiting for completed AI-ready data with extra introduced errors, in this work, we explore UniGIO, a novel generative framework for directly modeling global in-situ weather dynamics from native incomplete GIO. By generating missing data from observed ones annotated by masks, it unifies the coexisting forecasting, imputation, and generation under arbitrary missing ratios. Between the missing and observed, UniGIO captures station and region level complementarity through the Observation Mixer and Event Aligner, which diffuse discrete observations into continuous spaces where weather processes naturally span multiple stations. We further establish temporal dependencies with pattern shifts using the Adaptive Temporal Mixer, and track extreme events in chaotic local weather systems through a Mixture-ofExperts structure. Steady and extreme events are adapted in decoder by a Local Refiner. Extensive experiments on the up-todate largest global station weather dataset Weather-5K validate its SOTA performance with 11%, 12%, and 5% advantages on accuracy, fidelity, and extreme event capture, delivering a novel holistic solution for weather modeling in GIO networks.

</details>


### [198] [Toollery: Scaling LLM Agents to Thousands of Skills and Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22218)
*Xiangxi Tian,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Toollery 是一个免训练的候选压缩框架，通过为每个技能/工具规格生成用户意图查询并构建检索索引，将大规模技能/工具库压缩为紧凑的 top-k 候选集，从而提升 LLM 智能体技能/工具选择的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 智能体面对成百上千乃至上万项技能、工具和 API 函数，全库提示（full-library prompting）变得昂贵、缓慢且不可靠：每增加一个候选都会增加提示词 token 和延迟，更长的候选列表还会引入更多干扰项，降低 LLM 的选择准确性。因此需要一种可扩展的候选压缩方案。

Method: Toollery 沿用文档侧查询扩展（document-side query expansion）思路：从每个技能/工具的规格说明中生成用户意图查询，并构建一个检索索引，将真实用户请求映射到紧凑的候选集，再交由 LLM 做最终决策。该方法将高层技能（high-level skills）与原子工具（atomic tools）统一视为可选能力（selectable capabilities），因此可同时应用于技能库和工具注册表。整个框架免训练（training-free）。

Result: 在约 79K 能力的 SkillRouter 基准、包含 440 多个原子工具的 BFCL-V4，以及覆盖 220 多个工具的 3,396 条专有智能座舱（smart-cockpit）请求上进行评测。结果：Toollery 将在线选择限制在紧凑的 top-k 候选集内，相比普通规格检索提升了召回率；在固定 top-10 预算下，Toollery 在座舱数据集上改善了端到端选择效果，并在 BFCL-V4 上保持了可比的 AST Accuracy。

Conclusion: Toollery 是面向大规模且持续演进的智能体能力库的实用候选压缩框架。同时，实验表明其质量与成本收益取决于工作负载覆盖度（workload coverage）和提供商缓存（provider caching）。

Abstract: As LLM agents are exposed to hundreds to tens of thousands of skills, tools, and API functions, full-library prompting becomes costly, slow, and less reliable: each added candidate increases prompt tokens and latency, while longer candidate lists introduce more distractors for LLM selection. We present \textbf{Toollery}, a training-free candidate-compression framework for scalable LLM skill/tool selection. Following established document-side query expansion, Toollery generates user-intent queries from each skill/tool specification and builds a retrieval index that maps real user requests to compact candidate sets before final LLM decision-making. By treating high-level skills and atomic tools as selectable capabilities, Toollery can be applied to both skill libraries and tool registries. We evaluate Toollery on the roughly 79K-capability SkillRouter benchmark, BFCL-V4 with over 440 atomic tools, and 3,396 proprietary smart-cockpit requests over 220 tools. Across these settings, Toollery keeps online selection bounded to a compact top-$k$ candidate set and improves recall over ordinary specification retrieval. At a fixed top-10 budget, Toollery improves end-to-end selection on the cockpit dataset, and maintains comparable AST Accuracy on BFCL-V4. These results support Toollery as a practical candidate-compression framework for large and evolving agent capability libraries, while showing that quality and cost gains depend on workload coverage and provider caching.

</details>


### [199] [Measuring the Checker: Mutation Analysis for GPU-Kernel Benchmark Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22220)
*Mingzhe Du,Anh Tuan Luu,Dong Huang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用变异分析作为GPU内核基准测试判定器（oracle）的充分性度量，通过注入确定性故障来衡量测试协议能检测多少错误，并据此审计与优化现有检查方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM生成GPU内核基准仅用少量随机输入和宽松的浮点容差判定正确性，其结果却直接驱动排行榜与强化学习奖励。已有工作虽承认这些检查器薄弱并手工打补丁（增加输入分布、模糊测试配方、收紧容差），但缺少衡量补丁是否充分的量化手段。

Method: 引入变异分析（mutation analysis）作为内核基准判定器的充分性度量：用确定性规则向188个KernelBench问题的已验证CUDA实现注入10,303个可编译故障（其中7,384个有独立杀死见证），以测试协议检测到的故障比例作为评分。基于该度量审计官方检查、现有最强补丁及已发表的模糊测试配方，并在杀死矩阵上优化测试套件。

Result: 官方检查确定性地漏检六分之一（16.9%）有见证故障，且按故障族偏斜：算术故障逃逸8.7%，精度故障逃逸78.6%。度量解释了原因（容差盲带随归约规模增大；输入激进程度受合法浮点方差限制的上限），并剖析出KernelBench-Verified增益由隐藏输入+4.0点与更紧容差+4.5点构成。还发现某已发表模糊测试配方误拒正确内核107次。优化套件在每个问题两个输入下达98.0%检测率（留出集94.8%）。跨48个完整架构，盲区随规模增长，集中于深层同质流水线；有两个问题的官方参考违背基准自身相对fp64的容差，被证明无法裁判。

Conclusion: 变异分析为内核基准判定器提供了可量化的充分性度量，能解释漏检成因、审计现有补丁并提供细粒度归因，还可指导更优测试套件的生成；故障分类学比原始故障本身更能教会测试生成器。数据与代码以KernelBench-M公开发布。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-generated GPU kernels decide correctness with a few random inputs and a loose floating-point tolerance, and their verdicts now feed leaderboards and reinforcement-learning rewards. Recent work agrees these checkers are weak and patches them by hand---extra input distributions, fuzzing recipes, tighter tolerances---with no way to \emph{measure} whether any patch suffices. We introduce mutation analysis as an adequacy metric for kernel-benchmark oracles: deterministic rules inject 10{,}303 compilable faults into verified CUDA implementations of 188 KernelBench problems, 7{,}384 of them with an independent kill witness; any test protocol is scored by the fraction it detects. The official check misses \textbf{one in six} witnessed faults (16.9%), deterministically, and the misses are skewed by family: 8.7% of arithmetic faults escape, but 78.6% of precision faults do. The metric explains why (a tolerance blind band growing with reduction size; a measured ceiling on input aggressiveness set by legitimate floating-point variance), audits the strongest existing patch (KernelBench-Verified's gain splits into $+4.0$ points from hidden inputs and $+4.5$ from tighter tolerance, a split its authors could not compute), and exposes a published fuzzing recipe that rejects \emph{correct} kernels 107 times. Optimizing suites over the kill matrix reaches 98.0% detection with two inputs per problem (94.8% held-out), and the measurement's fault taxonomy teaches a test generator more than the raw faults themselves. Across 48 whole architectures, the blindness grows with scale, concentrating in deep homogeneous pipelines, and two problems prove unrefereeable: their official references violate the benchmark's own tolerance against fp64. We release everything as \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Elfsong/KernelBench-M}{KernelBench-M}.

</details>


### [200] [Can Coding Agents Reproduce Official Statistics? Metadata, Retry Budget and the Limits of Execution Feedback in a Controlled Eurostat Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22222)
*Sabina-Cristiana Necula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在360次任务运行中，权威元数据与执行反馈可提升欧盟统计局（Eurostat）数据问答的可复现性；真正起作用的是元数据而非执行诊断，且评测契约设计会显著影响测得误差（条件C被低估23.4个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成可执行的数据分析代码，但"执行成功"不等于"官方统计结果正确"。本文追问：权威元数据与执行反馈能否提升编码智能体回答Eurostat问题的可复现性，并分离出执行反馈本身的独立贡献。

Method: 构建含30个自然语言任务的基准，覆盖7个领域、7个Eurostat数据集、4个难度层级；设四种条件：A仅任务、B任务加冻结数据集元数据卡、C元数据加由净化后执行反馈驱动的修复循环、D元数据加相同尝试预算但无任何诊断信息。Claude Sonnet 5 经 Anthropic Messages API 生成Python，3次独立重复，共360次任务运行。正确性要求执行成功及数据集、过滤条件、输出形状、数值与单位均正确。另设一配对实验：在输出契约欠规范（排名键与单位表示未告知模型）下运行，用以检验评测设计的影响。

Result: 在欠规范的输出契约下，条件C的表现被低估23.4个百分点，说明评测器与契约设计可能主导所测得的智能体错误。执行反馈（诊断信息）本身并未带来预期增益。可靠性的提升主要来自权威元数据的提供。

Conclusion: 可靠的统计编码智能体需要三要素：针对冻结规范的语义校验、完全规范的输出契约、以及重试预算——而非执行诊断反馈。执行成功与统计正确性必须被明确区分，评测设计本身应被视为结果的一部分。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable data-analysis code, but successful execution is not equivalent to a valid official-statistics result. This study asks whether authoritative metadata and execution feedback improve the reproducibility of Eurostat answers produced by a coding agent, and isolates what execution feedback actually contributes. A benchmark of 30 natural-language tasks covering seven domains, seven Eurostat datasets and four difficulty tiers was run under four conditions: task only (A), task plus a frozen dataset metadata card (B), metadata plus a repair loop driven by sanitized execution feedback (C), and metadata plus the same attempt budget with no diagnostics of any kind (D). Claude Sonnet 5 generated Python through the Anthropic Messages API in three independent replicates, yielding 360 task-runs. Exact correctness required successful execution, the correct dataset, filters, output shape, values and unit. A companion experiment run under an under-specified output contract, in which the required ranking key and unit representation were never stated to the model, understated condition C by 23.4 points, showing that evaluator and contract design can dominate measured agent error. Reliable statistical coding agents need semantic validation against frozen specifications, a fully specified output contract, and a retry budget - not execution diagnostics.

</details>


### [201] [A Synthetic Multivariate Refrigerator Time-Series Dataset for Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22229)
*Islam Benamirouche,Feriel Fass,Djemel Ziou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个基于简化物理启发模拟器生成的27台冰箱多变量时间序列数据集，用于故障预测与退化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持故障预测、退化分析以及跨不同传感器配置冰箱的学习任务，提供具有已知退化过程与故障标签的合成时序数据。

Method: 采用简化物理启发模拟器，以一分钟分辨率生成27台冰箱的合成多变量时间序列。模拟器涵盖环境温度变化、门使用、温控器与压缩机运行、热交换、除霜、电耗以及六种渐进式退化类型；每台冰箱依据其配置输出15–20个传感器信号。

Result: 数据集包含27个时间序列文件、共7,066,161行数据，以及27份故障日志；同时发布Python生成器、冰箱配置与文档。

Conclusion: 该数据集可用于故障预测、退化分析，以及在不同传感器输出集合的冰箱之间进行迁移或联合学习。

Abstract: We generated synthetic multivariate time series for 27 refrigerators with a simplified physicsinspired simulator at one-minute resolution. The simulator includes ambient-temperature variation, door use, thermostat and compressor operation, heat exchange, defrost, electrical consumption, and six progressive degradation types. Each refrigerator provides 15 to 20 sensor outputs according to its configuration. The dataset contains 7,066,161 rows in 27 time-series files and 27 failure logs. The release also includes the Python generator, refrigerator configurations, and documentation. The data can support failure prediction, degradation analysis, and learning across refrigerators with different sensor-output sets.

</details>


### [202] [List Counting Failures Are Not One Phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22230)
*Iyad Ait Hou,Saad Mankarious,Aya Zirikly,Rebecca Hwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开放权重对话模型在统计括号列表项数时错误模式因模型而异，且一个模型上的“修复手段”不可直接迁移到另一个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计数括号列表项数看似简单，但开放权重对话模型常出错。以往研究多把原因归于输入瓶颈（子词碎片化、注意力稀释），并隐含预测不同模型应以相似方式失败。本文质疑这一预测，考察失败模式是否具有模型特异性。

Method: 在相同提示下测试七个instruct模型；比较错误答案的分布模式；检验子词碎片化程度与计数难度的关系；用线性探针从残差流中恢复真实计数；对比晚期MLP输出缩放（magnitude fix）与残差引导（residual steering）在不同模型上的效果，并进行迁移检验。

Result: 错误答案呈明显且互不相同的众数：Qwen与Gemma 27B常把奇数长度翻转为邻近偶数，OLMo错误集中于少数中等数字，Llama倾向于低估。这些模式只是有用标签，并非稳定的家族规律（Gemma 9B不复制Gemma 27B的奇转偶下降）；子词碎片化更重并未使计数更难。答错时线性探针通常仍能从残差流恢复真实计数。相同的奇转偶错误并不意味着相同的晚期MLP幅度修复：缩放晚期MLP输出对Qwen有小幅帮助，但在同一协议下对Gemma 27B近乎无效；残差引导对两者都只能以偶数损失换取奇数收益。

Conclusion: 计数失败模式具有模型特异性，不能作为稳定的家族规律；相同错误不等于相同机制。跨模型迁移“幅度修复”等干预前必须做迁移检验，否则结论不可靠。

Abstract: Counting the items in a bracketed list looks trivial, yet open-weight chat models often get it wrong. Prior work usually blames input bottlenecks such as subword fragmentation or attention dilution, which predict that different models should fail in roughly the same way.
